# Trail Runner Implementation Plan

## Overview

A git-native task queue where:
- **Trails** (task definitions) are stored in `entire/trails` orphan branch
- **Execution state** is tracked via lightweight git refs (no commits for state changes)
- **Trail Runner** discovers open trails, claims them atomically, creates worktrees, runs agents

## Architecture

```
entire/trails branch (task definitions)
├── <trail-id>/
│   ├── metadata.json     # title, description, branch, assignees, labels
│   ├── discussion.json   # comments (future)
│   └── checkpoints.json  # linked checkpoints (future)

refs/entire/trails/* (execution state - cheap, no commits)
├── <trail-id>/claimed    → branch HEAD when claimed
├── <trail-id>/completed  → branch HEAD when done
└── <trail-id>/failed     → branch HEAD on error

.worktrees/trail-<id>/    # isolated execution environment
```

## File Structure

```
cmd/entire/cli/
├── trail.go                    # `entire trail` command (list, show, create)
├── trail_runner.go             # `entire trail-runner` + `entire trail run`
└── trail/
    ├── trail.go               # Trail types, metadata parsing, validation
    ├── store.go               # Read/write entire/trails branch
    ├── state.go               # Ref-based state management
    ├── discovery.go           # List/filter trails by state
    ├── runner.go              # Execution loop (claim, worktree, agent, complete)
    ├── worktree.go            # Git worktree helpers
    └── trail_test.go          # Tests
```

## Commands

```bash
# Trail management
entire trail list [--status open|in_progress|completed|failed]
entire trail show <trail-id>
entire trail create "Title" [--branch <name>] [--base <branch>]

# Trail runner
entire trail-runner start [--daemon] [--poll-interval 30s] [--max-concurrent 1]
entire trail-runner run-once
entire trail run <trail-id>
```

## Key Types

### Trail (`trail/trail.go`)

```go
type TrailID string  // 12-hex-char, same pattern as CheckpointID

type Trail struct {
    ID          TrailID   `json:"id"`
    Title       string    `json:"title"`
    Description string    `json:"description"`
    Branch      string    `json:"branch"`
    BaseBranch  string    `json:"base_branch"`
    AuthorID    string    `json:"author_id"`
    Assignees   []string  `json:"assignees"`
    Labels      []string  `json:"labels"`
    CreatedAt   time.Time `json:"created_at"`
    UpdatedAt   time.Time `json:"updated_at"`
}

type TrailState string
const (
    TrailStateOpen       TrailState = "open"        // No refs
    TrailStateInProgress TrailState = "in_progress" // claimed ref exists
    TrailStateCompleted  TrailState = "completed"   // completed ref exists
    TrailStateFailed     TrailState = "failed"      // failed ref exists
)
```

### Store (`trail/store.go`)

```go
type Store struct {
    repo *git.Repository
}

func (s *Store) EnsureBranch() error                    // Create orphan branch if needed
func (s *Store) Create(ctx, *Trail) error              // Write trail to branch
func (s *Store) Get(ctx, TrailID) (*Trail, error)      // Read trail metadata
func (s *Store) List(ctx) ([]*Trail, error)            // List all trails
```

### StateManager (`trail/state.go`)

```go
const RefPrefix = "refs/entire/trails/"

type StateManager struct { repo *git.Repository }

func (m *StateManager) GetState(ctx, TrailID) (TrailState, error)
func (m *StateManager) Claim(ctx, TrailID) error      // Atomic - fails if exists
func (m *StateManager) Complete(ctx, TrailID) error
func (m *StateManager) Fail(ctx, TrailID) error
func (m *StateManager) Release(ctx, TrailID) error    // Remove claimed ref
```

### Runner (`trail/runner.go`)

```go
type RunnerConfig struct {
    PollInterval  time.Duration
    MaxConcurrent int
    Daemon        bool
    Agent         string        // "claude-code"
    Model         string        // Optional model override
    Timeout       time.Duration // Per-trail timeout
}

type Runner struct {
    store   *Store
    state   *StateManager
    config  RunnerConfig
}

func (r *Runner) Start(ctx) error       // Daemon mode
func (r *Runner) RunOnce(ctx) error     // Single iteration
func (r *Runner) RunTrail(ctx, TrailID) error
```

## Implementation Phases

### Phase 1: Core Types and Store
**Files:** `trail/trail.go`, `trail/store.go`

1. Define `TrailID` type with validation (pattern from `checkpoint/id/id.go`)
2. Define `Trail` struct matching entire.io PR #738 format
3. Implement `Store` with branch operations:
   - `EnsureBranch()` - create `entire/trails` orphan branch (pattern from `checkpoint/committed.go:ensureSessionsBranch`)
   - `Get()` - read `<id>/metadata.json` from branch tree
   - `List()` - enumerate trail directories
   - `Create()` - write metadata.json to branch

**Key patterns to follow:**
- `checkpoint/committed.go`: `getSessionsBranchEntries()`, `BuildTreeFromEntries()`
- `validation/validators.go`: ID validation

### Phase 2: State Management
**Files:** `trail/state.go`, `trail/discovery.go`

1. Implement ref-based state:
   ```go
   // Claim atomically
   ref := plumbing.NewHashReference(
       plumbing.ReferenceName("refs/entire/trails/"+id+"/claimed"),
       branchHead,
   )
   err := repo.Storer.SetReference(ref)
   ```

2. State checking via `repo.Reference()` existence
3. Discovery: combine `Store.List()` with state filtering

### Phase 3: Commands
**Files:** `trail.go`, `trail_runner.go`, update `root.go`

1. `entire trail list` - table output with ID, title, branch, state
2. `entire trail show <id>` - detailed view
3. `entire trail create` - interactive or flags
4. `entire trail-runner start` / `run-once`
5. `entire trail run <id>` - manual execution

**Pattern from:** `rewind.go`, `validate.go`

### Phase 4: Execution Engine
**Files:** `trail/runner.go`, `trail/worktree.go`

1. Worktree management (git CLI):
   ```go
   exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "worktree", "add", path, branch)
   exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "worktree", "remove", path)
   ```

2. Agent invocation (pattern from `e2e_test/agent_runner.go`):
   ```go
   cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "claude", "--model", model, "-p", prompt)
   cmd.Dir = worktreePath
   ```

3. Runner loop:
   - Discover open trails
   - Claim atomically (skip if fails)
   - Create worktree
   - Run agent with trail description
   - Update state on completion/failure
   - Cleanup worktree

### Phase 5: Testing
**Files:** `trail/trail_test.go`, `integration_test/trail_test.go`

1. Unit tests for ID validation, store CRUD, state transitions
2. Integration tests for end-to-end flow
3. Use `t.Parallel()` per CLAUDE.md guidelines

## Critical Files to Reference

| Purpose | File |
|---------|------|
| Orphan branch ops | `checkpoint/committed.go` |
| ID type pattern | `checkpoint/id/id.go` |
| Agent invocation | `e2e_test/agent_runner.go` |
| Command structure | `rewind.go` |
| Validation helpers | `validation/validators.go` |
| Worktree paths | `paths/worktree.go` |

## Verification

1. **Unit tests:** `mise run test`
2. **Integration tests:** `mise run test:integration`
3. **Manual testing:**
   ```bash
   # Create a trail
   entire trail create "Test task" --branch test/trail-runner

   # List trails
   entire trail list

   # Show trail details
   entire trail show <id>

   # Run single trail
   entire trail run <id>

   # Run trail runner once
   entire trail-runner run-once

   # Daemon mode
   entire trail-runner start --daemon --poll-interval 10s
   ```

4. **Verify state refs:**
   ```bash
   git for-each-ref 'refs/entire/trails/*'
   ```

5. **Verify worktree cleanup:**
   ```bash
   ls .worktrees/
   git worktree list
   ```

     1→// Package id provides the CheckpointID type for identifying checkpoints.
     2→// This is a separate package to avoid import cycles between paths, trailers, and checkpoint.
     3→package id
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"crypto/rand"
     7→	"encoding/hex"
     8→	"encoding/json"
     9→	"fmt"
    10→	"regexp"
    11→)
    12→
    13→// CheckpointID is a 12-character hex identifier for checkpoints.
    14→// It's used to link code commits to metadata on the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
    15→//
    16→//nolint:recvcheck // UnmarshalJSON requires pointer receiver, others use value receiver - standard pattern
    17→type CheckpointID string
    18→
    19→// EmptyCheckpointID represents an unset or invalid checkpoint ID.
    20→const EmptyCheckpointID CheckpointID = ""
    21→
    22→// Pattern is the regex pattern for a valid checkpoint ID: exactly 12 lowercase hex characters.
    23→// Exported for use in other packages (e.g., trailers) to avoid pattern duplication.
    24→const Pattern = `[0-9a-f]{12}`
    25→
    26→// ShortIDLength is the standard length for truncating IDs for display purposes.
    27→// Used for tool use IDs, session IDs, and commit hashes in logs and messages.
    28→const ShortIDLength = 12
    29→
    30→// checkpointIDRegex validates the format: exactly 12 lowercase hex characters.
    31→var checkpointIDRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`^` + Pattern + `$`)
    32→
    33→// NewCheckpointID creates a CheckpointID from a string, validating its format.
    34→// Returns an error if the string is not a valid 12-character hex ID.
    35→func NewCheckpointID(s string) (CheckpointID, error) {
    36→	if err := Validate(s); err != nil {
    37→		return EmptyCheckpointID, err
    38→	}
    39→	return CheckpointID(s), nil
    40→}
    41→
    42→// MustCheckpointID creates a CheckpointID from a string, panicking if invalid.
    43→// Use only when the ID is known to be valid (e.g., from trusted sources).
    44→func MustCheckpointID(s string) CheckpointID {
    45→	id, err := NewCheckpointID(s)
    46→	if err != nil {
    47→		panic(err)
    48→	}
    49→	return id
    50→}
    51→
    52→// Generate creates a new random 12-character hex checkpoint ID.
    53→func Generate() (CheckpointID, error) {
    54→	bytes := make([]byte, 6) // 6 bytes = 12 hex chars
    55→	if _, err := rand.Read(bytes); err != nil {
    56→		return EmptyCheckpointID, fmt.Errorf("failed to generate random checkpoint ID: %w", err)
    57→	}
    58→	return CheckpointID(hex.EncodeToString(bytes)), nil
    59→}
    60→
    61→// Validate checks if a string is a valid checkpoint ID format.
    62→// Returns an error if invalid, nil if valid.
    63→func Validate(s string) error {
    64→	if !checkpointIDRegex.MatchString(s) {
    65→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid checkpoint ID %q: must be 12 lowercase hex characters", s)
    66→	}
    67→	return nil
    68→}
    69→
    70→// String returns the checkpoint ID as a string.
    71→func (id CheckpointID) String() string {
    72→	return string(id)
    73→}
    74→
    75→// IsEmpty returns true if the checkpoint ID is empty or unset.
    76→func (id CheckpointID) IsEmpty() bool {
    77→	return id == EmptyCheckpointID
    78→}
    79→
    80→// Path returns the sharded path for this checkpoint ID on entire/checkpoints/v1.
    81→// Uses first 2 characters as shard (256 buckets), remaining as folder name.
    82→// Example: "a3b2c4d5e6f7" -> "a3/b2c4d5e6f7"
    83→func (id CheckpointID) Path() string {
    84→	if len(id) < 3 {
    85→		return string(id)
    86→	}
    87→	return string(id[:2]) + "/" + string(id[2:])
    88→}
    89→
    90→// MarshalJSON implements json.Marshaler.
    91→func (id CheckpointID) MarshalJSON() ([]byte, error) {
    92→	data, err := json.Marshal(string(id))
    93→	if err != nil {
    94→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal checkpoint ID: %w", err)
    95→	}
    96→	return data, nil
    97→}
    98→
    99→// UnmarshalJSON implements json.Unmarshaler with validation.
   100→// Returns an error if the JSON string is not a valid 12-character hex ID.
   101→// Empty strings are allowed and result in EmptyCheckpointID.
   102→func (id *CheckpointID) UnmarshalJSON(data []byte) error {
   103→	var s string
   104→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &s); err != nil {
   105→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal checkpoint ID: %w", err)
   106→	}
   107→	// Allow empty strings (represents unset checkpoint ID)
   108→	if s == "" {
   109→		*id = EmptyCheckpointID
   110→		return nil
   111→	}
   112→	if err := Validate(s); err != nil {
   113→		return err
   114→	}
   115→	*id = CheckpointID(s)
   116→	return nil
   117→}
   118→

     1→//go:build e2e
     2→
     3→package e2e
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"bytes"
     7→	"context"
     8→	"errors"
     9→	"fmt"
    10→	"os"
    11→	"os/exec"
    12→	"strings"
    13→	"time"

    15→
    16→// AgentNameClaudeCode is the name for Claude Code agent.
    17→const AgentNameClaudeCode = "claude-code"
    18→
    19→// AgentNameGemini is the name for Gemini CLI agent.
    20→const AgentNameGemini = "gemini"
    21→
    22→// AgentRunner abstracts invoking a coding agent for e2e tests.
    23→// This follows the multi-agent pattern from cmd/entire/cli/agent/agent.go.
    24→type AgentRunner interface {
    25→	// Name returns the agent name (e.g., "claude-code", "gemini-cli")
    26→	Name() string
    27→
    28→	// IsAvailable checks if the agent CLI is installed and authenticated
    29→	IsAvailable() (bool, error)
    30→
    31→	// RunPrompt executes a prompt and returns the result
    32→	RunPrompt(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string) (*AgentResult, error)
    33→
    34→	// RunPromptWithTools executes with specific allowed tools
    35→	RunPromptWithTools(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string, tools []string) (*AgentResult, error)
    36→}
    37→
    38→// AgentResult holds the result of an agent invocation.
    39→type AgentResult struct {
    40→	Stdout   string
    41→	Stderr   string
    42→	ExitCode int
    43→	Duration time.Duration
    44→}
    45→
    46→// AgentRunnerConfig holds configuration for agent runners.
    47→type AgentRunnerConfig struct {
    48→	Model   string        // Model to use (e.g., "haiku" for Claude)
    49→	Timeout time.Duration // Timeout per prompt
    50→}
    51→
    52→// NewAgentRunner creates an agent runner based on the agent name.
    53→//
    54→//nolint:ireturn // factory pattern intentionally returns interface
    55→func NewAgentRunner(name string, config AgentRunnerConfig) AgentRunner {
    56→	switch name {
    57→	case AgentNameClaudeCode:
    58→		return NewClaudeCodeRunner(config)
    59→	case AgentNameGemini:
    60→		return NewGeminiCLIRunner(config)
    61→	default:
    62→		// Return a runner that reports as unavailable
    63→		return &unavailableRunner{name: name}
    64→	}
    65→}
    66→
    67→// unavailableRunner is returned for unknown agent names.
    68→type unavailableRunner struct {
    69→	name string
    70→}
    71→
    72→func (r *unavailableRunner) Name() string { return r.name }
    73→
    74→func (r *unavailableRunner) IsAvailable() (bool, error) {
    75→	return false, fmt.Errorf("unknown agent: %s", r.name)
    76→}
    77→
    78→func (r *unavailableRunner) RunPrompt(_ context.Context, _ string, _ string) (*AgentResult, error) {
    79→	return nil, fmt.Errorf("agent %s is not available", r.name)
    80→}
    81→
    82→func (r *unavailableRunner) RunPromptWithTools(_ context.Context, _ string, _ string, _ []string) (*AgentResult, error) {
    83→	return nil, fmt.Errorf("agent %s is not available", r.name)
    84→}
    85→
    86→// ClaudeCodeRunner implements AgentRunner for Claude Code CLI.
    87→type ClaudeCodeRunner struct {
    88→	Model        string
    89→	Timeout      time.Duration
    90→	AllowedTools []string
    91→}
    92→
    93→// NewClaudeCodeRunner creates a new Claude Code runner with the given config.
    94→func NewClaudeCodeRunner(config AgentRunnerConfig) *ClaudeCodeRunner {
    95→	model := config.Model
    96→	if model == "" {
    97→		model = os.Getenv("E2E_CLAUDE_MODEL")
    98→		if model == "" {
    99→			model = "haiku"
   100→		}
   101→	}
   102→
   103→	timeout := config.Timeout
   104→	if timeout == 0 {
   105→		if envTimeout := os.Getenv("E2E_TIMEOUT"); envTimeout != "" {
   106→			if parsed, err := time.ParseDuration(envTimeout); err == nil {
   107→				timeout = parsed
   108→			}
   109→		}
   110→		if timeout == 0 {
   111→			timeout = 2 * time.Minute
   112→		}
   113→	}
   114→
   115→	return &ClaudeCodeRunner{
   116→		Model:        model,
   117→		Timeout:      timeout,
   118→		AllowedTools: []string{"Edit", "Read", "Write", "Bash", "Glob", "Grep"},
   119→	}
   120→}
   121→
   122→func (r *ClaudeCodeRunner) Name() string {
   123→	return AgentNameClaudeCode
   124→}
   125→
   126→// IsAvailable checks if Claude CLI is installed and responds to --version.
   127→// Note: This does NOT verify authentication status. Claude Code uses OAuth
   128→// authentication (via `claude login`), not ANTHROPIC_API_KEY. If the CLI is
   129→// installed but not logged in, tests will fail at RunPrompt time.
   130→func (r *ClaudeCodeRunner) IsAvailable() (bool, error) {
   131→	// Check if claude CLI is in PATH
   132→	if _, err := exec.LookPath("claude"); err != nil {
   133→		return false, fmt.Errorf("claude CLI not found in PATH: %w", err)
   134→	}
   135→
   136→	// Check if claude is working (--version doesn't require auth)
   137→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)
   138→	defer cancel()
   139→
   140→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "claude", "--version")
   141→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   142→		return false, fmt.Errorf("claude CLI not working: %w", err)
   143→	}
   144→
   145→	return true, nil
   146→}
   147→
   148→func (r *ClaudeCodeRunner) RunPrompt(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string) (*AgentResult, error) {
   149→	return r.RunPromptWithTools(ctx, workDir, prompt, r.AllowedTools)
   150→}
   151→
   152→func (r *ClaudeCodeRunner) RunPromptWithTools(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string, tools []string) (*AgentResult, error) {
   153→	// Build command: claude --model <model> -p "<prompt>" --allowedTools <tools>
   154→	args := []string{
   155→		"--model", r.Model,
   156→		"-p", prompt,
   157→	}
   158→
   159→	if len(tools) > 0 {
   160→		// Claude CLI expects each tool as a separate argument after --allowedTools
   161→		// e.g., --allowedTools Edit Read Bash (not --allowedTools "Edit,Read,Bash")
   162→		args = append(args, "--allowedTools")
   163→		args = append(args, tools...)
   164→	}
   165→
   166→	// Create context with timeout
   167→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, r.Timeout)
   168→	defer cancel()
   169→
   170→	//nolint:gosec // args are constructed from trusted config, not user input
   171→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "claude", args...)
   172→	cmd.Dir = workDir
   173→
   174→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   175→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   176→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   177→
   178→	start := time.Now()
   179→	err := cmd.Run()
   180→	duration := time.Since(start)
   181→
   182→	result := &AgentResult{
   183→		Stdout:   stdout.String(),
   184→		Stderr:   stderr.String(),
   185→		Duration: duration,
   186→	}
   187→
   188→	if err != nil {
   189→		exitErr := &exec.ExitError{}
   190→		if errors.As(err, &exitErr) {
   191→			result.ExitCode = exitErr.ExitCode()
   192→		} else {
   193→			result.ExitCode = -1
   194→		}
   195→		//nolint:wrapcheck // error is from exec.Run, caller can check ExitCode in result
   196→		return result, err
   197→	}
   198→
   199→	result.ExitCode = 0
   200→	return result, nil
   201→}
   202→
   203→// GeminiCLIRunner implements AgentRunner for Gemini CLI.
   204→// See: https://github.com/google-gemini/gemini-cli
   205→type GeminiCLIRunner struct {
   206→	Model   string
   207→	Timeout time.Duration
   208→}
   209→
   210→// NewGeminiCLIRunner creates a new Gemini CLI runner with the given config.
   211→func NewGeminiCLIRunner(config AgentRunnerConfig) *GeminiCLIRunner {
   212→	model := config.Model
   213→	if model == "" {
   214→		model = os.Getenv("E2E_GEMINI_MODEL")
   215→		// Default to gemini-2.5-flash if not specified
   216→		if model == "" {
   217→			model = "gemini-2.5-flash"
   218→		}
   219→	}
   220→
   221→	timeout := config.Timeout
   222→	if timeout == 0 {
   223→		if envTimeout := os.Getenv("E2E_TIMEOUT"); envTimeout != "" {
   224→			if parsed, err := time.ParseDuration(envTimeout); err == nil {
   225→				timeout = parsed
   226→			}
   227→		}
   228→		if timeout == 0 {
   229→			timeout = 2 * time.Minute
   230→		}
   231→	}
   232→
   233→	return &GeminiCLIRunner{
   234→		Model:   model,
   235→		Timeout: timeout,
   236→	}
   237→}
   238→
   239→func (r *GeminiCLIRunner) Name() string {
   240→	return AgentNameGemini
   241→}
   242→
   243→// IsAvailable checks if Gemini CLI is installed and authenticated.
   244→func (r *GeminiCLIRunner) IsAvailable() (bool, error) {
   245→	// Check if gemini CLI is in PATH
   246→	if _, err := exec.LookPath("gemini"); err != nil {
   247→		return false, fmt.Errorf("gemini CLI not found in PATH: %w", err)
   248→	}
   249→
   250→	// Check if gemini is working (--version doesn't require auth)
   251→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)
   252→	defer cancel()
   253→
   254→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "gemini", "--version")
   255→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   256→		return false, fmt.Errorf("gemini CLI not working: %w", err)
   257→	}
   258→
   259→	return true, nil
   260→}
   261→
   262→func (r *GeminiCLIRunner) RunPrompt(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string) (*AgentResult, error) {
   263→	return r.RunPromptWithTools(ctx, workDir, prompt, nil)
   264→}
   265→
   266→func (r *GeminiCLIRunner) RunPromptWithTools(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string, tools []string) (*AgentResult, error) {
   267→	// Build command: gemini -m <model> -p "<prompt>" --approval-mode auto_edit --allowed-tools <tools>
   268→	// --approval-mode auto_edit: auto-approves edit tools (write_file, replace) while prompting for others
   269→	// --allowed-tools: comma-separated list of tools that bypass confirmation (e.g., git commands)
   270→	args := []string{
   271→		"-m", r.Model,
   272→		"-p", prompt,
   273→		"--approval-mode", "auto_edit",
   274→	}
   275→
   276→	// Add default git tools that should be allowed without confirmation
   277→	defaultAllowedTools := []string{
   278→		"ShellTool(git status)",
   279→		"ShellTool(git add)",
   280→		"ShellTool(git commit)",
   281→		"ShellTool(git diff)",
   282→		"ShellTool(git log)",
   283→	}
   284→
   285→	// Merge with any additional tools passed in
   286→	allTools := append(defaultAllowedTools, tools...)
   287→	if len(allTools) > 0 {
   288→		args = append(args, "--allowed-tools", strings.Join(allTools, ","))
   289→	}
   290→
   291→	// Create context with timeout
   292→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, r.Timeout)
   293→	defer cancel()
   294→
   295→	//nolint:gosec // args are constructed from trusted config, not user input
   296→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "gemini", args...)
   297→	cmd.Dir = workDir
   298→
   299→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   300→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   301→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   302→
   303→	start := time.Now()
   304→	err := cmd.Run()
   305→	duration := time.Since(start)
   306→
   307→	result := &AgentResult{
   308→		Stdout:   stdout.String(),
   309→		Stderr:   stderr.String(),
   310→		Duration: duration,
   311→	}
   312→
   313→	if err != nil {
   314→		exitErr := &exec.ExitError{}
   315→		if errors.As(err, &exitErr) {
   316→			result.ExitCode = exitErr.ExitCode()
   317→		} else {
   318→			result.ExitCode = -1
   319→		}
   320→		//nolint:wrapcheck // error is from exec.Run, caller can check ExitCode in result
   321→		return result, err
   322→	}
   323→
   324→	result.ExitCode = 0
   325→	return result, nil
   326→}
   327→
