    36→// errStopIteration is used to stop commit iteration early in GetCheckpointAuthor.
    37→var errStopIteration = errors.New("stop iteration")
    38→
    39→// WriteCommitted writes a committed checkpoint to the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
    40→// Checkpoints are stored at sharded paths: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/
    41→//
    42→// For task checkpoints (IsTask=true), additional files are written under tasks/<tool-use-id>/:
    43→//   - For incremental checkpoints: checkpoints/NNN-<tool-use-id>.json
    44→//   - For final checkpoints: checkpoint.json and agent-<agent-id>.jsonl
    45→func (s *GitStore) WriteCommitted(ctx context.Context, opts WriteCommittedOptions) error {
    46→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
    47→
    48→	// Validate identifiers to prevent path traversal and malformed data
    49→	if opts.CheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
    50→		return errors.New("invalid checkpoint options: checkpoint ID is required")
    51→	}
    52→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(opts.SessionID); err != nil {
    53→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid checkpoint options: %w", err)
    54→	}
    55→	if err := validation.ValidateToolUseID(opts.ToolUseID); err != nil {
    56→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid checkpoint options: %w", err)
    57→	}
    58→	if err := validation.ValidateAgentID(opts.AgentID); err != nil {
    59→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid checkpoint options: %w", err)
    60→	}
    61→
    62→	// Ensure sessions branch exists
    63→	if err := s.ensureSessionsBranch(); err != nil {
    64→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to ensure sessions branch: %w", err)
    65→	}
    66→
    67→	// Get current branch tip and flatten tree
    68→	ref, entries, err := s.getSessionsBranchEntries()
    69→	if err != nil {
    70→		return err
    71→	}
    72→
    73→	// Use sharded path: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/
    74→	basePath := opts.CheckpointID.Path() + "/"
    75→
    76→	// Track task metadata path for commit trailer
    77→	var taskMetadataPath string
    78→
    79→	// Handle task checkpoints
    80→	if opts.IsTask && opts.ToolUseID != "" {
    81→		taskMetadataPath, err = s.writeTaskCheckpointEntries(opts, basePath, entries)
    82→		if err != nil {
    83→			return err
    84→		}
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	// Write standard checkpoint entries (transcript, prompts, context, metadata)
    88→	if err := s.writeStandardCheckpointEntries(opts, basePath, entries); err != nil {
    89→		return err
    90→	}
    91→
    92→	// Build and commit
    93→	newTreeHash, err := BuildTreeFromEntries(s.repo, entries)
    94→	if err != nil {
    95→		return err
    96→	}
    97→
    98→	commitMsg := s.buildCommitMessage(opts, taskMetadataPath)
    99→	newCommitHash, err := s.createCommit(newTreeHash, ref.Hash(), commitMsg, opts.AuthorName, opts.AuthorEmail)
   100→	if err != nil {
   101→		return err
   102→	}
   103→
   104→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   105→	newRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, newCommitHash)
   106→	if err := s.repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {
   107→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to set branch reference: %w", err)
   108→	}
   109→
   110→	return nil
   111→}
   112→
   113→// getSessionsBranchEntries returns the sessions branch reference and flattened tree entries.
   114→func (s *GitStore) getSessionsBranchEntries() (*plumbing.Reference, map[string]object.TreeEntry, error) {
   115→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   116→	ref, err := s.repo.Reference(refName, true)
   117→	if err != nil {
   118→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get sessions branch reference: %w", err)
   119→	}
   120→
   121→	parentCommit, err := s.repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   122→	if err != nil {
   123→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit object: %w", err)
   124→	}
   125→
   126→	baseTree, err := parentCommit.Tree()
   127→	if err != nil {
   128→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit tree: %w", err)
   129→	}
   130→
   131→	entries := make(map[string]object.TreeEntry)
   132→	if err := FlattenTree(s.repo, baseTree, "", entries); err != nil {
   133→		return nil, nil, err
   134→	}
   135→
   136→	return ref, entries, nil
   137→}
   138→
   139→// writeTaskCheckpointEntries writes task-specific checkpoint entries and returns the task metadata path.
   140→func (s *GitStore) writeTaskCheckpointEntries(opts WriteCommittedOptions, basePath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) (string, error) {
   141→	taskPath := basePath + "tasks/" + opts.ToolUseID + "/"
   142→
   143→	if opts.IsIncremental {
   144→		return s.writeIncrementalTaskCheckpoint(opts, taskPath, entries)
   145→	}
   146→	return s.writeFinalTaskCheckpoint(opts, taskPath, entries)
   147→}
   148→
   149→// writeIncrementalTaskCheckpoint writes an incremental checkpoint file during task execution.
   150→func (s *GitStore) writeIncrementalTaskCheckpoint(opts WriteCommittedOptions, taskPath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) (string, error) {
   151→	incData, err := redact.JSONLBytes(opts.IncrementalData)
   152→	if err != nil {
   153→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to redact incremental checkpoint: %w", err)
   154→	}
   155→	checkpoint := incrementalCheckpointData{
   156→		Type:      opts.IncrementalType,
   157→		ToolUseID: opts.ToolUseID,
   158→		Timestamp: time.Now().UTC(),
   159→		Data:      incData,
   160→	}
   161→	cpData, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(checkpoint, "", "  ")
   162→	if err != nil {
   163→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal incremental checkpoint: %w", err)
   164→	}
   165→	cpBlobHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, cpData)
   166→	if err != nil {
   167→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to create incremental checkpoint blob: %w", err)
   168→	}
   169→
   170→	cpFilename := fmt.Sprintf("%03d-%s.json", opts.IncrementalSequence, opts.ToolUseID)
   171→	cpPath := taskPath + "checkpoints/" + cpFilename
   172→	entries[cpPath] = object.TreeEntry{
   173→		Name: cpPath,
   174→		Mode: filemode.Regular,
   175→		Hash: cpBlobHash,
   176→	}
   177→	return cpPath, nil
   178→}
   179→
   180→// writeFinalTaskCheckpoint writes the final checkpoint.json and subagent transcript.
   181→func (s *GitStore) writeFinalTaskCheckpoint(opts WriteCommittedOptions, taskPath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) (string, error) {
   182→	checkpoint := taskCheckpointData{
   183→		SessionID:      opts.SessionID,
   184→		ToolUseID:      opts.ToolUseID,
   185→		CheckpointUUID: opts.CheckpointUUID,
   186→		AgentID:        opts.AgentID,
   187→	}
   188→	checkpointData, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(checkpoint, "", "  ")
   189→	if err != nil {
   190→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal task checkpoint: %w", err)
   191→	}
   192→	blobHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, checkpointData)
   193→	if err != nil {
   194→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to create task checkpoint blob: %w", err)
   195→	}
   196→
   197→	checkpointFile := taskPath + "checkpoint.json"
   198→	entries[checkpointFile] = object.TreeEntry{
   199→		Name: checkpointFile,
   200→		Mode: filemode.Regular,
   201→		Hash: blobHash,
   202→	}
   203→
   204→	// Write subagent transcript if available
   205→	if opts.SubagentTranscriptPath != "" && opts.AgentID != "" {
   206→		agentContent, readErr := os.ReadFile(opts.SubagentTranscriptPath)
   207→		if readErr == nil {
   208→			// Try JSONL-aware redaction first; fall back to plain string redaction
   209→			// if the content is not valid JSONL (avoids silently dropping the transcript).
   210→			redacted, jsonlErr := redact.JSONLBytes(agentContent)
   211→			if jsonlErr != nil {
   212→				logging.Warn(context.Background(), "subagent transcript is not valid JSONL, falling back to plain redaction",
   213→					slog.String("path", opts.SubagentTranscriptPath),
   214→					slog.String("error", jsonlErr.Error()),
   215→				)
   216→				redacted = redact.Bytes(agentContent)
   217→			}
   218→			agentContent = redacted
   219→
   220→			agentBlobHash, agentBlobErr := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, agentContent)
   221→			if agentBlobErr == nil {
   222→				agentPath := taskPath + "agent-" + opts.AgentID + ".jsonl"
   223→				entries[agentPath] = object.TreeEntry{
   224→					Name: agentPath,
   225→					Mode: filemode.Regular,
   226→					Hash: agentBlobHash,
   227→				}
   228→			}
   229→		}
   230→	}
   231→
   232→	// Return task path without trailing slash
   233→	return taskPath[:len(taskPath)-1], nil
   234→}
   235→
   236→// writeStandardCheckpointEntries writes session files to numbered subdirectories and
   237→// maintains a CheckpointSummary at the root level with aggregated statistics.
   238→//
   239→// Structure:
   240→//
   241→//	basePath/
   242→//	├── metadata.json         # CheckpointSummary (aggregated stats)
   243→//	├── 1/                    # First session
   244→//	│   ├── metadata.json     # CommittedMetadata (session-specific, includes initial_attribution)
   245→//	│   ├── full.jsonl
   246→//	│   ├── prompt.txt
   247→//	│   ├── context.md
   248→//	│   └── content_hash.txt
   249→//	├── 2/                    # Second session
   250→//	└── ...
   251→func (s *GitStore) writeStandardCheckpointEntries(opts WriteCommittedOptions, basePath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) error {
   252→	// Read existing summary to get current session count
   253→	var existingSummary *CheckpointSummary
   254→	metadataPath := basePath + paths.MetadataFileName
   255→	if entry, exists := entries[metadataPath]; exists {
   256→		existing, err := s.readSummaryFromBlob(entry.Hash)
   257→		if err == nil {
   258→			existingSummary = existing
   259→		}
   260→	}
   261→
   262→	// Determine session index: reuse existing slot if session ID matches, otherwise append
   263→	sessionIndex := s.findSessionIndex(basePath, existingSummary, entries, opts.SessionID)
   264→
   265→	// Write session files to numbered subdirectory
   266→	sessionPath := fmt.Sprintf("%s%d/", basePath, sessionIndex)
   267→	sessionFilePaths, err := s.writeSessionToSubdirectory(opts, sessionPath, entries)
   268→	if err != nil {
   269→		return err
   270→	}
   271→
   272→	// Copy additional metadata files from directory if specified (to session subdirectory)
   273→	if opts.MetadataDir != "" {
   274→		if err := s.copyMetadataDir(opts.MetadataDir, sessionPath, entries); err != nil {
   275→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to copy metadata directory: %w", err)
   276→		}
   277→	}
   278→
   279→	// Build the sessions array
   280→	var sessions []SessionFilePaths
   281→	if existingSummary != nil {
   282→		sessions = make([]SessionFilePaths, max(len(existingSummary.Sessions), sessionIndex+1))
   283→		copy(sessions, existingSummary.Sessions)
   284→	} else {
   285→		sessions = make([]SessionFilePaths, 1)
   286→	}
   287→	sessions[sessionIndex] = sessionFilePaths
   288→
   289→	// Update root metadata.json with CheckpointSummary
   290→	return s.writeCheckpointSummary(opts, basePath, entries, sessions)
   291→}
   292→
   293→// writeSessionToSubdirectory writes a single session's files to a numbered subdirectory.
   294→// Returns the absolute file paths from the git tree root for the sessions map.
   295→func (s *GitStore) writeSessionToSubdirectory(opts WriteCommittedOptions, sessionPath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) (SessionFilePaths, error) {
   296→	filePaths := SessionFilePaths{}
   297→
   298→	// Clear any existing entries at this path so stale files from a previous
   299→	// write (e.g. prompt.txt, context.md) don't persist on overwrite.
   300→	for key := range entries {
   301→		if strings.HasPrefix(key, sessionPath) {
   302→			delete(entries, key)
   303→		}
   304→	}
   305→
   306→	// Write transcript
   307→	if err := s.writeTranscript(opts, sessionPath, entries); err != nil {
   308→		return filePaths, err
   309→	}
   310→	filePaths.Transcript = "/" + sessionPath + paths.TranscriptFileName
   311→	filePaths.ContentHash = "/" + sessionPath + paths.ContentHashFileName
   312→
   313→	// Write prompts
   314→	if len(opts.Prompts) > 0 {
   315→		promptContent := redact.String(strings.Join(opts.Prompts, "\n\n---\n\n"))
   316→		blobHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, []byte(promptContent))
   317→		if err != nil {
   318→			return filePaths, err
   319→		}
   320→		entries[sessionPath+paths.PromptFileName] = object.TreeEntry{
   321→			Name: sessionPath + paths.PromptFileName,
   322→			Mode: filemode.Regular,
   323→			Hash: blobHash,
   324→		}
   325→		filePaths.Prompt = "/" + sessionPath + paths.PromptFileName
   326→	}
   327→
   328→	// Write context
   329→	if len(opts.Context) > 0 {
   330→		blobHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, redact.Bytes(opts.Context))
   331→		if err != nil {
   332→			return filePaths, err
   333→		}
   334→		entries[sessionPath+paths.ContextFileName] = object.TreeEntry{
   335→			Name: sessionPath + paths.ContextFileName,
   336→			Mode: filemode.Regular,
   337→			Hash: blobHash,
   338→		}
   339→		filePaths.Context = "/" + sessionPath + paths.ContextFileName
   340→	}
   341→
   342→	// Write session-level metadata.json (CommittedMetadata with all fields including initial_attribution)
   343→	sessionMetadata := CommittedMetadata{
   344→		CheckpointID:                opts.CheckpointID,
   345→		SessionID:                   opts.SessionID,
   346→		Strategy:                    opts.Strategy,
   347→		CreatedAt:                   time.Now().UTC(),
   348→		Branch:                      opts.Branch,
   349→		CheckpointsCount:            opts.CheckpointsCount,
   350→		FilesTouched:                opts.FilesTouched,
   351→		Agent:                       opts.Agent,
   352→		TurnID:                      opts.TurnID,
   353→		IsTask:                      opts.IsTask,
   354→		ToolUseID:                   opts.ToolUseID,
   355→		TranscriptIdentifierAtStart: opts.TranscriptIdentifierAtStart,
   356→		CheckpointTranscriptStart:   opts.CheckpointTranscriptStart,
   357→		TranscriptLinesAtStart:      opts.CheckpointTranscriptStart, // Deprecated: kept for backward compat
   358→		TokenUsage:                  opts.TokenUsage,
   359→		InitialAttribution:          opts.InitialAttribution,
   360→		Summary:                     redactSummary(opts.Summary),
   361→		CLIVersion:                  buildinfo.Version,
   362→	}
   363→
   364→	metadataJSON, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(sessionMetadata, "", "  ")
   365→	if err != nil {
   366→		return filePaths, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal session metadata: %w", err)
   367→	}
   368→	metadataHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, metadataJSON)
   369→	if err != nil {
   370→		return filePaths, err
   371→	}
   372→	entries[sessionPath+paths.MetadataFileName] = object.TreeEntry{
   373→		Name: sessionPath + paths.MetadataFileName,
   374→		Mode: filemode.Regular,
   375→		Hash: metadataHash,
   376→	}
   377→	filePaths.Metadata = "/" + sessionPath + paths.MetadataFileName
   378→
   379→	return filePaths, nil
   380→}
   381→
   382→// writeCheckpointSummary writes the root-level CheckpointSummary with aggregated statistics.
   383→// sessions is the complete sessions array (already built by the caller).
   384→func (s *GitStore) writeCheckpointSummary(opts WriteCommittedOptions, basePath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry, sessions []SessionFilePaths) error {
   385→	checkpointsCount, filesTouched, tokenUsage, err :=
   386→		s.reaggregateFromEntries(basePath, len(sessions), entries)
   387→	if err != nil {
   388→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to aggregate session stats: %w", err)
   389→	}
   390→
   391→	summary := CheckpointSummary{
   392→		CheckpointID:     opts.CheckpointID,
   393→		CLIVersion:       buildinfo.Version,
   394→		Strategy:         opts.Strategy,
   395→		Branch:           opts.Branch,
   396→		CheckpointsCount: checkpointsCount,
   397→		FilesTouched:     filesTouched,
   398→		Sessions:         sessions,
   399→		TokenUsage:       tokenUsage,
   400→	}
   401→
   402→	metadataJSON, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(summary, "", "  ")
   403→	if err != nil {
   404→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal checkpoint summary: %w", err)
   405→	}
   406→	metadataHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, metadataJSON)
   407→	if err != nil {
   408→		return err
   409→	}
   410→	entries[basePath+paths.MetadataFileName] = object.TreeEntry{
   411→		Name: basePath + paths.MetadataFileName,
   412→		Mode: filemode.Regular,
   413→		Hash: metadataHash,
   414→	}
   415→	return nil
   416→}
   417→
   418→// findSessionIndex returns the index of an existing session with the given ID,
   419→// or the next available index if not found. This prevents duplicate session entries.
   420→func (s *GitStore) findSessionIndex(basePath string, existingSummary *CheckpointSummary, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry, sessionID string) int {
   421→	if existingSummary == nil {
   422→		return 0
   423→	}
   424→	for i := range len(existingSummary.Sessions) {
   425→		path := fmt.Sprintf("%s%d/%s", basePath, i, paths.MetadataFileName)
   426→		if entry, exists := entries[path]; exists {
   427→			meta, err := s.readMetadataFromBlob(entry.Hash)
   428→			if err != nil {
   429→				logging.Warn(context.Background(), "failed to read session metadata during dedup check",
   430→					slog.Int("session_index", i),
   431→					slog.String("session_id", sessionID),
   432→					slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   433→				)
   434→				continue
   435→			}
   436→			if meta.SessionID == sessionID {
   437→				return i
   438→			}
   439→		}
   440→	}
   441→	return len(existingSummary.Sessions)
   442→}
   443→
   444→// reaggregateFromEntries reads all session metadata from the entries map and
   445→// reaggregates CheckpointsCount, FilesTouched, and TokenUsage.
   446→func (s *GitStore) reaggregateFromEntries(basePath string, sessionCount int, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) (int, []string, *agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   447→	var totalCount int
   448→	var allFiles []string
   449→	var totalTokens *agent.TokenUsage
   450→
   451→	for i := range sessionCount {
   452→		path := fmt.Sprintf("%s%d/%s", basePath, i, paths.MetadataFileName)
   453→		entry, exists := entries[path]
   454→		if !exists {
   455→			return 0, nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("session %d metadata not found at %s", i, path)
   456→		}
   457→		meta, err := s.readMetadataFromBlob(entry.Hash)
   458→		if err != nil {
   459→			return 0, nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read session %d metadata: %w", i, err)
   460→		}
   461→		totalCount += meta.CheckpointsCount
   462→		allFiles = mergeFilesTouched(allFiles, meta.FilesTouched)
   463→		totalTokens = aggregateTokenUsage(totalTokens, meta.TokenUsage)
   464→	}
   465→
   466→	return totalCount, allFiles, totalTokens, nil
   467→}
   468→
   469→// readJSONFromBlob reads JSON from a blob hash and decodes it to the given type.
   470→func readJSONFromBlob[T any](repo *git.Repository, hash plumbing.Hash) (*T, error) {
   471→	blob, err := repo.BlobObject(hash)
   472→	if err != nil {
   473→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get blob: %w", err)
   474→	}
   475→
   476→	reader, err := blob.Reader()
   477→	if err != nil {
   478→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get blob reader: %w", err)
   479→	}
   480→	defer reader.Close()
   481→
   482→	var result T
   483→	if err := json.NewDecoder(reader).Decode(&result); err != nil {
   484→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to decode: %w", err)
   485→	}
   486→
   487→	return &result, nil
   488→}
   489→
   490→// readSummaryFromBlob reads CheckpointSummary from a blob hash.
   491→func (s *GitStore) readSummaryFromBlob(hash plumbing.Hash) (*CheckpointSummary, error) {
   492→	return readJSONFromBlob[CheckpointSummary](s.repo, hash)
   493→}
   494→
   495→// aggregateTokenUsage sums two TokenUsage structs.
   496→// Returns nil if both inputs are nil.
   497→func aggregateTokenUsage(a, b *agent.TokenUsage) *agent.TokenUsage {
   498→	if a == nil && b == nil {
   499→		return nil
   500→	}
   501→	result := &agent.TokenUsage{}
   502→	if a != nil {
   503→		result.InputTokens = a.InputTokens
   504→		result.CacheCreationTokens = a.CacheCreationTokens
   505→		result.CacheReadTokens = a.CacheReadTokens
   506→		result.OutputTokens = a.OutputTokens
   507→		result.APICallCount = a.APICallCount
   508→	}
   509→	if b != nil {
   510→		result.InputTokens += b.InputTokens
   511→		result.CacheCreationTokens += b.CacheCreationTokens
   512→		result.CacheReadTokens += b.CacheReadTokens
   513→		result.OutputTokens += b.OutputTokens
   514→		result.APICallCount += b.APICallCount
   515→	}
   516→	return result
   517→}
   518→
   519→// writeTranscript writes the transcript file from in-memory content or file path.
   520→// If the transcript exceeds MaxChunkSize, it's split into multiple chunk files.
   521→func (s *GitStore) writeTranscript(opts WriteCommittedOptions, basePath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) error {
   522→	transcript := opts.Transcript
   523→	if len(transcript) == 0 && opts.TranscriptPath != "" {
   524→		var readErr error
   525→		transcript, readErr = os.ReadFile(opts.TranscriptPath)
   526→		if readErr != nil {
   527→			// Non-fatal: transcript may not exist yet
   528→			transcript = nil
   529→		}
   530→	}
   531→	if len(transcript) == 0 {
   532→		return nil
   533→	}
   534→
   535→	// Redact secrets before chunking so content hash reflects redacted content
   536→	transcript, err := redact.JSONLBytes(transcript)
   537→	if err != nil {
   538→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to redact transcript secrets: %w", err)
   539→	}
   540→
   541→	// Chunk the transcript if it's too large
   542→	chunks, err := agent.ChunkTranscript(transcript, opts.Agent)
   543→	if err != nil {
   544→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to chunk transcript: %w", err)
   545→	}
   546→
   547→	// Write chunk files
   548→	for i, chunk := range chunks {
   549→		chunkPath := basePath + agent.ChunkFileName(paths.TranscriptFileName, i)
   550→		blobHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, chunk)
   551→		if err != nil {
   552→			return err
   553→		}
   554→		entries[chunkPath] = object.TreeEntry{
   555→			Name: chunkPath,
   556→			Mode: filemode.Regular,
   557→			Hash: blobHash,
   558→		}
   559→	}
   560→
   561→	// Content hash for deduplication (hash of full transcript)
   562→	contentHash := fmt.Sprintf("sha256:%x", sha256.Sum256(transcript))
   563→	hashBlob, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, []byte(contentHash))
   564→	if err != nil {
   565→		return err
   566→	}
   567→	entries[basePath+paths.ContentHashFileName] = object.TreeEntry{
   568→		Name: basePath + paths.ContentHashFileName,
   569→		Mode: filemode.Regular,
   570→		Hash: hashBlob,
   571→	}
   572→	return nil
   573→}
   574→
   575→// mergeFilesTouched combines two file lists, removing duplicates.
   576→func mergeFilesTouched(existing, additional []string) []string {
   577→	seen := make(map[string]bool)
   578→	var result []string
   579→
   580→	for _, f := range existing {
   581→		if !seen[f] {
   582→			seen[f] = true
   583→			result = append(result, f)
   584→		}
   585→	}
   586→	for _, f := range additional {
   587→		if !seen[f] {
   588→			seen[f] = true
   589→			result = append(result, f)
   590→		}
   591→	}
   592→
   593→	sort.Strings(result)
   594→	return result
   595→}
   596→
   597→// redactSummary returns a copy of the summary with text fields redacted.
   598→// Structural fields (Path, Line, EndLine) are preserved.
   599→// NOTE: When adding new text fields to Summary, LearningsSummary, or CodeLearning,
   600→// update this function to include them in redaction.
   601→func redactSummary(s *Summary) *Summary {
   602→	if s == nil {
   603→		return nil
   604→	}
   605→	return &Summary{
   606→		Intent:    redact.String(s.Intent),
   607→		Outcome:   redact.String(s.Outcome),
   608→		Friction:  redactStringSlice(s.Friction),
   609→		OpenItems: redactStringSlice(s.OpenItems),
   610→		Learnings: LearningsSummary{
   611→			Repo:     redactStringSlice(s.Learnings.Repo),
   612→			Workflow: redactStringSlice(s.Learnings.Workflow),
   613→			Code:     redactCodeLearnings(s.Learnings.Code),
   614→		},
   615→	}
   616→}
   617→
   618→// redactStringSlice applies redact.String to each element.
   619→func redactStringSlice(ss []string) []string {
   620→	if ss == nil {
   621→		return nil
   622→	}
   623→	out := make([]string, len(ss))
   624→	for i, s := range ss {
   625→		out[i] = redact.String(s)
   626→	}
   627→	return out
   628→}
   629→
   630→// redactCodeLearnings redacts only the Finding field, preserving Path/Line/EndLine.
   631→func redactCodeLearnings(cls []CodeLearning) []CodeLearning {
   632→	if cls == nil {
   633→		return nil
   634→	}
   635→	out := make([]CodeLearning, len(cls))
   636→	for i, cl := range cls {
   637→		out[i] = CodeLearning{
   638→			Path:    cl.Path,
   639→			Line:    cl.Line,
   640→			EndLine: cl.EndLine,
   641→			Finding: redact.String(cl.Finding),
   642→		}
   643→	}
   644→	return out
   645→}
   646→
   647→// readMetadataFromBlob reads CommittedMetadata from a blob hash.
   648→func (s *GitStore) readMetadataFromBlob(hash plumbing.Hash) (*CommittedMetadata, error) {
   649→	return readJSONFromBlob[CommittedMetadata](s.repo, hash)
   650→}
   651→
   652→// buildCommitMessage constructs the commit message with proper trailers.
   653→// The commit subject is always "Checkpoint: <id>" for consistency.
   654→// If CommitSubject is provided (e.g., for task checkpoints), it's included in the body.
   655→func (s *GitStore) buildCommitMessage(opts WriteCommittedOptions, taskMetadataPath string) string {
   656→	var commitMsg strings.Builder
   657→
   658→	// Subject line is always the checkpoint ID for consistent formatting
   659→	commitMsg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("Checkpoint: %s\n\n", opts.CheckpointID))
   660→
   661→	// Include custom description in body if provided (e.g., task checkpoint details)
   662→	if opts.CommitSubject != "" {
   663→		commitMsg.WriteString(opts.CommitSubject + "\n\n")
   664→	}
   665→	commitMsg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s\n", trailers.SessionTrailerKey, opts.SessionID))
   666→	commitMsg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s\n", trailers.StrategyTrailerKey, opts.Strategy))
   667→	if opts.Agent != "" {
   668→		commitMsg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s\n", trailers.AgentTrailerKey, opts.Agent))
   669→	}
   670→	if opts.EphemeralBranch != "" {
   671→		commitMsg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s\n", trailers.EphemeralBranchTrailerKey, opts.EphemeralBranch))
   672→	}
   673→	if taskMetadataPath != "" {
   674→		commitMsg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s\n", trailers.MetadataTaskTrailerKey, taskMetadataPath))
   675→	}
   676→
   677→	return commitMsg.String()
   678→}
   679→
   680→// incrementalCheckpointData represents an incremental checkpoint during subagent execution.
   681→// This mirrors strategy.SubagentCheckpoint but avoids import cycles.
   682→type incrementalCheckpointData struct {
   683→	Type      string          `json:"type"`
   684→	ToolUseID string          `json:"tool_use_id"`
   685→	Timestamp time.Time       `json:"timestamp"`
   686→	Data      json.RawMessage `json:"data"`
   687→}
   688→
   689→// taskCheckpointData represents a final task checkpoint.
   690→// This mirrors strategy.TaskCheckpoint but avoids import cycles.
   691→type taskCheckpointData struct {
   692→	SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
   693→	ToolUseID      string `json:"tool_use_id"`
   694→	CheckpointUUID string `json:"checkpoint_uuid"`
   695→	AgentID        string `json:"agent_id,omitempty"`
   696→}
   697→
   698→// ReadCommitted reads a committed checkpoint's summary by ID from the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   699→// Returns only the CheckpointSummary (paths + aggregated stats), not actual content.
   700→// Use ReadSessionContent to read actual transcript/prompts/context.
   701→// Returns nil, nil if the checkpoint doesn't exist.
   702→//
   703→// The storage format uses numbered subdirectories for each session (0-based):
   704→//
   705→//	<checkpoint-id>/
   706→//	├── metadata.json      # CheckpointSummary with sessions map
   707→//	├── 0/                 # First session
   708→//	│   ├── metadata.json  # Session-specific metadata
   709→//	│   └── full.jsonl     # Transcript
   710→//	├── 1/                 # Second session
   711→//	└── ...
   712→func (s *GitStore) ReadCommitted(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (*CheckpointSummary, error) {
   713→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   714→
   715→	tree, err := s.getSessionsBranchTree()
   716→	if err != nil {
   717→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil,nilerr // No sessions branch means no checkpoint exists
   718→	}
   719→
   720→	checkpointPath := checkpointID.Path()
   721→	checkpointTree, err := tree.Tree(checkpointPath)
   722→	if err != nil {
   723→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil,nilerr // Checkpoint directory not found
   724→	}
   725→
   726→	// Read root metadata.json as CheckpointSummary
   727→	metadataFile, err := checkpointTree.File(paths.MetadataFileName)
   728→	if err != nil {
   729→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil,nilerr // metadata.json not found
   730→	}
   731→
   732→	content, err := metadataFile.Contents()
   733→	if err != nil {
   734→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read metadata.json: %w", err)
   735→	}
   736→
   737→	var summary CheckpointSummary
   738→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &summary); err != nil {
   739→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse metadata.json: %w", err)
   740→	}
   741→
   742→	return &summary, nil
   743→}
   744→
   745→// ReadSessionContent reads the actual content for a specific session within a checkpoint.
   746→// sessionIndex is 0-based (0 for first session, 1 for second, etc.).
   747→// Returns the session's metadata, transcript, prompts, and context.
   748→// Returns an error if the checkpoint or session doesn't exist.
   749→func (s *GitStore) ReadSessionContent(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, sessionIndex int) (*SessionContent, error) {
   750→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   751→
   752→	tree, err := s.getSessionsBranchTree()
   753→	if err != nil {
   754→		return nil, ErrCheckpointNotFound
   755→	}
   756→
   757→	checkpointPath := checkpointID.Path()
   758→	checkpointTree, err := tree.Tree(checkpointPath)
   759→	if err != nil {
   760→		return nil, ErrCheckpointNotFound
   761→	}
   762→
   763→	// Get the session subdirectory
   764→	sessionDir := strconv.Itoa(sessionIndex)
   765→	sessionTree, err := checkpointTree.Tree(sessionDir)
   766→	if err != nil {
   767→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("session %d not found: %w", sessionIndex, err)
   768→	}
   769→
   770→	result := &SessionContent{}
   771→
   772→	// Read session-specific metadata
   773→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   774→	if metadataFile, fileErr := sessionTree.File(paths.MetadataFileName); fileErr == nil {
   775→		if content, contentErr := metadataFile.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   776→			if jsonErr := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &result.Metadata); jsonErr == nil {
   777→				agentType = result.Metadata.Agent
   778→			}
   779→		}
   780→	}
   781→
   782→	// Read transcript
   783→	if transcript, transcriptErr := readTranscriptFromTree(sessionTree, agentType); transcriptErr == nil && transcript != nil {
   784→		result.Transcript = transcript
   785→	}
   786→
   787→	// Read prompts
   788→	if file, fileErr := sessionTree.File(paths.PromptFileName); fileErr == nil {
   789→		if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   790→			result.Prompts = content
   791→		}
   792→	}
   793→
   794→	// Read context
   795→	if file, fileErr := sessionTree.File(paths.ContextFileName); fileErr == nil {
   796→		if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   797→			result.Context = content
   798→		}
   799→	}
   800→
   801→	return result, nil
   802→}
   803→
   804→// ReadLatestSessionContent is a convenience method that reads the latest session's content.
   805→// This is equivalent to ReadSessionContent(ctx, checkpointID, len(summary.Sessions)-1).
   806→func (s *GitStore) ReadLatestSessionContent(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (*SessionContent, error) {
   807→	summary, err := s.ReadCommitted(ctx, checkpointID)
   808→	if err != nil {
   809→		return nil, err
   810→	}
   811→	if summary == nil {
   812→		return nil, ErrCheckpointNotFound
   813→	}
   814→	if len(summary.Sessions) == 0 {
   815→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("checkpoint has no sessions: %s", checkpointID)
   816→	}
   817→
   818→	latestIndex := len(summary.Sessions) - 1
   819→	return s.ReadSessionContent(ctx, checkpointID, latestIndex)
   820→}
   821→
   822→// ReadSessionContentByID reads a session's content by its session ID.
   823→// This is useful when you have the session ID but don't know its index within the checkpoint.
   824→// Returns ErrCheckpointNotFound if the checkpoint doesn't exist.
   825→// Returns an error if no session with the given ID exists in the checkpoint.
   826→func (s *GitStore) ReadSessionContentByID(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, sessionID string) (*SessionContent, error) {
   827→	summary, err := s.ReadCommitted(ctx, checkpointID)
   828→	if err != nil {
   829→		return nil, err
   830→	}
   831→	if summary == nil {
   832→		return nil, ErrCheckpointNotFound
   833→	}
   834→
   835→	// Iterate through sessions to find the one with matching session ID
   836→	for i := range len(summary.Sessions) {
   837→		content, readErr := s.ReadSessionContent(ctx, checkpointID, i)
   838→		if readErr != nil {
   839→			continue
   840→		}
   841→		if content != nil && content.Metadata.SessionID == sessionID {
   842→			return content, nil
   843→		}
   844→	}
   845→
   846→	return nil, fmt.Errorf("session %q not found in checkpoint %s", sessionID, checkpointID)
   847→}
   848→
   849→// ListCommitted lists all committed checkpoints from the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   850→// Scans sharded paths: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/ directories containing metadata.json.
   851→//
   852→
   853→func (s *GitStore) ListCommitted(ctx context.Context) ([]CommittedInfo, error) {
   854→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   855→
   856→	tree, err := s.getSessionsBranchTree()
   857→	if err != nil {
   858→		return []CommittedInfo{}, nil //nolint:nilerr // No sessions branch means empty list
   859→	}
   860→
   861→	var checkpoints []CommittedInfo
   862→
   863→	// Scan sharded structure: <2-char-prefix>/<remaining-id>/metadata.json
   864→	for _, bucketEntry := range tree.Entries {
   865→		if bucketEntry.Mode != filemode.Dir {
   866→			continue
   867→		}
   868→		// Bucket should be 2 hex chars
   869→		if len(bucketEntry.Name) != 2 {
   870→			continue
   871→		}
   872→
   873→		bucketTree, treeErr := s.repo.TreeObject(bucketEntry.Hash)
   874→		if treeErr != nil {
   875→			continue
   876→		}
   877→
   878→		// Each entry in the bucket is the remaining part of the checkpoint ID
   879→		for _, checkpointEntry := range bucketTree.Entries {
   880→			if checkpointEntry.Mode != filemode.Dir {
   881→				continue
   882→			}
   883→
   884→			checkpointTree, cpTreeErr := s.repo.TreeObject(checkpointEntry.Hash)
   885→			if cpTreeErr != nil {
   886→				continue
   887→			}
   888→
   889→			// Reconstruct checkpoint ID: <bucket><remaining>
   890→			checkpointIDStr := bucketEntry.Name + checkpointEntry.Name
   891→			checkpointID, cpIDErr := id.NewCheckpointID(checkpointIDStr)
   892→			if cpIDErr != nil {
   893→				// Skip invalid checkpoint IDs (shouldn't happen with our own data)
   894→				continue
   895→			}
   896→
   897→			info := CommittedInfo{
   898→				CheckpointID: checkpointID,
   899→			}
   900→
   901→			// Get details from root metadata file (CheckpointSummary format)
   902→			if metadataFile, fileErr := checkpointTree.File(paths.MetadataFileName); fileErr == nil {
   903→				if content, contentErr := metadataFile.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   904→					var summary CheckpointSummary
   905→					if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &summary); err == nil {
   906→						info.CheckpointsCount = summary.CheckpointsCount
   907→						info.FilesTouched = summary.FilesTouched
   908→						info.SessionCount = len(summary.Sessions)
   909→
   910→						// Read session metadata from latest session to get Agent, SessionID, CreatedAt
   911→						if len(summary.Sessions) > 0 {
   912→							latestIndex := len(summary.Sessions) - 1
   913→							latestDir := strconv.Itoa(latestIndex)
   914→							if sessionTree, treeErr := checkpointTree.Tree(latestDir); treeErr == nil {
   915→								if sessionMetadataFile, smErr := sessionTree.File(paths.MetadataFileName); smErr == nil {
   916→									if sessionContent, scErr := sessionMetadataFile.Contents(); scErr == nil {
   917→										var sessionMetadata CommittedMetadata
   918→										if json.Unmarshal([]byte(sessionContent), &sessionMetadata) == nil {
   919→											info.Agent = sessionMetadata.Agent
   920→											info.SessionID = sessionMetadata.SessionID
   921→											info.CreatedAt = sessionMetadata.CreatedAt
   922→										}
   923→									}
   924→								}
   925→							}
   926→						}
   927→					}
   928→				}
   929→			}
   930→
   931→			checkpoints = append(checkpoints, info)
   932→		}
   933→	}
   934→
   935→	// Sort by time (most recent first)
   936→	sort.Slice(checkpoints, func(i, j int) bool {
   937→		return checkpoints[i].CreatedAt.After(checkpoints[j].CreatedAt)
   938→	})
   939→
   940→	return checkpoints, nil
   941→}
   942→
   943→// GetTranscript retrieves the transcript for a specific checkpoint ID.
   944→// Returns the latest session's transcript.
   945→func (s *GitStore) GetTranscript(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) ([]byte, error) {
   946→	content, err := s.ReadLatestSessionContent(ctx, checkpointID)
   947→	if err != nil {
   948→		return nil, err
   949→	}
   950→	if len(content.Transcript) == 0 {
   951→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no transcript found for checkpoint: %s", checkpointID)
   952→	}
   953→	return content.Transcript, nil
   954→}
   955→
   956→// GetSessionLog retrieves the session transcript and session ID for a checkpoint.
   957→// This is the primary method for looking up session logs by checkpoint ID.
   958→// Returns ErrCheckpointNotFound if the checkpoint doesn't exist.
   959→// Returns ErrNoTranscript if the checkpoint exists but has no transcript.
   960→func (s *GitStore) GetSessionLog(cpID id.CheckpointID) ([]byte, string, error) {
   961→	content, err := s.ReadLatestSessionContent(context.Background(), cpID)
   962→	if err != nil {
   963→		if errors.Is(err, ErrCheckpointNotFound) {
   964→			return nil, "", ErrCheckpointNotFound
   965→		}
   966→		return nil, "", fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint: %w", err)
   967→	}
   968→	if len(content.Transcript) == 0 {
   969→		return nil, "", ErrNoTranscript
   970→	}
   971→	return content.Transcript, content.Metadata.SessionID, nil
   972→}
   973→
   974→// LookupSessionLog is a convenience function that opens the repository and retrieves
   975→// a session log by checkpoint ID. This is the primary entry point for callers that
   976→// don't already have a GitStore instance.
   977→// Returns ErrCheckpointNotFound if the checkpoint doesn't exist.
   978→// Returns ErrNoTranscript if the checkpoint exists but has no transcript.
   979→func LookupSessionLog(cpID id.CheckpointID) ([]byte, string, error) {
   980→	repo, err := git.PlainOpenWithOptions(".", &git.PlainOpenOptions{DetectDotGit: true})
   981→	if err != nil {
   982→		return nil, "", fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   983→	}
   984→	store := NewGitStore(repo)
   985→	return store.GetSessionLog(cpID)
   986→}
   987→
   988→// UpdateSummary updates the summary field in the latest session's metadata.
   989→// Returns ErrCheckpointNotFound if the checkpoint doesn't exist.
   990→func (s *GitStore) UpdateSummary(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, summary *Summary) error {
   991→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   992→
   993→	// Ensure sessions branch exists
   994→	if err := s.ensureSessionsBranch(); err != nil {
   995→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to ensure sessions branch: %w", err)
   996→	}
   997→
   998→	// Get current branch tip and flatten tree
   999→	ref, entries, err := s.getSessionsBranchEntries()
  1000→	if err != nil {
  1001→		return err
  1002→	}
  1003→
  1004→	// Read root CheckpointSummary to find the latest session
  1005→	basePath := checkpointID.Path() + "/"
  1006→	rootMetadataPath := basePath + paths.MetadataFileName
  1007→	entry, exists := entries[rootMetadataPath]
  1008→	if !exists {
  1009→		return ErrCheckpointNotFound
  1010→	}
  1011→
  1012→	checkpointSummary, err := s.readSummaryFromBlob(entry.Hash)
  1013→	if err != nil {
  1014→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint summary: %w", err)
  1015→	}
  1016→
  1017→	// Find the latest session's metadata path (0-based indexing)
  1018→	latestIndex := len(checkpointSummary.Sessions) - 1
  1019→	sessionMetadataPath := fmt.Sprintf("%s%d/%s", basePath, latestIndex, paths.MetadataFileName)
  1020→	sessionEntry, exists := entries[sessionMetadataPath]
  1021→	if !exists {
  1022→		return fmt.Errorf("session metadata not found at %s", sessionMetadataPath)
  1023→	}
  1024→
  1025→	// Read and update session metadata
  1026→	existingMetadata, err := s.readMetadataFromBlob(sessionEntry.Hash)
  1027→	if err != nil {
  1028→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read session metadata: %w", err)
  1029→	}
  1030→
  1031→	// Update the summary
  1032→	existingMetadata.Summary = redactSummary(summary)
  1033→
  1034→	// Write updated session metadata
  1035→	metadataJSON, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(existingMetadata, "", "  ")
  1036→	if err != nil {
  1037→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal metadata: %w", err)
  1038→	}
  1039→	metadataHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, metadataJSON)
  1040→	if err != nil {
  1041→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create metadata blob: %w", err)
  1042→	}
  1043→	entries[sessionMetadataPath] = object.TreeEntry{
  1044→		Name: sessionMetadataPath,
  1045→		Mode: filemode.Regular,
  1046→		Hash: metadataHash,
  1047→	}
  1048→
  1049→	// Build and commit
  1050→	newTreeHash, err := BuildTreeFromEntries(s.repo, entries)
  1051→	if err != nil {
  1052→		return err
  1053→	}
  1054→
  1055→	authorName, authorEmail := GetGitAuthorFromRepo(s.repo)
  1056→	commitMsg := fmt.Sprintf("Update summary for checkpoint %s (session: %s)", checkpointID, existingMetadata.SessionID)
  1057→	newCommitHash, err := s.createCommit(newTreeHash, ref.Hash(), commitMsg, authorName, authorEmail)
  1058→	if err != nil {
  1059→		return err
  1060→	}
  1061→
  1062→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
  1063→	newRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, newCommitHash)
  1064→	if err := s.repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {
  1065→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to set branch reference: %w", err)
  1066→	}
  1067→
  1068→	return nil
  1069→}
  1070→
  1071→// UpdateCommitted replaces the transcript, prompts, and context for an existing
  1072→// committed checkpoint. Uses replace semantics: the full session transcript is
  1073→// written, replacing whatever was stored at initial condensation time.
  1074→//
  1075→// This is called at stop time to finalize all checkpoints from the current turn
  1076→// with the complete session transcript (from prompt to stop event).
  1077→//
  1078→// Returns ErrCheckpointNotFound if the checkpoint doesn't exist.
  1079→func (s *GitStore) UpdateCommitted(ctx context.Context, opts UpdateCommittedOptions) error {
  1080→	if opts.CheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
  1081→		return errors.New("invalid update options: checkpoint ID is required")
  1082→	}
  1083→
  1084→	// Ensure sessions branch exists
  1085→	if err := s.ensureSessionsBranch(); err != nil {
  1086→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to ensure sessions branch: %w", err)
  1087→	}
  1088→
  1089→	// Get current branch tip and flatten tree
  1090→	ref, entries, err := s.getSessionsBranchEntries()
  1091→	if err != nil {
  1092→		return err
  1093→	}
  1094→
  1095→	// Read root CheckpointSummary to find the session slot
  1096→	basePath := opts.CheckpointID.Path() + "/"
  1097→	rootMetadataPath := basePath + paths.MetadataFileName
  1098→	entry, exists := entries[rootMetadataPath]
  1099→	if !exists {
  1100→		return ErrCheckpointNotFound
  1101→	}
  1102→
  1103→	checkpointSummary, err := s.readSummaryFromBlob(entry.Hash)
  1104→	if err != nil {
  1105→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint summary: %w", err)
  1106→	}
  1107→	if len(checkpointSummary.Sessions) == 0 {
  1108→		return ErrCheckpointNotFound
  1109→	}
  1110→
  1111→	// Find session index matching opts.SessionID
  1112→	sessionIndex := -1
  1113→	for i := range len(checkpointSummary.Sessions) {
  1114→		metaPath := fmt.Sprintf("%s%d/%s", basePath, i, paths.MetadataFileName)
  1115→		if metaEntry, metaExists := entries[metaPath]; metaExists {
  1116→			meta, metaErr := s.readMetadataFromBlob(metaEntry.Hash)
  1117→			if metaErr == nil && meta.SessionID == opts.SessionID {
  1118→				sessionIndex = i
  1119→				break
  1120→			}
  1121→		}
  1122→	}
  1123→	if sessionIndex == -1 {
  1124→		// Fall back to latest session; log so mismatches are diagnosable.
  1125→		sessionIndex = len(checkpointSummary.Sessions) - 1
  1126→		logging.Debug(ctx, "UpdateCommitted: session ID not found, falling back to latest",
  1127→			slog.String("session_id", opts.SessionID),
  1128→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", string(opts.CheckpointID)),
  1129→			slog.Int("fallback_index", sessionIndex),
  1130→		)
  1131→	}
  1132→
  1133→	sessionPath := fmt.Sprintf("%s%d/", basePath, sessionIndex)
  1134→
  1135→	// Replace transcript (full replace, not append)
  1136→	// Apply redaction as safety net (caller should redact, but we ensure it here)
  1137→	if len(opts.Transcript) > 0 {
  1138→		transcript, err := redact.JSONLBytes(opts.Transcript)
  1139→		if err != nil {
  1140→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to redact transcript secrets: %w", err)
  1141→		}
  1142→		if err := s.replaceTranscript(transcript, opts.Agent, sessionPath, entries); err != nil {
  1143→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to replace transcript: %w", err)
  1144→		}
  1145→	}
  1146→
  1147→	// Replace prompts (apply redaction as safety net)
  1148→	if len(opts.Prompts) > 0 {
  1149→		promptContent := redact.String(strings.Join(opts.Prompts, "\n\n---\n\n"))
  1150→		blobHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, []byte(promptContent))
  1151→		if err != nil {
  1152→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to create prompt blob: %w", err)
  1153→		}
  1154→		entries[sessionPath+paths.PromptFileName] = object.TreeEntry{
  1155→			Name: sessionPath + paths.PromptFileName,
  1156→			Mode: filemode.Regular,
  1157→			Hash: blobHash,
  1158→		}
  1159→	}
  1160→
  1161→	// Replace context (apply redaction as safety net)
  1162→	if len(opts.Context) > 0 {
  1163→		contextBlob, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, redact.Bytes(opts.Context))
  1164→		if err != nil {
  1165→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to create context blob: %w", err)
  1166→		}
  1167→		entries[sessionPath+paths.ContextFileName] = object.TreeEntry{
  1168→			Name: sessionPath + paths.ContextFileName,
  1169→			Mode: filemode.Regular,
  1170→			Hash: contextBlob,
  1171→		}
  1172→	}
  1173→
  1174→	// Build and commit
  1175→	newTreeHash, err := BuildTreeFromEntries(s.repo, entries)
  1176→	if err != nil {
  1177→		return err
  1178→	}
  1179→
  1180→	authorName, authorEmail := GetGitAuthorFromRepo(s.repo)
  1181→	commitMsg := fmt.Sprintf("Finalize transcript for Checkpoint: %s", opts.CheckpointID)
  1182→	newCommitHash, err := s.createCommit(newTreeHash, ref.Hash(), commitMsg, authorName, authorEmail)
  1183→	if err != nil {
  1184→		return err
  1185→	}
  1186→
  1187→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
  1188→	newRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, newCommitHash)
  1189→	if err := s.repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {
  1190→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to set branch reference: %w", err)
  1191→	}
  1192→
  1193→	return nil
  1194→}
  1195→
  1196→// replaceTranscript writes the full transcript content, replacing any existing transcript.
  1197→// Also removes any chunk files from a previous write and updates the content hash.
  1198→func (s *GitStore) replaceTranscript(transcript []byte, agentType agent.AgentType, sessionPath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) error {
  1199→	// Remove existing transcript files (base + any chunks)
  1200→	transcriptBase := sessionPath + paths.TranscriptFileName
  1201→	for key := range entries {
  1202→		if key == transcriptBase || strings.HasPrefix(key, transcriptBase+".") {
  1203→			delete(entries, key)
  1204→		}
  1205→	}
  1206→
  1207→	// Chunk the transcript (matches writeTranscript behavior)
  1208→	chunks, err := agent.ChunkTranscript(transcript, agentType)
  1209→	if err != nil {
  1210→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to chunk transcript: %w", err)
  1211→	}
  1212→
  1213→	// Write chunk files
  1214→	for i, chunk := range chunks {
  1215→		chunkPath := sessionPath + agent.ChunkFileName(paths.TranscriptFileName, i)
  1216→		blobHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, chunk)
  1217→		if err != nil {
  1218→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to create transcript blob: %w", err)
  1219→		}
  1220→		entries[chunkPath] = object.TreeEntry{
  1221→			Name: chunkPath,
  1222→			Mode: filemode.Regular,
  1223→			Hash: blobHash,
  1224→		}
  1225→	}
  1226→
  1227→	// Update content hash
  1228→	contentHash := fmt.Sprintf("sha256:%x", sha256.Sum256(transcript))
  1229→	hashBlob, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, []byte(contentHash))
  1230→	if err != nil {
  1231→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create content hash blob: %w", err)
  1232→	}
  1233→	hashPath := sessionPath + paths.ContentHashFileName
  1234→	entries[hashPath] = object.TreeEntry{
  1235→		Name: hashPath,
  1236→		Mode: filemode.Regular,
  1237→		Hash: hashBlob,
  1238→	}
  1239→
  1240→	return nil
  1241→}
  1242→
  1243→// ensureSessionsBranch ensures the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch exists.
  1244→func (s *GitStore) ensureSessionsBranch() error {
  1245→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
  1246→	_, err := s.repo.Reference(refName, true)
  1247→	if err == nil {
  1248→		return nil // Branch exists
  1249→	}
  1250→
  1251→	// Create orphan branch with empty tree
  1252→	emptyTreeHash, err := BuildTreeFromEntries(s.repo, make(map[string]object.TreeEntry))
  1253→	if err != nil {
  1254→		return err
  1255→	}
  1256→
  1257→	authorName, authorEmail := GetGitAuthorFromRepo(s.repo)
  1258→	commitHash, err := s.createCommit(emptyTreeHash, plumbing.ZeroHash, "Initialize sessions branch", authorName, authorEmail)
  1259→	if err != nil {
  1260→		return err
  1261→	}
  1262→
  1263→	newRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, commitHash)
  1264→	if err := s.repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {
  1265→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to set branch reference: %w", err)
  1266→	}
  1267→	return nil
  1268→}
  1269→
  1270→// getSessionsBranchTree returns the tree object for the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
  1271→// Falls back to origin/entire/checkpoints/v1 if the local branch doesn't exist.
  1272→func (s *GitStore) getSessionsBranchTree() (*object.Tree, error) {
  1273→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
  1274→	ref, err := s.repo.Reference(refName, true)
  1275→	if err != nil {
  1276→		// Local branch doesn't exist, try remote-tracking branch
  1277→		remoteRefName := plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName("origin", paths.MetadataBranchName)
  1278→		ref, err = s.repo.Reference(remoteRefName, true)
  1279→		if err != nil {
  1280→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("sessions branch not found: %w", err)
  1281→		}
  1282→	}
  1283→
  1284→	commit, err := s.repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
  1285→	if err != nil {
  1286→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit object: %w", err)
  1287→	}
  1288→
  1289→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
  1290→	if err != nil {
  1291→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit tree: %w", err)
  1292→	}
  1293→
  1294→	return tree, nil
  1295→}
  1296→
  1297→// CreateBlobFromContent creates a blob object from in-memory content.
  1298→// Exported for use by strategy package (session_test.go)
  1299→func CreateBlobFromContent(repo *git.Repository, content []byte) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
  1300→	obj := repo.Storer.NewEncodedObject()
  1301→	obj.SetType(plumbing.BlobObject)
  1302→	obj.SetSize(int64(len(content)))
  1303→
  1304→	writer, err := obj.Writer()
  1305→	if err != nil {
  1306→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to get object writer: %w", err)
  1307→	}
  1308→
  1309→	_, err = writer.Write(content)
  1310→	if err != nil {
  1311→		_ = writer.Close()
  1312→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to write blob content: %w", err)
  1313→	}
  1314→	if err := writer.Close(); err != nil {
  1315→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to close blob writer: %w", err)
  1316→	}
  1317→
  1318→	hash, err := repo.Storer.SetEncodedObject(obj)
  1319→	if err != nil {
  1320→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to store blob object: %w", err)
  1321→	}
  1322→	return hash, nil
  1323→}
  1324→
  1325→// copyMetadataDir copies all files from a directory to the checkpoint path.
  1326→// Used to include additional metadata files like task checkpoints, subagent transcripts, etc.
  1327→func (s *GitStore) copyMetadataDir(metadataDir, basePath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) error {
  1328→	err := filepath.Walk(metadataDir, func(path string, info os.FileInfo, err error) error {
  1329→		if err != nil {
  1330→			return err
  1331→		}
  1332→
  1333→		// Skip symlinks to prevent reading files outside the metadata directory.
  1334→		// A symlink could point to sensitive files (e.g., /etc/passwd) which would
  1335→		// then be captured in the checkpoint and stored in git history.
  1336→		// NOTE: filepath.Walk uses os.Stat (follows symlinks), so info.Mode() never
  1337→		// reports ModeSymlink. We use os.Lstat to check the entry itself.
  1338→		// This check MUST come before IsDir() because Walk follows symlinked
  1339→		// directories and would recurse into them otherwise.
  1340→		linfo, lstatErr := os.Lstat(path)
  1341→		if lstatErr != nil {
  1342→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to lstat %s: %w", path, lstatErr)
  1343→		}
  1344→		if linfo.Mode()&os.ModeSymlink != 0 {
  1345→			if info.IsDir() {
  1346→				return filepath.SkipDir
  1347→			}
  1348→			return nil
  1349→		}
  1350→
  1351→		if info.IsDir() {
  1352→			return nil
  1353→		}
  1354→
  1355→		// Get relative path within metadata dir
  1356→		relPath, err := filepath.Rel(metadataDir, path)
  1357→		if err != nil {
  1358→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to get relative path for %s: %w", path, err)
  1359→		}
  1360→
  1361→		// Prevent path traversal via symlinks pointing outside the metadata dir
  1362→		if strings.HasPrefix(relPath, "..") {
  1363→			return fmt.Errorf("path traversal detected: %s", relPath)
  1364→		}
  1365→
  1366→		// Create blob from file with secrets redaction
  1367→		blobHash, mode, err := createRedactedBlobFromFile(s.repo, path, relPath)
  1368→		if err != nil {
  1369→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to create blob for %s: %w", path, err)
  1370→		}
  1371→
  1372→		// Store at checkpoint path (use forward slashes for git tree compatibility on Windows)
  1373→		fullPath := basePath + filepath.ToSlash(relPath)
  1374→		entries[fullPath] = object.TreeEntry{
  1375→			Name: fullPath,
  1376→			Mode: mode,
  1377→			Hash: blobHash,
  1378→		}
  1379→
  1380→		return nil
  1381→	})
  1382→	if err != nil {
  1383→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to walk metadata directory: %w", err)
  1384→	}
  1385→	return nil
  1386→}
  1387→
  1388→// createRedactedBlobFromFile reads a file, applies secrets redaction, and creates a git blob.
  1389→// JSONL files get JSONL-aware redaction; all other files get plain string redaction.
  1390→func createRedactedBlobFromFile(repo *git.Repository, filePath, treePath string) (plumbing.Hash, filemode.FileMode, error) {
  1391→	info, err := os.Stat(filePath)
  1392→	if err != nil {
  1393→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to stat file: %w", err)
  1394→	}
  1395→
  1396→	mode := filemode.Regular
  1397→	if info.Mode()&0o111 != 0 {
  1398→		mode = filemode.Executable
  1399→	}
  1400→
  1401→	content, err := os.ReadFile(filePath) //nolint:gosec // filePath comes from walking the metadata directory
  1402→	if err != nil {
  1403→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read file: %w", err)
  1404→	}
  1405→
  1406→	// Skip redaction for binary files — they can't contain text secrets and
  1407→	// running string replacement on them would corrupt the data.
  1408→	isBin, binErr := binary.IsBinary(bytes.NewReader(content))
  1409→	if binErr != nil || isBin {
  1410→		hash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(repo, content)
  1411→		if err != nil {
  1412→			return plumbing.ZeroHash, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create blob: %w", err)
  1413→		}
  1414→		return hash, mode, nil
  1415→	}
  1416→
  1417→	if strings.HasSuffix(treePath, ".jsonl") {
  1418→		redacted, jsonlErr := redact.JSONLBytes(content)
  1419→		if jsonlErr != nil {
  1420→			redacted = redact.Bytes(content)
  1421→		}
  1422→		content = redacted
  1423→	} else {
  1424→		content = redact.Bytes(content)
  1425→	}
  1426→
  1427→	hash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(repo, content)
  1428→	if err != nil {
  1429→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create blob: %w", err)
  1430→	}
  1431→	return hash, mode, nil
  1432→}
  1433→
  1434→// GetGitAuthorFromRepo retrieves the git user.name and user.email,
  1435→// checking both the repository-local config and the global ~/.gitconfig.
  1436→func GetGitAuthorFromRepo(repo *git.Repository) (name, email string) {
  1437→	// Get repository config (includes local settings)
  1438→	cfg, err := repo.Config()
  1439→	if err == nil {
  1440→		name = cfg.User.Name
  1441→		email = cfg.User.Email
  1442→	}
  1443→
  1444→	// If not found in local config, try global config
  1445→	if name == "" || email == "" {
  1446→		globalCfg, err := config.LoadConfig(config.GlobalScope)
  1447→		if err == nil {
  1448→			if name == "" {
  1449→				name = globalCfg.User.Name
  1450→			}
  1451→			if email == "" {
  1452→				email = globalCfg.User.Email
  1453→			}
  1454→		}
  1455→	}
  1456→
  1457→	// Provide sensible defaults if git user is not configured
  1458→	if name == "" {
  1459→		name = "Unknown"
  1460→	}
  1461→	if email == "" {
  1462→		email = "unknown@local"
  1463→	}
  1464→
  1465→	return name, email
  1466→}
  1467→
  1468→// readTranscriptFromTree reads a transcript from a git tree, handling both chunked and non-chunked formats.
  1469→// It checks for chunk files first (.001, .002, etc.), then falls back to the base file.
  1470→// The agentType is used for reassembling chunks in the correct format.
  1471→func readTranscriptFromTree(tree *object.Tree, agentType agent.AgentType) ([]byte, error) {
  1472→	// Collect all transcript-related files
  1473→	var chunkFiles []string
  1474→	var hasBaseFile bool
  1475→
  1476→	for _, entry := range tree.Entries {
  1477→		if entry.Name == paths.TranscriptFileName || entry.Name == paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy {
  1478→			hasBaseFile = true
  1479→		}
  1480→		// Check for chunk files (full.jsonl.001, full.jsonl.002, etc.)
  1481→		if strings.HasPrefix(entry.Name, paths.TranscriptFileName+".") {
  1482→			idx := agent.ParseChunkIndex(entry.Name, paths.TranscriptFileName)
  1483→			if idx > 0 {
  1484→				chunkFiles = append(chunkFiles, entry.Name)
  1485→			}
  1486→		}
  1487→	}
  1488→
  1489→	// If we have chunk files, read and reassemble them
  1490→	if len(chunkFiles) > 0 {
  1491→		// Sort chunk files by index
  1492→		chunkFiles = agent.SortChunkFiles(chunkFiles, paths.TranscriptFileName)
  1493→
  1494→		// Check if base file should be included as chunk 0.
  1495→		// NOTE: This assumes the chunking convention where the unsuffixed file
  1496→		// (full.jsonl) is chunk 0, and numbered files (.001, .002) are chunks 1+.
  1497→		if hasBaseFile {
  1498→			chunkFiles = append([]string{paths.TranscriptFileName}, chunkFiles...)
  1499→		}
  1500→
  1501→		var chunks [][]byte
  1502→		for _, chunkFile := range chunkFiles {
  1503→			file, err := tree.File(chunkFile)
  1504→			if err != nil {
  1505→				logging.Warn(context.Background(), "failed to read transcript chunk file from tree",
  1506→					slog.String("chunk_file", chunkFile),
  1507→					slog.String("error", err.Error()),
  1508→				)
  1509→				continue
  1510→			}
  1511→			content, err := file.Contents()
  1512→			if err != nil {
  1513→				logging.Warn(context.Background(), "failed to read transcript chunk contents",
  1514→					slog.String("chunk_file", chunkFile),
  1515→					slog.String("error", err.Error()),
  1516→				)
  1517→				continue
  1518→			}
  1519→			chunks = append(chunks, []byte(content))
  1520→		}
  1521→
  1522→		if len(chunks) > 0 {
  1523→			result, err := agent.ReassembleTranscript(chunks, agentType)
  1524→			if err != nil {
  1525→				return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to reassemble transcript: %w", err)
  1526→			}
  1527→			return result, nil
  1528→		}
  1529→	}
  1530→
  1531→	// Fall back to reading base file (non-chunked or backwards compatibility)
  1532→	if file, err := tree.File(paths.TranscriptFileName); err == nil {
  1533→		if content, err := file.Contents(); err == nil {
  1534→			return []byte(content), nil
  1535→		}
  1536→	}
  1537→
  1538→	// Try legacy filename
  1539→	if file, err := tree.File(paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy); err == nil {
  1540→		if content, err := file.Contents(); err == nil {
  1541→			return []byte(content), nil
  1542→		}
  1543→	}
  1544→
  1545→	return nil, nil
  1546→}
  1547→
  1548→// Author contains author information for a checkpoint.
  1549→type Author struct {
  1550→	Name  string
  1551→	Email string
  1552→}
  1553→
  1554→// GetCheckpointAuthor retrieves the author of a checkpoint from the entire/checkpoints/v1 commit history.
  1555→// Returns the author of the commit that introduced this checkpoint's metadata.json file.
  1556→// Returns empty Author if the checkpoint is not found or the sessions branch doesn't exist.
  1557→func (s *GitStore) GetCheckpointAuthor(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (Author, error) {
  1558→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
  1559→
  1560→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
  1561→	ref, err := s.repo.Reference(refName, true)
  1562→	if err != nil {
  1563→		return Author{}, nil
  1564→	}
  1565→
  1566→	// Path to the checkpoint's metadata file
  1567→	metadataPath := checkpointID.Path() + "/" + paths.MetadataFileName
  1568→
  1569→	// Walk commit history looking for the commit that introduced this file
  1570→	iter, err := s.repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{
  1571→		From:  ref.Hash(),
  1572→		Order: git.LogOrderCommitterTime,
  1573→	})
  1574→	if err != nil {
  1575→		return Author{}, nil
  1576→	}
  1577→	defer iter.Close()
  1578→
  1579→	var author Author
  1580→	var foundCommit *object.Commit
  1581→
  1582→	err = iter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error {
  1583→		tree, treeErr := c.Tree()
  1584→		if treeErr != nil {
  1585→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Skip commits we can't read, continue searching
  1586→		}
  1587→
  1588→		_, fileErr := tree.File(metadataPath)
  1589→		if fileErr != nil {
  1590→			// File doesn't exist in this commit - we've gone past the creation point
  1591→			if foundCommit != nil {
  1592→				return errStopIteration
  1593→			}
  1594→			return nil
  1595→		}
  1596→
  1597→		// File exists - track it (oldest one with file is the creator)
  1598→		foundCommit = c
  1599→		author = Author{
  1600→			Name:  c.Author.Name,
  1601→			Email: c.Author.Email,
  1602→		}
  1603→		return nil
  1604→	})
  1605→
  1606→	// Ignore errStopIteration - it's just for early exit
  1607→	if err != nil && !errors.Is(err, errStopIteration) {
  1608→		return Author{}, nil
  1609→	}
  1610→
  1611→	return author, nil
  1612→}
  1613→

     1→package paths
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"fmt"
     5→	"os"
     6→	"path/filepath"
     7→	"strings"
     8→)
     9→
    10→// GetWorktreeID returns the internal git worktree identifier for the given path.
    11→// For the main worktree (where .git is a directory), returns empty string.
    12→// For linked worktrees (where .git is a file), extracts the name from
    13→// .git/worktrees/<name>/ path. This name is stable across `git worktree move`.
    14→func GetWorktreeID(worktreePath string) (string, error) {
    15→	gitPath := filepath.Join(worktreePath, ".git")
    16→
    17→	info, err := os.Stat(gitPath)
    18→	if err != nil {
    19→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to stat .git: %w", err)
    20→	}
    21→
    22→	// Main worktree has .git as a directory
    23→	if info.IsDir() {
    24→		return "", nil
    25→	}
    26→
    27→	// Linked worktree has .git as a file with content: "gitdir: /path/to/.git/worktrees/<name>"
    28→	content, err := os.ReadFile(gitPath) //nolint:gosec // gitPath is constructed from worktreePath + ".git"
    29→	if err != nil {
    30→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to read .git file: %w", err)
    31→	}
    32→
    33→	line := strings.TrimSpace(string(content))
    34→	if !strings.HasPrefix(line, "gitdir: ") {
    35→		return "", fmt.Errorf("invalid .git file format: %s", line)
    36→	}
    37→
    38→	gitdir := strings.TrimPrefix(line, "gitdir: ")
    39→
    40→	// Extract worktree name from path like /repo/.git/worktrees/<name>
    41→	// The path after ".git/worktrees/" is the worktree identifier
    42→	const marker = ".git/worktrees/"
    43→	_, worktreeID, found := strings.Cut(gitdir, marker)
    44→	if !found {
    45→		return "", fmt.Errorf("unexpected gitdir format (no worktrees): %s", gitdir)
    46→	}
    47→	// Remove trailing slashes if any
    48→	worktreeID = strings.TrimSuffix(worktreeID, "/")
    49→
    50→	return worktreeID, nil
    51→}
    52→

     1→// Package validation provides input validation functions for the Entire CLI.
     2→// This package has no dependencies to avoid import cycles.
     3→package validation
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"errors"
     7→	"fmt"
     8→	"regexp"
     9→	"strings"
    10→)
    11→
    12→// pathSafeRegex matches alphanumeric characters, underscores, and hyphens only.
    13→// Used to validate IDs that will be used in file paths.
    14→var pathSafeRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$`)
    15→
    16→// ValidateSessionID validates that a session ID doesn't contain path separators.
    17→// This prevents path traversal attacks when session IDs are used in file paths.
    18→func ValidateSessionID(id string) error {
    19→	if id == "" {
    20→		return errors.New("session ID cannot be empty")
    21→	}
    22→	if strings.ContainsAny(id, "/\\") {
    23→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID %q: contains path separators", id)
    24→	}
    25→	return nil
    26→}
    27→
    28→// ValidateToolUseID validates that a tool use ID contains only safe characters for paths.
    29→// Tool use IDs can be UUIDs or prefixed identifiers like "toolu_xxx".
    30→func ValidateToolUseID(id string) error {
    31→	if id == "" {
    32→		return nil // Empty is allowed (optional field)
    33→	}
    34→	if !pathSafeRegex.MatchString(id) {
    35→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid tool use ID %q: must be alphanumeric with underscores/hyphens only", id)
    36→	}
    37→	return nil
    38→}
    39→
    40→// ValidateAgentID validates that an agent ID contains only safe characters for paths.
    41→func ValidateAgentID(id string) error {
    42→	if id == "" {
    43→		return nil // Empty is allowed (optional field)
    44→	}
    45→	if !pathSafeRegex.MatchString(id) {
    46→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid agent ID %q: must be alphanumeric with underscores/hyphens only", id)
    47→	}
    48→	return nil
    49→}
    50→
    51→// ValidateAgentSessionID validates that an agent session ID contains only safe characters for paths.
    52→// Agent session IDs can be UUIDs (Claude Code), test identifiers, or other formats depending on the agent.
    53→// This prevents path traversal attacks when the ID is used in file path construction.
    54→func ValidateAgentSessionID(id string) error {
    55→	if id == "" {
    56→		return errors.New("agent session ID cannot be empty")
    57→	}
    58→	if !pathSafeRegex.MatchString(id) {
    59→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid agent session ID %q: must be alphanumeric with underscores/hyphens only", id)
    60→	}
    61→	return nil
    62→}
    63→

    25→                mode. This uses simpler text prompts instead of interactive

    28→
    29→func NewRootCmd() *cobra.Command {
    30→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
    31→		Use:   "entire",
    32→		Short: "Entire CLI",
    33→		Long:  "The command-line interface for Entire" + gettingStarted + accessibilityHelp,
    34→		// Let main.go handle error printing to avoid duplication
    35→		SilenceErrors: true,
    36→		SilenceUsage:  true,
    37→		// Hide completion command from help but keep it functional
    38→		CompletionOptions: cobra.CompletionOptions{
    39→			HiddenDefaultCmd: true,
    40→		},
    41→		PersistentPostRun: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) {
    42→			// Skip for hidden commands (walk parent chain — Cobra doesn't propagate Hidden)
    43→			for c := cmd; c != nil; c = c.Parent() {
    44→				if c.Hidden {
    45→					return
    46→				}
    47→			}
    48→
    49→			// Load settings once for telemetry and version check
    50→			var telemetryEnabled *bool
    51→			settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
    52→			if err == nil {
    53→				telemetryEnabled = settings.Telemetry
    54→			}
    55→
    56→			// Check if telemetry is enabled
    57→			if telemetryEnabled != nil && *telemetryEnabled {
    58→				// Use detached tracking (non-blocking)
    59→				installedAgents := GetAgentsWithHooksInstalled()
    60→				agentStr := JoinAgentNames(installedAgents)
    61→				telemetry.TrackCommandDetached(cmd, settings.Strategy, agentStr, settings.Enabled, buildinfo.Version)
    62→			}
    63→
    64→			// Version check and notification (synchronous with 2s timeout)
    65→			// Runs AFTER command completes to avoid interfering with interactive modes
    66→			versioncheck.CheckAndNotify(cmd.OutOrStdout(), buildinfo.Version)
    67→		},
    68→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
    69→			return cmd.Help()
    70→		},
    71→	}
    72→

     1→package cli
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"bytes"
     5→	"context"
     6→	"encoding/json"
     7→	"errors"
     8→	"fmt"
     9→	"io"
    10→	"os/exec"
    11→	"strings"
    12→	"time"

    14→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
