    16→)
    17→
    18→// ProofConfig defines a single proof (validation check) to run.
    19→type ProofConfig struct {
    20→	// Type is the proof identifier (e.g., "test", "lint", "typecheck")
    21→	Type string `json:"type"`
    22→	// Command is the shell command to execute
    23→	Command string `json:"command"`
    24→	// Required indicates if this proof must pass for validation to succeed
    25→	Required bool `json:"required"`
    26→}
    27→
    28→// ValidationConfig holds the validation configuration from settings.
    29→type ValidationConfig struct {
    30→	// Enabled controls whether validation runs automatically
    31→	Enabled bool `json:"enabled"`
    32→	// Proofs is the list of proof checks to run
    33→	Proofs []ProofConfig `json:"proofs"`
    34→}
    35→
    36→// ProofResult holds the result of running a single proof.
    37→type ProofResult struct {
    38→	Type     string        `json:"type"`
    39→	Command  string        `json:"command"`
    40→	Passed   bool          `json:"passed"`
    41→	Required bool          `json:"required"`
    42→	Output   string        `json:"output,omitempty"`
    43→	Error    string        `json:"error,omitempty"`
    44→	Duration time.Duration `json:"duration"`
    45→}
    46→
    47→// ValidationResult holds the complete validation results.
    48→type ValidationResult struct {
    49→	Passed    bool          `json:"passed"`
    50→	Proofs    []ProofResult `json:"proofs"`
    51→	Duration  time.Duration `json:"duration"`
    52→	Timestamp time.Time     `json:"timestamp"`
    53→}
    54→
    55→// DefaultValidationConfig returns the default validation configuration.
    56→func DefaultValidationConfig() *ValidationConfig {
    57→	return &ValidationConfig{
    58→		Enabled: false,
    59→		Proofs: []ProofConfig{
    60→			{Type: "test", Command: "mise run test", Required: true},
    61→			{Type: "lint", Command: "mise run lint", Required: true},
    62→		},
    63→	}
    64→}
    65→
    66→func newValidateCmd() *cobra.Command {
    67→	var (
    68→		jsonOutput bool
    69→		proofTypes []string
    70→	)
    71→
    72→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
    73→		Use:    "validate",
    74→		Short:  "Run validation proofs on the codebase",
    75→		Hidden: true, // Hidden experimental command
    76→		Long: `Run configured validation proofs to verify code quality.
    77→
    78→This command runs the validation proofs configured in .entire/settings.json
    79→under the "validation" key. Each proof is a command that must exit with
    80→status 0 to pass.
    81→
    82→Example configuration in .entire/settings.json:
    83→  {
    84→    "validation": {
    85→      "enabled": true,
    86→      "proofs": [
    87→        {"type": "test", "command": "mise run test", "required": true},
    88→        {"type": "lint", "command": "mise run lint", "required": true},
    89→        {"type": "typecheck", "command": "go build ./...", "required": true}
    90→      ]
    91→    }
    92→  }
    93→
    94→This is an experimental feature for autonomous validation workflows.`,
    95→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
    96→			return runValidate(cmd.OutOrStdout(), cmd.ErrOrStderr(), jsonOutput, proofTypes)
    97→		},
    98→	}
    99→
   100→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&jsonOutput, "json", false, "Output results as JSON")
   101→	cmd.Flags().StringSliceVar(&proofTypes, "proof", nil, "Run only specific proof types (can be repeated)")
   102→
   103→	return cmd
   104→}
   105→
   106→func runValidate(stdout, stderr io.Writer, jsonOutput bool, filterProofs []string) error {
   107→	// Check if we're in a git repository
   108→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   109→	if err != nil {
   110→		return fmt.Errorf("not in a git repository: %w", err)
   111→	}
   112→
   113→	// Load validation config
   114→	config, err := loadValidationConfig()
   115→	if err != nil {
   116→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load validation config: %w", err)
   117→	}
   118→
   119→	if len(config.Proofs) == 0 {
   120→		if jsonOutput {
   121→			result := &ValidationResult{
   122→				Passed:    true,
   123→				Proofs:    []ProofResult{},
   124→				Timestamp: time.Now(),
   125→			}
   126→			return outputJSON(stdout, result)
   127→		}
   128→		fmt.Fprintln(stdout, "No validation proofs configured.")
   129→		fmt.Fprintln(stdout, "\nAdd proofs to .entire/settings.json:")
   130→		fmt.Fprintln(stdout, `  "validation": {`)
   131→		fmt.Fprintln(stdout, `    "enabled": true,`)
   132→		fmt.Fprintln(stdout, `    "proofs": [`)
   133→		fmt.Fprintln(stdout, `      {"type": "test", "command": "mise run test", "required": true}`)
   134→		fmt.Fprintln(stdout, `    ]`)
   135→		fmt.Fprintln(stdout, `  }`)
   136→		return nil
   137→	}
   138→
   139→	// Filter proofs if specific types requested
   140→	proofsToRun := config.Proofs
   141→	if len(filterProofs) > 0 {
   142→		proofsToRun = filterProofsByType(config.Proofs, filterProofs)
   143→		if len(proofsToRun) == 0 {
   144→			return fmt.Errorf("no matching proofs found for types: %v", filterProofs)
   145→		}
   146→	}
   147→
   148→	// Run validation
   149→	result := runProofs(stdout, stderr, repoRoot, proofsToRun, jsonOutput)
   150→
   151→	if jsonOutput {
   152→		return outputJSON(stdout, result)
   153→	}
   154→
   155→	// Print summary
   156→	printValidationSummary(stdout, result)
   157→
   158→	if !result.Passed {
   159→		return NewSilentError(errors.New("validation failed"))
   160→	}
   161→	return nil
   162→}
   163→
   164→func loadValidationConfig() (*ValidationConfig, error) {
   165→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   166→	if err != nil {
   167→		return DefaultValidationConfig(), nil //nolint:nilerr // use defaults on error
   168→	}
   169→
   170→	// Check if validation is configured in strategy_options
   171→	if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   172→		return DefaultValidationConfig(), nil
   173→	}
   174→
   175→	validationRaw, ok := settings.StrategyOptions["validation"]
   176→	if !ok {
   177→		return DefaultValidationConfig(), nil
   178→	}
   179→
   180→	// Convert to JSON and back to parse into ValidationConfig
   181→	data, err := json.Marshal(validationRaw)
   182→	if err != nil {
   183→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("marshaling validation config: %w", err)
   184→	}
   185→
   186→	var config ValidationConfig
   187→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &config); err != nil {
   188→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("parsing validation config: %w", err)
   189→	}
   190→
   191→	return &config, nil
   192→}
   193→
   194→func filterProofsByType(proofs []ProofConfig, types []string) []ProofConfig {
   195→	typeSet := make(map[string]bool)
   196→	for _, t := range types {
   197→		typeSet[strings.ToLower(t)] = true
   198→	}
   199→
   200→	var filtered []ProofConfig
   201→	for _, p := range proofs {
   202→		if typeSet[strings.ToLower(p.Type)] {
   203→			filtered = append(filtered, p)
   204→		}
   205→	}
   206→	return filtered
   207→}
   208→
   209→func runProofs(stdout, stderr io.Writer, repoRoot string, proofs []ProofConfig, quiet bool) *ValidationResult {
   210→	start := time.Now()
   211→	result := &ValidationResult{
   212→		Passed:    true,
   213→		Proofs:    make([]ProofResult, 0, len(proofs)),
   214→		Timestamp: start,
   215→	}
   216→
   217→	for i, proof := range proofs {
   218→		if !quiet {
   219→			fmt.Fprintf(stdout, "\n[%d/%d] Running %s: %s\n", i+1, len(proofs), proof.Type, proof.Command)
   220→		}
   221→
   222→		proofResult := runSingleProof(repoRoot, proof)
   223→		result.Proofs = append(result.Proofs, proofResult)
   224→
   225→		if !quiet {
   226→			if proofResult.Passed {
   227→				fmt.Fprintf(stdout, "  ✓ PASSED (%s)\n", proofResult.Duration.Round(time.Millisecond))
   228→			} else {
   229→				fmt.Fprintf(stdout, "  ✗ FAILED (%s)\n", proofResult.Duration.Round(time.Millisecond))
   230→				if proofResult.Output != "" {
   231→					// Print last 20 lines of output
   232→					lines := strings.Split(strings.TrimSpace(proofResult.Output), "\n")
   233→					if len(lines) > 20 {
   234→						fmt.Fprintf(stderr, "  ... (%d lines truncated)\n", len(lines)-20)
   235→						lines = lines[len(lines)-20:]
   236→					}
   237→					for _, line := range lines {
   238→						fmt.Fprintf(stderr, "  │ %s\n", line)
   239→					}
   240→				}
   241→				if proofResult.Error != "" {
   242→					fmt.Fprintf(stderr, "  Error: %s\n", proofResult.Error)
   243→				}
   244→			}
   245→		}
   246→
   247→		// Update overall pass status
   248→		if !proofResult.Passed && proof.Required {
   249→			result.Passed = false
   250→		}
   251→	}
   252→
   253→	result.Duration = time.Since(start)
   254→	return result
   255→}
   256→
   257→func runSingleProof(repoRoot string, proof ProofConfig) ProofResult {
   258→	start := time.Now()
   259→	result := ProofResult{
   260→		Type:     proof.Type,
   261→		Command:  proof.Command,
   262→		Required: proof.Required,
   263→	}
   264→
   265→	// Create command with shell
   266→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Minute)
   267→	defer cancel()
   268→
   269→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "sh", "-c", proof.Command) //nolint:gosec // command is user-configured in settings.json
   270→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   271→
   272→	// Capture output
   273→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   274→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   275→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   276→
   277→	err := cmd.Run()
   278→	result.Duration = time.Since(start)
   279→
   280→	// Combine stdout and stderr for output
   281→	output := stdout.String()
   282→	if stderr.Len() > 0 {
   283→		if output != "" {
   284→			output += "\n"
   285→		}
   286→		output += stderr.String()
   287→	}
   288→	result.Output = strings.TrimSpace(output)
   289→
   290→	if err != nil {
   291→		result.Passed = false
   292→		if ctx.Err() == context.DeadlineExceeded {
   293→			result.Error = "command timed out after 10 minutes"
   294→		} else {
   295→			result.Error = err.Error()
   296→		}
   297→	} else {
   298→		result.Passed = true
   299→	}
   300→
   301→	return result
   302→}
   303→
   304→func printValidationSummary(w io.Writer, result *ValidationResult) {
   305→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\n"+strings.Repeat("─", 50))
   306→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Validation Summary")
   307→	fmt.Fprintln(w, strings.Repeat("─", 50))
   308→
   309→	passed := 0
   310→	failed := 0
   311→	for _, p := range result.Proofs {
   312→		status := "✓"
   313→		if !p.Passed {
   314→			status = "✗"
   315→			failed++
   316→		} else {
   317→			passed++
   318→		}
   319→		reqLabel := ""
   320→		if !p.Required {
   321→			reqLabel = " (optional)"
   322→		}
   323→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  %s %s%s\n", status, p.Type, reqLabel)
   324→	}
   325→
   326→	fmt.Fprintln(w, strings.Repeat("─", 50))
   327→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "Total: %d passed, %d failed (%.1fs)\n", passed, failed, result.Duration.Seconds())
   328→
   329→	if result.Passed {
   330→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "\n✓ All required proofs passed")
   331→	} else {
   332→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "\n✗ Validation failed - required proofs did not pass")
   333→	}
   334→}
   335→
   336→func outputJSON(w io.Writer, result *ValidationResult) error {
   337→	enc := json.NewEncoder(w)
   338→	enc.SetIndent("", "  ")
   339→	if err := enc.Encode(result); err != nil {
   340→		return fmt.Errorf("encoding validation result: %w", err)
   341→	}
   342→	return nil
   343→}

    51→		Long: `Interactive command for rewinding and managing agent sessions.
    52→
    53→This command will show you an interactive list of recent checkpoints.  You'll be
    54→able to select one for Entire to rewind your branch state, including your code and
    55→your agent's context.`,

    74→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&logsOnlyFlag, "logs-only", false, "Only restore logs, don't modify working directory (for logs-only points)")
    75→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&resetFlag, "reset", false, "Reset branch to commit (destructive, for logs-only points)")
    76→

    80→func runRewindInteractive() error { //nolint:maintidx // already present in codebase
    81→	// Get the configured strategy
    82→	start := GetStrategy()
    83→
    84→	// Check for uncommitted changes first
    85→	canRewind, changeMsg, err := start.CanRewind()
    86→	if err != nil {
    87→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to check for uncommitted changes: %w", err)
    88→	}
    89→	if !canRewind {
    90→		fmt.Println(changeMsg)
    91→		return nil
    92→	}
    93→
    94→	// Get rewind points from strategy
    95→	points, err := start.GetRewindPoints(20)
    96→	if err != nil {
    97→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to find rewind points: %w", err)
    98→	}
    99→
   100→	if len(points) == 0 {
   101→		fmt.Println("No rewind points found.")
   102→		fmt.Println("Rewind points are created automatically when agent sessions end.")
   103→		return nil
   104→	}
   105→
   106→	// Check if there are multiple sessions (to show session identifier)
   107→	sessionIDs := make(map[string]bool)
   108→	for _, p := range points {
   109→		if p.SessionID != "" {
   110→			sessionIDs[p.SessionID] = true
   111→		}
   112→	}
   113→	hasMultipleSessions := len(sessionIDs) > 1
   114→
   115→	// Build options for the select menu
   116→	options := make([]huh.Option[string], 0, len(points)+1)
   117→	for _, p := range points {
   118→		var label string
   119→		timestamp := p.Date.Format("2006-01-02 15:04")
   120→
   121→		// Build session identifier for display when multiple sessions exist
   122→		sessionLabel := ""
   123→		if hasMultipleSessions && p.SessionPrompt != "" {
   124→			// Show truncated prompt to identify the session
   125→			sessionLabel = fmt.Sprintf(" [%s]", sanitizeForTerminal(p.SessionPrompt))
   126→		}
   127→
   128→		switch {
   129→		case p.IsLogsOnly:
   130→			// Committed checkpoint - show commit sha (this is the real user commit)
   131→			shortID := p.ID
   132→			if len(shortID) >= 7 {
   133→				shortID = shortID[:7]
   134→			}
   135→			label = fmt.Sprintf("%s (%s) %s%s", shortID, timestamp, sanitizeForTerminal(p.Message), sessionLabel)
   136→		case p.IsTaskCheckpoint:
   137→			// Task checkpoint (uncommitted) - no sha shown
   138→			label = fmt.Sprintf("        (%s) [Task] %s%s", timestamp, sanitizeForTerminal(p.Message), sessionLabel)
   139→		default:
   140→			// Shadow checkpoint (uncommitted) - no sha shown (internal commit)
   141→			label = fmt.Sprintf("        (%s) %s%s", timestamp, sanitizeForTerminal(p.Message), sessionLabel)
   142→		}
   143→		options = append(options, huh.NewOption(label, p.ID))
   144→	}
   145→	options = append(options, huh.NewOption("Cancel", "cancel"))
   146→
   147→	var selectedID string
   148→	form := NewAccessibleForm(
   149→		huh.NewGroup(
   150→			huh.NewSelect[string]().
   151→				Title("Select a checkpoint to restore").
   152→				Description("Your working directory will be restored to this checkpoint's state").
   153→				Options(options...).
   154→				Value(&selectedID),
   155→		),
   156→	)
   157→
   158→	if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   159→		return fmt.Errorf("selection cancelled: %w", err)
   160→	}
   161→
   162→	if selectedID == "cancel" {
   163→		fmt.Println("Rewind cancelled.")
   164→		return nil
   165→	}
   166→
   167→	// Find the selected point
   168→	var selectedPoint *strategy.RewindPoint
   169→	for _, p := range points {
   170→		if p.ID == selectedID {
   171→			pointCopy := p
   172→			selectedPoint = &pointCopy
   173→			break
   174→		}
   175→	}
   176→
   177→	if selectedPoint == nil {
   178→		return errors.New("rewind point not found")
   179→	}
   180→
   181→	shortID := selectedPoint.ID
   182→	if len(shortID) > 7 {
   183→		shortID = shortID[:7]
   184→	}
   185→
   186→	// Show what was selected
   187→	switch {
   188→	case selectedPoint.IsLogsOnly:
   189→		// Committed checkpoint - show sha
   190→		fmt.Printf("\nSelected: %s %s\n", shortID, sanitizeForTerminal(selectedPoint.Message))
   191→	case selectedPoint.IsTaskCheckpoint:
   192→		// Task checkpoint - no sha
   193→		fmt.Printf("\nSelected: [Task] %s\n", sanitizeForTerminal(selectedPoint.Message))
   194→	default:
   195→		// Shadow checkpoint - no sha
   196→		fmt.Printf("\nSelected: %s\n", sanitizeForTerminal(selectedPoint.Message))
   197→	}
   198→
   199→	// Handle logs-only points with a sub-choice menu
   200→	if selectedPoint.IsLogsOnly {
   201→		return handleLogsOnlyRewindInteractive(start, *selectedPoint, shortID)
   202→	}
   203→
   204→	// Preview rewind to show warnings about files that will be deleted
   205→	preview, previewErr := start.PreviewRewind(*selectedPoint)
   206→	if previewErr == nil && preview != nil && len(preview.FilesToDelete) > 0 {
   207→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\nWarning: The following untracked files will be DELETED:\n")
   208→		for _, f := range preview.FilesToDelete {
   209→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", f)
   210→		}
   211→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\n")
   212→	}
   213→
   214→	// Confirm rewind
   215→	var confirm bool
   216→	description := fmt.Sprintf("This will reset to: %s\nChanges after this point may be lost!", selectedPoint.Message)
   217→	confirmForm := NewAccessibleForm(
   218→		huh.NewGroup(
   219→			huh.NewConfirm().
   220→				Title(fmt.Sprintf("Reset to %s?", shortID)).
   221→				Description(description).
   222→				Value(&confirm),
   223→		),
   224→	)
   225→
   226→	if err := confirmForm.Run(); err != nil {
   227→		return fmt.Errorf("confirmation cancelled: %w", err)
   228→	}
   229→
   230→	if !confirm {
   231→		fmt.Println("Rewind cancelled.")
   232→		return nil
   233→	}
   234→
   235→	// Resolve agent once for use throughout
   236→	agent, err := getAgent(selectedPoint.Agent)
   237→	if err != nil {
   238→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   239→	}
   240→
   241→	// Initialize logging context with agent from checkpoint
   242→	ctx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "rewind")
   243→	ctx = logging.WithAgent(ctx, agent.Name())
   244→
   245→	logging.Debug(ctx, "rewind started",
   246→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", selectedPoint.ID),
   247→		slog.String("session_id", selectedPoint.SessionID),
   248→		slog.Bool("is_task_checkpoint", selectedPoint.IsTaskCheckpoint),
   249→	)
   250→
   251→	// Perform the rewind using strategy
   252→	if err := start.Rewind(*selectedPoint); err != nil {
   253→		logging.Error(ctx, "rewind failed",
   254→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", selectedPoint.ID),
   255→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   256→		)
   257→		return err //nolint:wrapcheck // already present in codebase
   258→	}
   259→
   260→	logging.Debug(ctx, "rewind completed",
   261→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", selectedPoint.ID),
   262→	)
   263→
   264→	// Handle transcript restoration differently for task checkpoints
   265→	var sessionID string
   266→	var transcriptFile string
   267→
   268→	if selectedPoint.IsTaskCheckpoint {
   269→		// For task checkpoint: read checkpoint.json to get UUID and truncate transcript
   270→		checkpoint, err := start.GetTaskCheckpoint(*selectedPoint)
   271→		if err != nil {
   272→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to read task checkpoint: %v\n", err)
   273→			return nil
   274→		}
   275→
   276→		sessionID = checkpoint.SessionID
   277→
   278→		if checkpoint.CheckpointUUID != "" {
   279→			// Truncate transcript at checkpoint UUID
   280→			if err := restoreTaskCheckpointTranscript(start, *selectedPoint, sessionID, checkpoint.CheckpointUUID, agent); err != nil {
   281→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to restore truncated session transcript: %v\n", err)
   282→			} else {
   283→				fmt.Printf("Rewound to task checkpoint. %s\n", agent.FormatResumeCommand(sessionID))
   284→			}
   285→			return nil
   286→		}
   287→	} else {
   288→		// For session checkpoint: restore full transcript
   289→		// Prefer SessionID from trailer (set by GetRewindPoints from Entire-Session trailer)
   290→		// over path-based extraction which is less reliable.
   291→		sessionID = selectedPoint.SessionID
   292→		if sessionID == "" {
   293→			sessionID = filepath.Base(selectedPoint.MetadataDir)
   294→		}
   295→		transcriptFile = filepath.Join(selectedPoint.MetadataDir, paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy)
   296→	}
   297→
   298→	// Try to restore transcript using the appropriate method:
   299→	// 1. Checkpoint storage (committed checkpoints with valid checkpoint ID)
   300→	// 2. Shadow branch (uncommitted checkpoints with commit hash)
   301→	// 3. Local file (active sessions)
   302→	var restored bool
   303→	if !selectedPoint.CheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
   304→		// Try checkpoint storage first for committed checkpoints
   305→		if returnedSessionID, err := restoreSessionTranscriptFromStrategy(selectedPoint.CheckpointID, sessionID, agent); err == nil {
   306→			sessionID = returnedSessionID
   307→			restored = true
   308→		}
   309→	}
   310→
   311→	if !restored && selectedPoint.MetadataDir != "" && len(selectedPoint.ID) == 40 {
   312→		// Try shadow branch for uncommitted checkpoints (ID is a 40-char commit hash)
   313→		if returnedSessionID, err := restoreSessionTranscriptFromShadow(selectedPoint.ID, selectedPoint.MetadataDir, sessionID, agent); err == nil {
   314→			sessionID = returnedSessionID
   315→			restored = true
   316→		}
   317→	}
   318→
   319→	if !restored {
   320→		// Fall back to local file
   321→		if err := restoreSessionTranscript(transcriptFile, sessionID, agent); err != nil {
   322→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to restore session transcript: %v\n", err)
   323→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Source: %s\n", transcriptFile)
   324→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Session ID: %s\n", sessionID)
   325→		}
   326→	}
   327→
   328→	fmt.Printf("Rewound to %s. %s\n", shortID, agent.FormatResumeCommand(sessionID))
   329→	return nil
   330→}
   331→
   332→func runRewindList() error {
   333→	start := GetStrategy()
   334→
   335→	points, err := start.GetRewindPoints(20)
   336→	if err != nil {
   337→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to find rewind points: %w", err)
   338→	}
   339→
   340→	// Output as JSON for programmatic use
   341→	type jsonPoint struct {
   342→		ID               string `json:"id"`
   343→		Message          string `json:"message"`
   344→		MetadataDir      string `json:"metadata_dir"`
   345→		Date             string `json:"date"`
   346→		IsTaskCheckpoint bool   `json:"is_task_checkpoint"`
   347→		ToolUseID        string `json:"tool_use_id,omitempty"`
   348→		IsLogsOnly       bool   `json:"is_logs_only"`
   349→		CondensationID   string `json:"condensation_id,omitempty"`
   350→		SessionID        string `json:"session_id,omitempty"`
   351→		SessionPrompt    string `json:"session_prompt,omitempty"`
   352→	}
   353→
   354→	output := make([]jsonPoint, len(points))
   355→	for i, p := range points {
   356→		output[i] = jsonPoint{
   357→			ID:               p.ID,
   358→			Message:          p.Message,
   359→			MetadataDir:      p.MetadataDir,
   360→			Date:             p.Date.Format(time.RFC3339),
   361→			IsTaskCheckpoint: p.IsTaskCheckpoint,
   362→			ToolUseID:        p.ToolUseID,
   363→			IsLogsOnly:       p.IsLogsOnly,
   364→			CondensationID:   p.CheckpointID.String(),
   365→			SessionID:        p.SessionID,
   366→			SessionPrompt:    p.SessionPrompt,
   367→		}
   368→	}
   369→
   370→	// Print as JSON
   371→	data, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(output, "", "  ")
   372→	if err != nil {
   373→		return err //nolint:wrapcheck // already present in codebase
   374→	}
   375→	fmt.Println(string(data))
   376→	return nil
   377→}
   378→
   379→func runRewindToWithOptions(commitID string, logsOnly bool, reset bool) error {
   380→	return runRewindToInternal(commitID, logsOnly, reset)
   381→}
   382→
   383→func runRewindToInternal(commitID string, logsOnly bool, reset bool) error {
   384→	start := GetStrategy()
   385→
   386→	// Check for uncommitted changes (skip for reset which handles this itself)
   387→	if !reset {
   388→		canRewind, changeMsg, err := start.CanRewind()
   389→		if err != nil {
   390→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to check for uncommitted changes: %w", err)
   391→		}
   392→		if !canRewind {
   393→			return fmt.Errorf("%s", changeMsg)
   394→		}
   395→	}
   396→
   397→	// Get rewind points
   398→	points, err := start.GetRewindPoints(20)
   399→	if err != nil {
   400→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to find rewind points: %w", err)
   401→	}
   402→
   403→	// Find the matching point (support both full and short commit IDs)
   404→	var selectedPoint *strategy.RewindPoint
   405→	for _, p := range points {
   406→		if p.ID == commitID || (len(commitID) >= 7 && len(p.ID) >= 7 && strings.HasPrefix(p.ID, commitID)) {
   407→			pointCopy := p
   408→			selectedPoint = &pointCopy
   409→			break
   410→		}
   411→	}
   412→
   413→	if selectedPoint == nil {
   414→		return fmt.Errorf("rewind point not found: %s", commitID)
   415→	}
   416→
   417→	// Handle reset mode (for logs-only points)
   418→	if reset {
   419→		return handleLogsOnlyResetNonInteractive(start, *selectedPoint)
   420→	}
   421→
   422→	// Handle logs-only restoration:
   423→	// 1. For logs-only points, always use logs-only restoration
   424→	// 2. If --logs-only flag is set, use logs-only restoration even for checkpoint points
   425→	if selectedPoint.IsLogsOnly || logsOnly {
   426→		return handleLogsOnlyRewindNonInteractive(start, *selectedPoint)
   427→	}
   428→
   429→	// Preview rewind to show warnings about files that will be deleted
   430→	preview, previewErr := start.PreviewRewind(*selectedPoint)
   431→	if previewErr == nil && preview != nil && len(preview.FilesToDelete) > 0 {
   432→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\nWarning: The following untracked files will be DELETED:\n")
   433→		for _, f := range preview.FilesToDelete {
   434→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", f)
   435→		}
   436→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\n")
   437→	}
   438→
   439→	// Resolve agent once for use throughout
   440→	agent, err := getAgent(selectedPoint.Agent)
   441→	if err != nil {
   442→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   443→	}
   444→
   445→	// Initialize logging context with agent from checkpoint
   446→	ctx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "rewind")
   447→	ctx = logging.WithAgent(ctx, agent.Name())
   448→
   449→	logging.Debug(ctx, "rewind started",
   450→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", selectedPoint.ID),
   451→		slog.String("session_id", selectedPoint.SessionID),
   452→		slog.Bool("is_task_checkpoint", selectedPoint.IsTaskCheckpoint),
   453→	)
   454→
   455→	// Perform the rewind
   456→	if err := start.Rewind(*selectedPoint); err != nil {
   457→		logging.Error(ctx, "rewind failed",
   458→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", selectedPoint.ID),
   459→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   460→		)
   461→		return err //nolint:wrapcheck // already present in codebase
   462→	}
   463→
   464→	logging.Debug(ctx, "rewind completed",
   465→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", selectedPoint.ID),
   466→	)
   467→
   468→	// Handle transcript restoration
   469→	var sessionID string
   470→	var transcriptFile string
   471→
   472→	if selectedPoint.IsTaskCheckpoint {
   473→		checkpoint, err := start.GetTaskCheckpoint(*selectedPoint)
   474→		if err != nil {
   475→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to read task checkpoint: %v\n", err)
   476→			return nil
   477→		}
   478→
   479→		sessionID = checkpoint.SessionID
   480→
   481→		if checkpoint.CheckpointUUID != "" {
   482→			// Use strategy-based transcript restoration for task checkpoints
   483→			if err := restoreTaskCheckpointTranscript(start, *selectedPoint, sessionID, checkpoint.CheckpointUUID, agent); err != nil {
   484→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to restore truncated session transcript: %v\n", err)
   485→			} else {
   486→				fmt.Printf("Rewound to task checkpoint. %s\n", agent.FormatResumeCommand(sessionID))
   487→			}
   488→			return nil
   489→		}
   490→	} else {
   491→		// Prefer SessionID from trailer over path-based extraction
   492→		sessionID = selectedPoint.SessionID
   493→		if sessionID == "" {
   494→			sessionID = filepath.Base(selectedPoint.MetadataDir)
   495→		}
   496→		transcriptFile = filepath.Join(selectedPoint.MetadataDir, paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy)
   497→	}
   498→
   499→	// Try to restore transcript using the appropriate method:
   500→	// 1. Checkpoint storage (committed checkpoints with valid checkpoint ID)
   501→	// 2. Shadow branch (uncommitted checkpoints with commit hash)
   502→	// 3. Local file (active sessions)
   503→	var restored bool
   504→	if !selectedPoint.CheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
   505→		// Try checkpoint storage first for committed checkpoints
   506→		if returnedSessionID, err := restoreSessionTranscriptFromStrategy(selectedPoint.CheckpointID, sessionID, agent); err == nil {
   507→			sessionID = returnedSessionID
   508→			restored = true
   509→		}
   510→	}
   511→
   512→	if !restored && selectedPoint.MetadataDir != "" && len(selectedPoint.ID) == 40 {
   513→		// Try shadow branch for uncommitted checkpoints (ID is a 40-char commit hash)
   514→		if returnedSessionID, err := restoreSessionTranscriptFromShadow(selectedPoint.ID, selectedPoint.MetadataDir, sessionID, agent); err == nil {
   515→			sessionID = returnedSessionID
   516→			restored = true
   517→		}
   518→	}
   519→
   520→	if !restored {
   521→		// Fall back to local file
   522→		if err := restoreSessionTranscript(transcriptFile, sessionID, agent); err != nil {
   523→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to restore session transcript: %v\n", err)
   524→		}
   525→	}
   526→
   527→	fmt.Printf("Rewound to %s. %s\n", selectedPoint.ID[:7], agent.FormatResumeCommand(sessionID))
   528→	return nil
   529→}
   530→
   531→// handleLogsOnlyRewindNonInteractive handles logs-only rewind in non-interactive mode.
   532→// Defaults to restoring logs only (no checkout) for safety.
   533→func handleLogsOnlyRewindNonInteractive(start strategy.Strategy, point strategy.RewindPoint) error {
   534→	// Resolve agent once for use throughout
   535→	agent, err := getAgent(point.Agent)
   536→	if err != nil {
   537→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   538→	}
   539→
   540→	// Initialize logging context with agent from checkpoint
   541→	ctx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "rewind")
   542→	ctx = logging.WithAgent(ctx, agent.Name())
   543→
   544→	logging.Debug(ctx, "logs-only rewind started",
   545→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   546→		slog.String("session_id", point.SessionID),
   547→	)
   548→
   549→	restorer, ok := start.(strategy.LogsOnlyRestorer)
   550→	if !ok {
   551→		return errors.New("strategy does not support logs-only restoration")
   552→	}
   553→
   554→	sessions, err := restorer.RestoreLogsOnly(point, true) // force=true for explicit rewind
   555→	if err != nil {
   556→		logging.Error(ctx, "logs-only rewind failed",
   557→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   558→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   559→		)
   560→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to restore logs: %w", err)
   561→	}
   562→
   563→	logging.Debug(ctx, "logs-only rewind completed",
   564→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   565→	)
   566→
   567→	// Show resume commands for all sessions
   568→	printMultiSessionResumeCommands(sessions)
   569→
   570→	fmt.Println("Note: Working directory unchanged. Use interactive mode for full checkout.")
   571→	return nil
   572→}
   573→
   574→// handleLogsOnlyResetNonInteractive handles reset in non-interactive mode.
   575→// This performs a git reset --hard to the target commit.
   576→func handleLogsOnlyResetNonInteractive(start strategy.Strategy, point strategy.RewindPoint) error {
   577→	// Resolve agent once for use throughout
   578→	agent, err := getAgent(point.Agent)
   579→	if err != nil {
   580→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   581→	}
   582→
   583→	// Initialize logging context with agent from checkpoint
   584→	ctx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "rewind")
   585→	ctx = logging.WithAgent(ctx, agent.Name())
   586→
   587→	logging.Debug(ctx, "logs-only reset started",
   588→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   589→		slog.String("session_id", point.SessionID),
   590→	)
   591→
   592→	restorer, ok := start.(strategy.LogsOnlyRestorer)
   593→	if !ok {
   594→		return errors.New("strategy does not support logs-only restoration")
   595→	}
   596→
   597→	// Get current HEAD before reset (for recovery message)
   598→	currentHead, headErr := getCurrentHeadHash()
   599→	if headErr != nil {
   600→		currentHead = ""
   601→	}
   602→
   603→	// Restore logs first
   604→	sessions, err := restorer.RestoreLogsOnly(point, true) // force=true for explicit rewind
   605→	if err != nil {
   606→		logging.Error(ctx, "logs-only reset failed during log restoration",
   607→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   608→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   609→		)
   610→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to restore logs: %w", err)
   611→	}
   612→
   613→	// Perform git reset --hard
   614→	if err := performGitResetHard(point.ID); err != nil {
   615→		logging.Error(ctx, "logs-only reset failed during git reset",
   616→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   617→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   618→		)
   619→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to reset branch: %w", err)
   620→	}
   621→
   622→	logging.Debug(ctx, "logs-only reset completed",
   623→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   624→	)
   625→
   626→	shortID := point.ID
   627→	if len(shortID) > 7 {
   628→		shortID = shortID[:7]
   629→	}
   630→
   631→	fmt.Printf("Reset branch to %s.\n", shortID)
   632→
   633→	// Show resume commands for all sessions
   634→	printMultiSessionResumeCommands(sessions)
   635→
   636→	// Show recovery instructions
   637→	if currentHead != "" && currentHead != point.ID {
   638→		currentShort := currentHead
   639→		if len(currentShort) > 7 {
   640→			currentShort = currentShort[:7]
   641→		}
   642→		fmt.Printf("\nTo undo this reset: git reset --hard %s\n", currentShort)
   643→	}
   644→
   645→	return nil
   646→}
   647→
   648→func restoreSessionTranscript(transcriptFile, sessionID string, agent agentpkg.Agent) error {
   649→	sessionFile, err := resolveTranscriptPath(sessionID, agent)
   650→	if err != nil {
   651→		return err
   652→	}
   653→
   654→	// Ensure parent directory exists
   655→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(sessionFile), 0o750); err != nil {
   656→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create agent session directory: %w", err)
   657→	}
   658→
   659→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Copying transcript:\n  From: %s\n  To: %s\n", transcriptFile, sessionFile)
   660→	if err := copyFile(transcriptFile, sessionFile); err != nil {
   661→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to copy transcript: %w", err)
   662→	}
   663→
   664→	return nil
   665→}
   666→
   667→// restoreSessionTranscriptFromStrategy restores a session transcript from checkpoint storage.
   668→// This is used for strategies that store transcripts in git branches rather than local files.
   669→// Returns the session ID that was actually used (may differ from input if checkpoint provides one).
   670→func restoreSessionTranscriptFromStrategy(cpID id.CheckpointID, sessionID string, agent agentpkg.Agent) (string, error) {
   671→	// Get transcript content from checkpoint storage
   672→	content, returnedSessionID, err := checkpoint.LookupSessionLog(cpID)
   673→	if err != nil {
   674→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get session log: %w", err)
   675→	}
   676→
   677→	// Use session ID returned from checkpoint if available
   678→	// Otherwise fall back to the passed-in sessionID
   679→	if returnedSessionID != "" {
   680→		sessionID = returnedSessionID
   681→	}
   682→
   683→	return writeTranscriptToAgentSession(content, sessionID, agent)
   684→}
   685→
   686→// restoreSessionTranscriptFromShadow restores a session transcript from a shadow branch commit.
   687→// This is used for uncommitted checkpoints where the transcript is stored in the shadow branch tree.
   688→func restoreSessionTranscriptFromShadow(commitHash, metadataDir, sessionID string, agent agentpkg.Agent) (string, error) {
   689→	// Open repository
   690→	repo, err := git.PlainOpenWithOptions(".", &git.PlainOpenOptions{DetectDotGit: true})
   691→	if err != nil {
   692→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   693→	}
   694→
   695→	// Parse commit hash
   696→	hash := plumbing.NewHash(commitHash)
   697→	if hash.IsZero() {
   698→		return "", fmt.Errorf("invalid commit hash: %s", commitHash)
   699→	}
   700→
   701→	// Get transcript from shadow branch commit tree
   702→	store := checkpoint.NewGitStore(repo)
   703→	content, err := store.GetTranscriptFromCommit(hash, metadataDir, agent.Type())
   704→	if err != nil {
   705→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get transcript from shadow branch: %w", err)
   706→	}
   707→
   708→	return writeTranscriptToAgentSession(content, sessionID, agent)
   709→}
   710→
   711→// writeTranscriptToAgentSession writes transcript content to the agent's session storage.
   712→func writeTranscriptToAgentSession(content []byte, sessionID string, agent agentpkg.Agent) (string, error) {
   713→	sessionFile, err := resolveTranscriptPath(sessionID, agent)
   714→	if err != nil {
   715→		return "", err
   716→	}
   717→
   718→	// Ensure parent directory exists
   719→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(sessionFile), 0o750); err != nil {
   720→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to create agent session directory: %w", err)
   721→	}
   722→
   723→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Writing transcript to: %s\n", sessionFile)
   724→	if err := os.WriteFile(sessionFile, content, 0o600); err != nil {
   725→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   726→	}
   727→
   728→	return sessionID, nil
   729→}
   730→
   731→// restoreTaskCheckpointTranscript restores a truncated transcript for a task checkpoint.
   732→// Uses GetTaskCheckpointTranscript to fetch the transcript from the strategy.
   733→//
   734→// NOTE: The transcript parsing/truncation/writing pipeline (transcript.ParseFromBytes,
   735→// TruncateTranscriptAtUUID, writeTranscript) assumes Claude's JSONL format.
   736→// This is acceptable because task checkpoints are currently only created by Claude Code's
   737→// PostToolUse hook. If other agents gain sub-agent support, this will need a
   738→// format-aware refactor (agent-specific parsing, truncation, and serialization).
   739→func restoreTaskCheckpointTranscript(strat strategy.Strategy, point strategy.RewindPoint, sessionID, checkpointUUID string, agent agentpkg.Agent) error {
   740→	// Get transcript content from strategy
   741→	content, err := strat.GetTaskCheckpointTranscript(point)
   742→	if err != nil {
   743→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get task checkpoint transcript: %w", err)
   744→	}
   745→
   746→	// Parse the transcript
   747→	parsed, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(content)
   748→	if err != nil {
   749→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   750→	}
   751→
   752→	// Truncate at checkpoint UUID
   753→	truncated := TruncateTranscriptAtUUID(parsed, checkpointUUID)
   754→
   755→	sessionFile, err := resolveTranscriptPath(sessionID, agent)
   756→	if err != nil {
   757→		return err
   758→	}
   759→
   760→	// Ensure parent directory exists
   761→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(sessionFile), 0o750); err != nil {
   762→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create agent session directory: %w", err)
   763→	}
   764→
   765→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Writing truncated transcript to: %s\n", sessionFile)
   766→
   767→	if err := writeTranscript(sessionFile, truncated); err != nil {
   768→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write truncated transcript: %w", err)
   769→	}
   770→
   771→	return nil
   772→}
   773→
   774→// handleLogsOnlyRewindInteractive handles rewind for logs-only points with a sub-choice menu.
   775→func handleLogsOnlyRewindInteractive(start strategy.Strategy, point strategy.RewindPoint, shortID string) error {
   776→	var action string
   777→
   778→	form := NewAccessibleForm(
   779→		huh.NewGroup(
   780→			huh.NewSelect[string]().
   781→				Title("Logs-only point: "+shortID).
   782→				Description("This commit has session logs but no checkpoint state. Choose an action:").
   783→				Options(
   784→					huh.NewOption("Restore logs only (keep current files)", "logs"),
   785→					huh.NewOption("Checkout commit (detached HEAD, for viewing)", "checkout"),
   786→					huh.NewOption("Reset branch to this commit (destructive!)", "reset"),
   787→					huh.NewOption("Cancel", "cancel"),
   788→				).
   789→				Value(&action),
   790→		),
   791→	)
   792→
   793→	if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   794→		return fmt.Errorf("action selection cancelled: %w", err)
   795→	}
   796→
   797→	switch action {
   798→	case "logs":
   799→		return handleLogsOnlyRestore(start, point)
   800→	case "checkout":
   801→		return handleLogsOnlyCheckout(start, point, shortID)
   802→	case "reset":
   803→		return handleLogsOnlyReset(start, point, shortID)
   804→	case "cancel":
   805→		fmt.Println("Rewind cancelled.")
   806→		return nil
   807→	}
   808→
   809→	return nil
   810→}
   811→
   812→// handleLogsOnlyRestore restores only the session logs without changing files.
   813→func handleLogsOnlyRestore(start strategy.Strategy, point strategy.RewindPoint) error {
   814→	// Resolve agent once for use throughout
   815→	agent, err := getAgent(point.Agent)
   816→	if err != nil {
   817→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   818→	}
   819→
   820→	// Initialize logging context with agent from checkpoint
   821→	ctx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "rewind")
   822→	ctx = logging.WithAgent(ctx, agent.Name())
   823→
   824→	logging.Debug(ctx, "logs-only restore started",
   825→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   826→		slog.String("session_id", point.SessionID),
   827→	)
   828→
   829→	// Check if strategy supports logs-only restoration
   830→	restorer, ok := start.(strategy.LogsOnlyRestorer)
   831→	if !ok {
   832→		return errors.New("strategy does not support logs-only restoration")
   833→	}
   834→
   835→	// Restore logs
   836→	sessions, err := restorer.RestoreLogsOnly(point, true) // force=true for explicit rewind
   837→	if err != nil {
   838→		logging.Error(ctx, "logs-only restore failed",
   839→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   840→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   841→		)
   842→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to restore logs: %w", err)
   843→	}
   844→
   845→	logging.Debug(ctx, "logs-only restore completed",
   846→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   847→	)
   848→
   849→	// Show resume commands for all sessions
   850→	fmt.Println("Restored session logs.")
   851→	printMultiSessionResumeCommands(sessions)
   852→	return nil
   853→}
   854→
   855→// handleLogsOnlyCheckout restores logs and checks out the commit (detached HEAD).
   856→func handleLogsOnlyCheckout(start strategy.Strategy, point strategy.RewindPoint, shortID string) error {
   857→	// Resolve agent once for use throughout
   858→	agent, err := getAgent(point.Agent)
   859→	if err != nil {
   860→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   861→	}
   862→
   863→	// Initialize logging context with agent from checkpoint
   864→	ctx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "rewind")
   865→	ctx = logging.WithAgent(ctx, agent.Name())
   866→
   867→	logging.Debug(ctx, "logs-only checkout started",
   868→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   869→		slog.String("session_id", point.SessionID),
   870→	)
   871→
   872→	// First, restore the logs
   873→	restorer, ok := start.(strategy.LogsOnlyRestorer)
   874→	if !ok {
   875→		return errors.New("strategy does not support logs-only restoration")
   876→	}
   877→
   878→	sessions, err := restorer.RestoreLogsOnly(point, true) // force=true for explicit rewind
   879→	if err != nil {
   880→		logging.Error(ctx, "logs-only checkout failed during log restoration",
   881→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   882→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   883→		)
   884→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to restore logs: %w", err)
   885→	}
   886→
   887→	// Show warning about detached HEAD
   888→	var confirm bool
   889→	confirmForm := NewAccessibleForm(
   890→		huh.NewGroup(
   891→			huh.NewConfirm().
   892→				Title("Create detached HEAD?").
   893→				Description("This will checkout the commit directly. You'll be in 'detached HEAD' state.\nAny uncommitted changes will be lost!").
   894→				Value(&confirm),
   895→		),
   896→	)
   897→
   898→	if err := confirmForm.Run(); err != nil {
   899→		return fmt.Errorf("confirmation cancelled: %w", err)
   900→	}
   901→
   902→	if !confirm {
   903→		fmt.Println("Checkout cancelled. Session logs were still restored.")
   904→		printMultiSessionResumeCommands(sessions)
   905→		return nil
   906→	}
   907→
   908→	// Perform git checkout
   909→	if err := CheckoutBranch(point.ID); err != nil {
   910→		logging.Error(ctx, "logs-only checkout failed during git checkout",
   911→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   912→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   913→		)
   914→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to checkout commit: %w", err)
   915→	}
   916→
   917→	logging.Debug(ctx, "logs-only checkout completed",
   918→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   919→	)
   920→
   921→	fmt.Printf("Checked out %s (detached HEAD).\n", shortID)
   922→	printMultiSessionResumeCommands(sessions)
   923→	return nil
   924→}
   925→
   926→// handleLogsOnlyReset restores logs and resets the branch to the commit (destructive).
   927→func handleLogsOnlyReset(start strategy.Strategy, point strategy.RewindPoint, shortID string) error {
   928→	// Resolve agent once for use throughout
   929→	agent, agentErr := getAgent(point.Agent)
   930→	if agentErr != nil {
   931→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", agentErr)
   932→	}
   933→
   934→	// Initialize logging context with agent from checkpoint
   935→	ctx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "rewind")
   936→	ctx = logging.WithAgent(ctx, agent.Name())
   937→
   938→	logging.Debug(ctx, "logs-only reset (interactive) started",
   939→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   940→		slog.String("session_id", point.SessionID),
   941→	)
   942→
   943→	// First, restore the logs
   944→	restorer, ok := start.(strategy.LogsOnlyRestorer)
   945→	if !ok {
   946→		return errors.New("strategy does not support logs-only restoration")
   947→	}
   948→
   949→	sessions, restoreErr := restorer.RestoreLogsOnly(point, true) // force=true for explicit rewind
   950→	if restoreErr != nil {
   951→		logging.Error(ctx, "logs-only reset failed during log restoration",
   952→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   953→			slog.String("error", restoreErr.Error()),
   954→		)
   955→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to restore logs: %w", restoreErr)
   956→	}
   957→
   958→	// Get current HEAD before reset (for recovery message)
   959→	currentHead, err := getCurrentHeadHash()
   960→	if err != nil {
   961→		// Non-fatal - just won't show recovery message
   962→		currentHead = ""
   963→	}
   964→
   965→	// Get detailed uncommitted changes warning from strategy
   966→	var uncommittedWarning string
   967→	if _, warn, err := start.CanRewind(); err == nil {
   968→		uncommittedWarning = warn
   969→	}
   970→
   971→	// Check for safety issues
   972→	warnings, err := checkResetSafety(point.ID, uncommittedWarning)
   973→	if err != nil {
   974→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to check reset safety: %w", err)
   975→	}
   976→
   977→	// Build confirmation message based on warnings
   978→	var confirmTitle, confirmDesc string
   979→	if len(warnings) > 0 {
   980→		confirmTitle = "⚠️  Reset branch with warnings?"
   981→		confirmDesc = "WARNING - the following issues were detected:\n" +
   982→			strings.Join(warnings, "\n") +
   983→			"\n\nThis will move your branch to " + shortID + " and DISCARD commits after it!"
   984→	} else {
   985→		confirmTitle = "Reset branch to " + shortID + "?"
   986→		confirmDesc = "This will move your branch pointer to this commit.\nCommits after this point will be orphaned (but recoverable via reflog)."
   987→	}
   988→
   989→	var confirm bool
   990→	confirmForm := NewAccessibleForm(
   991→		huh.NewGroup(
   992→			huh.NewConfirm().
   993→				Title(confirmTitle).
   994→				Description(confirmDesc).
   995→				Value(&confirm),
   996→		),
   997→	)
   998→
   999→	if err := confirmForm.Run(); err != nil {
  1000→		return fmt.Errorf("confirmation cancelled: %w", err)
  1001→	}
  1002→
  1003→	if !confirm {
  1004→		fmt.Println("Reset cancelled. Session logs were still restored.")
  1005→		printMultiSessionResumeCommands(sessions)
  1006→		return nil
  1007→	}
  1008→
  1009→	// Perform git reset --hard
  1010→	if err := performGitResetHard(point.ID); err != nil {
  1011→		logging.Error(ctx, "logs-only reset failed during git reset",
  1012→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
  1013→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
  1014→		)
  1015→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to reset branch: %w", err)
  1016→	}
  1017→
  1018→	logging.Debug(ctx, "logs-only reset (interactive) completed",
  1019→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
  1020→	)
  1021→
  1022→	fmt.Printf("Reset branch to %s.\n", shortID)
  1023→	printMultiSessionResumeCommands(sessions)
  1024→
  1025→	// Show recovery instructions
  1026→	if currentHead != "" && currentHead != point.ID {
  1027→		currentShort := currentHead
  1028→		if len(currentShort) > 7 {
  1029→			currentShort = currentShort[:7]
  1030→		}
  1031→		fmt.Printf("\nTo undo this reset: git reset --hard %s\n", currentShort)
  1032→	}
  1033→
  1034→	return nil
  1035→}
  1036→
  1037→// getCurrentHeadHash returns the current HEAD commit hash.
  1038→func getCurrentHeadHash() (string, error) {
  1039→	repo, err := openRepository()
  1040→	if err != nil {
  1041→		return "", err
  1042→	}
  1043→
  1044→	head, err := repo.Head()
  1045→	if err != nil {
  1046→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
  1047→	}
  1048→
  1049→	return head.Hash().String(), nil
  1050→}
  1051→
  1052→// checkResetSafety checks for potential issues before a git reset --hard.
  1053→// Returns a list of warning messages (empty if safe to proceed without warnings).
  1054→// If uncommittedChangesWarning is provided, it will be used instead of a generic warning.
  1055→func checkResetSafety(targetCommitHash string, uncommittedChangesWarning string) ([]string, error) {
  1056→	var warnings []string
  1057→
  1058→	repo, err := openRepository()
  1059→	if err != nil {
  1060→		return nil, err
  1061→	}
  1062→
  1063→	// Check for uncommitted changes
  1064→	if uncommittedChangesWarning != "" {
  1065→		// Use the detailed warning from strategy's CanRewind()
  1066→		warnings = append(warnings, uncommittedChangesWarning)
  1067→	} else {
  1068→		// Fall back to generic check
  1069→		worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
  1070→		if err != nil {
  1071→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree: %w", err)
  1072→		}
  1073→
  1074→		status, err := worktree.Status()
  1075→		if err != nil {
  1076→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get status: %w", err)
  1077→		}
  1078→
  1079→		if !status.IsClean() {
  1080→			warnings = append(warnings, "• You have uncommitted changes that will be LOST")
  1081→		}
  1082→	}
  1083→
  1084→	// Check if current HEAD is ahead of target (we'd be discarding commits)
  1085→	head, err := repo.Head()
  1086→	if err != nil {
  1087→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
  1088→	}
  1089→
  1090→	targetHash := plumbing.NewHash(targetCommitHash)
  1091→
  1092→	// Count commits between target and HEAD
  1093→	commitsAhead, err := countCommitsBetween(repo, targetHash, head.Hash())
  1094→	if err != nil {
  1095→		// Non-fatal - just can't show commit count
  1096→		commitsAhead = -1
  1097→	}
  1098→
  1099→	if commitsAhead > 0 {
  1100→		warnings = append(warnings, fmt.Sprintf("• %d commit(s) after this point will be orphaned", commitsAhead))
  1101→	}
  1102→
  1103→	return warnings, nil
  1104→}
  1105→
  1106→// countCommitsBetween counts commits between ancestor and descendant.
  1107→// Returns 0 if ancestor == descendant, -1 on error.
  1108→func countCommitsBetween(repo *git.Repository, ancestor, descendant plumbing.Hash) (int, error) {
  1109→	if ancestor == descendant {
  1110→		return 0, nil
  1111→	}
  1112→
  1113→	// Walk from descendant back to ancestor
  1114→	count := 0
  1115→	current := descendant
  1116→
  1117→	for count < 1000 { // Safety limit
  1118→		if current == ancestor {
  1119→			return count, nil
  1120→		}
  1121→
  1122→		commit, err := repo.CommitObject(current)
  1123→		if err != nil {
  1124→			return -1, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit: %w", err)
  1125→		}
  1126→
  1127→		if commit.NumParents() == 0 {
  1128→			// Reached root without finding ancestor - ancestor not in history
  1129→			return -1, nil
  1130→		}
  1131→
  1132→		count++
  1133→		current = commit.ParentHashes[0] // Follow first parent
  1134→	}
  1135→
  1136→	return -1, nil
  1137→}
  1138→
  1139→// performGitResetHard performs a git reset --hard to the specified commit.
  1140→// Uses the git CLI instead of go-git because go-git's HardReset incorrectly
  1141→// deletes untracked directories (like .entire/) even when they're in .gitignore.
  1142→func performGitResetHard(commitHash string) error {
  1143→	ctx := context.Background()
  1144→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "reset", "--hard", commitHash)
  1145→	if output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
  1146→		return fmt.Errorf("reset failed: %s: %w", strings.TrimSpace(string(output)), err)
  1147→	}
  1148→	return nil
  1149→}
  1150→
  1151→// sanitizeForTerminal removes or replaces characters that cause rendering issues
  1152→// in terminal UI components. This includes emojis with skin-tone modifiers and
  1153→// other multi-codepoint characters that confuse width calculations.
  1154→func sanitizeForTerminal(s string) string {
  1155→	var result strings.Builder
  1156→	result.Grow(len(s))
  1157→
  1158→	for _, r := range s {
  1159→		// Skip emoji skin tone modifiers (U+1F3FB to U+1F3FF)
  1160→		if r >= 0x1F3FB && r <= 0x1F3FF {
  1161→			continue
  1162→		}
  1163→		// Skip zero-width joiners used in emoji sequences
  1164→		if r == 0x200D {
  1165→			continue
  1166→		}
  1167→		// Skip variation selectors (U+FE00 to U+FE0F)
  1168→		if r >= 0xFE00 && r <= 0xFE0F {
  1169→			continue
  1170→		}
  1171→		// Keep printable characters and common whitespace
  1172→		if unicode.IsPrint(r) || r == '\t' || r == '\n' {
  1173→			result.WriteRune(r)
  1174→		}
  1175→	}
  1176→
  1177→	return result.String()
  1178→}
  1179→
  1180→// printMultiSessionResumeCommands prints resume commands for restored sessions.
  1181→// Each session may have a different agent, so per-session agent resolution is used.
  1182→func printMultiSessionResumeCommands(sessions []strategy.RestoredSession) {
  1183→	if len(sessions) == 0 {
  1184→		return
  1185→	}
  1186→
  1187→	if len(sessions) > 1 {
  1188→		fmt.Printf("\nRestored %d sessions. Resume with:\n", len(sessions))
  1189→	}
  1190→
  1191→	for i, sess := range sessions {
  1192→		ag, err := strategy.ResolveAgentForRewind(sess.Agent)
  1193→		if err != nil {
  1194→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Warning: could not resolve agent %q for session %s, skipping\n", sess.Agent, sess.SessionID)
  1195→			continue
  1196→		}
  1197→
  1198→		cmd := ag.FormatResumeCommand(sess.SessionID)
  1199→
  1200→		if len(sessions) > 1 {
  1201→			// Add "(most recent)" label to the last session
  1202→			if i == len(sessions)-1 {
  1203→				if sess.Prompt != "" {
  1204→					fmt.Printf("  %s  # %s (most recent)\n", cmd, sess.Prompt)
  1205→				} else {
  1206→					fmt.Printf("  %s  # (most recent)\n", cmd)
  1207→				}
  1208→			} else {
  1209→				if sess.Prompt != "" {
  1210→					fmt.Printf("  %s  # %s\n", cmd, sess.Prompt)
  1211→				} else {
  1212→					fmt.Printf("  %s\n", cmd)
  1213→				}
  1214→			}
  1215→		} else {
  1216→			if sess.Prompt != "" {
  1217→				fmt.Printf("%s  # %s\n", cmd, sess.Prompt)
  1218→			} else {
  1219→				fmt.Printf("%s\n", cmd)
  1220→			}
  1221→		}
  1222→	}
  1223→}
  1224→

    39→// CheckpointPath returns the sharded storage path for a checkpoint ID.
    40→// Uses first 2 characters as shard (256 buckets), remaining as folder name.
    41→// Example: "a3b2c4d5e6f7" -> "a3/b2c4d5e6f7"
    42→//

    46→}
    47→
    48→// repoRootCache caches the repository root to avoid repeated git commands.
    49→// The cache is keyed by the current working directory to handle directory changes.
    50→var (
    51→	repoRootMu       sync.RWMutex
    52→	repoRootCache    string
    53→	repoRootCacheDir string
    54→)
    55→
    56→// RepoRoot returns the git repository root directory.
    57→// Uses 'git rev-parse --show-toplevel' which works from any subdirectory.
    58→// The result is cached per working directory.
    59→// Returns an error if not inside a git repository.
    60→func RepoRoot() (string, error) {
    61→	// Get current working directory to check cache validity
    62→	cwd, err := os.Getwd() //nolint:forbidigo // already present in codebase
    63→	if err != nil {
    64→		cwd = ""
    65→	}
    66→
    67→	// Check cache with read lock first
    68→	repoRootMu.RLock()
    69→	if repoRootCache != "" && repoRootCacheDir == cwd {
    70→		cached := repoRootCache
    71→		repoRootMu.RUnlock()
    72→		return cached, nil
    73→	}
    74→	repoRootMu.RUnlock()
    75→
    76→	// Cache miss - get repo root and update cache with write lock
    77→	ctx := context.Background()
    78→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel")
    79→	output, err := cmd.Output()
    80→	if err != nil {
    81→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get git repository root: %w", err)
    82→	}
    83→
    84→	root := strings.TrimSpace(string(output))
    85→
    86→	repoRootMu.Lock()
    87→	repoRootCache = root
    88→	repoRootCacheDir = cwd
    89→	repoRootMu.Unlock()
    90→
    91→	return root, nil
    92→}
    93→
    94→// ClearRepoRootCache clears the cached repository root.
    95→// This is primarily useful for testing when changing directories.
    96→func ClearRepoRootCache() {
    97→	repoRootMu.Lock()
    98→	repoRootCache = ""
    99→	repoRootCacheDir = ""
   100→	repoRootMu.Unlock()
   101→}
   102→
   103→// AbsPath returns the absolute path for a relative path within the repository.
   104→// If the path is already absolute, it is returned as-is.
   105→// Uses RepoRoot() to resolve paths relative to the repository root.
   106→func AbsPath(relPath string) (string, error) {
   107→	if filepath.IsAbs(relPath) {
   108→		return relPath, nil
   109→	}
   110→
   111→	root, err := RepoRoot()
   112→	if err != nil {
   113→		return "", err
   114→	}
   115→
   116→	return filepath.Join(root, relPath), nil
   117→}
   118→
   119→// IsInfrastructurePath returns true if the path is part of CLI infrastructure
   120→// (i.e., inside the .entire directory)
   121→func IsInfrastructurePath(path string) bool {
   122→	return strings.HasPrefix(path, EntireDir+"/") || path == EntireDir
   123→}
   124→
   125→// ToRelativePath converts an absolute path to relative.
   126→// Returns empty string if the path is outside the working directory.
   127→func ToRelativePath(absPath, cwd string) string {
   128→	if !filepath.IsAbs(absPath) {
   129→		return absPath
   130→	}
   131→	relPath, err := filepath.Rel(cwd, absPath)
   132→	if err != nil || strings.HasPrefix(relPath, "..") {
   133→		return ""
   134→	}
   135→	return relPath
   136→}
   137→
   138→// nonAlphanumericRegex matches any non-alphanumeric character
   139→var nonAlphanumericRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`[^a-zA-Z0-9]`)
   140→
   141→// SanitizePathForClaude converts a path to Claude's project directory format.
   142→// Claude replaces any non-alphanumeric character with a dash.
   143→func SanitizePathForClaude(path string) string {
   144→	return nonAlphanumericRegex.ReplaceAllString(path, "-")
   145→}
   146→
   147→// GetClaudeProjectDir returns the directory where Claude stores session transcripts
   148→// for the given repository path.
   149→//
   150→// In test environments, set ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR to override the default location.
   151→func GetClaudeProjectDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {
   152→	override := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR")
   153→	if override != "" {
   154→		return override, nil
   155→	}
   156→
   157→	homeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   158→	if err != nil {
   159→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get home directory: %w", err)
   160→	}
   161→
   162→	projectDir := SanitizePathForClaude(repoPath)
   163→	return filepath.Join(homeDir, ".claude", "projects", projectDir), nil
   164→}
   165→
   166→// SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID returns the path to a session's metadata directory
   167→// for the given Entire session ID. The sessionID must be the full, already date-prefixed
   168→// Entire session identifier as stored on disk, not an agent-specific or raw Claude ID.
   169→func SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID string) string {
   170→	return EntireMetadataDir + "/" + sessionID
   171→}
   172→
   173→// ExtractSessionIDFromTranscriptPath attempts to extract a session ID from a transcript path.
   174→// Claude transcripts are stored at ~/.claude/projects/<project>/sessions/<id>.jsonl
   175→// If the path doesn't match expected format, returns empty string.
   176→func ExtractSessionIDFromTranscriptPath(transcriptPath string) string {
   177→	// Try to extract from typical path: ~/.claude/projects/<project>/sessions/<id>.jsonl
   178→	parts := strings.Split(filepath.ToSlash(transcriptPath), "/")
   179→	for i, part := range parts {
   180→		if part == "sessions" && i+1 < len(parts) {
   181→			// Return filename without extension
   182→			filename := parts[i+1]
   183→			if strings.HasSuffix(filename, ".jsonl") {
   184→				return strings.TrimSuffix(filename, ".jsonl")
   185→			}
   186→			return filename
   187→		}
   188→	}
   189→	return ""
   190→}

  "content": "// Package trail provides a git-native task queue for autonomous agent execution.\n//\n// Trails are task definitions stored in the entire/trails orphan branch.\n// Execution state is tracked via lightweight git refs (no commits for state changes).\n// The Trail Runner discovers open trails, claims them atomically, creates worktrees,\n// and runs agents to complete the work.\npackage trail\n\nimport (\n\t\"crypto/rand\"\n\t\"encoding/hex\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"regexp\"\n\t\"time\"\n)\n\n// TrailID is a 12-character hex identifier for trails.\n// This follows the same pattern as CheckpointID for consistency.\n//\n//nolint:recvcheck // UnmarshalJSON requires pointer receiver, others use value receiver\ntype TrailID string\n\n// EmptyTrailID represents an unset or invalid trail ID.\nconst EmptyTrailID TrailID = \"\"\n\n// trailIDPattern is the regex pattern for a valid trail ID: exactly 12 lowercase hex characters.\nconst trailIDPattern = `[0-9a-f]{12}`\n\n// trailIDRegex validates the format: exactly 12 lowercase hex characters.\nvar trailIDRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`^` + trailIDPattern + `$`)\n\n// NewTrailID creates a TrailID from a string, validating its format.\n// Returns an error if the string is not a valid 12-character hex ID.\nfunc NewTrailID(s string) (TrailID, error) {\n\tif err := ValidateTrailID(s); err != nil {\n\t\treturn EmptyTrailID, err\n\t}\n\treturn TrailID(s), nil\n}\n\n// MustTrailID creates a TrailID from a string, panicking if invalid.\n// Use only when the ID is known to be valid (e.g., from trusted sources).\nfunc MustTrailID(s string) TrailID {\n\tid, err := NewTrailID(s)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tpanic(err)\n\t}\n\treturn id\n}\n\n// GenerateTrailID creates a new random 12-character hex trail ID.\nfunc GenerateTrailID() (TrailID, error) {\n\tbytes := make([]byte, 6) // 6 bytes = 12 hex chars\n\tif _, err := rand.Read(bytes); err != nil {\n\t\treturn EmptyTrailID, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to generate random trail ID: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn TrailID(hex.EncodeToString(bytes)), nil\n}\n\n// ValidateTrailID checks if a string is a valid trail ID format.\n// Returns an error if invalid, nil if valid.\nfunc ValidateTrailID(s string) error {\n\tif !trailIDRegex.MatchString(s) {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"invalid trail ID %q: must be 12 lowercase hex characters\", s)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// String returns the trail ID as a string.\nfunc (id TrailID) String() string {\n\treturn string(id)\n}\n\n// IsEmpty returns true if the trail ID is empty or unset.\nfunc (id TrailID) IsEmpty() bool {\n\treturn id == EmptyTrailID\n}\n\n// Short returns the first 8 characters of the trail ID for display.\nfunc (id TrailID) Short() string {\n\tif len(id) >= 8 {\n\t\treturn string(id[:8])\n\t}\n\treturn string(id)\n}\n\n// MarshalJSON implements json.Marshaler.\nfunc (id TrailID) MarshalJSON() ([]byte, error) {\n\tdata, err := json.Marshal(string(id))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to marshal trail ID: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn data, nil\n}\n\n// UnmarshalJSON implements json.Unmarshaler with validation.\n// Returns an error if the JSON string is not a valid 12-character hex ID.\n// Empty strings are allowed and result in EmptyTrailID.\nfunc (id *TrailID) UnmarshalJSON(data []byte) error {\n\tvar s string\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &s); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to unmarshal trail ID: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\t// Allow empty strings (represents unset trail ID)\n\tif s == \"\" {\n\t\t*id = EmptyTrailID\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tif err := ValidateTrailID(s); err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\t*id = TrailID(s)\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// TrailState represents the execution state of a trail.\ntype TrailState string\n\nconst (\n\t// TrailStateOpen indicates the trail is available for execution.\n\t// No state refs exist for this trail.\n\tTrailStateOpen TrailState = \"open\"\n\n\t// TrailStateInProgress indicates the trail is being executed.\n\t// The claimed ref exists.\n\tTrailStateInProgress TrailState = \"in_progress\"\n\n\t// TrailStateCompleted indicates the trail finished successfully.\n\t// The completed ref exists.\n\tTrailStateCompleted TrailState = \"completed\"\n\n\t// TrailStateFailed indicates the trail execution failed.\n\t// The failed ref exists.\n\tTrailStateFailed TrailState = \"failed\"\n)\n\n// Trail represents a task definition stored in the entire/trails branch.\ntype Trail struct {\n\t// ID is the unique identifier for this trail.\n\tID TrailID `json:\"id\"`\n\n\t// Title is a brief description of the task.\n\tTitle string `json:\"title\"`\n\n\t// Description is the full task description/prompt for the agent.\n\tDescription string `json:\"description\"`\n\n\t// Branch is the name of the branch to create for this trail's work.\n\t// If empty, defaults to \"trail/<id>\".\n\tBranch string `json:\"branch\"`\n\n\t// BaseBranch is the branch to create the work branch from.\n\t// If empty, defaults to the repository's default branch.\n\tBaseBranch string `json:\"base_branch\"`\n\n\t// AuthorID identifies who created this trail.\n\tAuthorID string `json:\"author_id,omitempty\"`\n\n\t// Assignees are optional user IDs that should work on this trail.\n\tAssignees []string `json:\"assignees,omitempty\"`\n\n\t// Labels are optional tags for categorization.\n\tLabels []string `json:\"labels,omitempty\"`\n\n\t// CreatedAt is when the trail was created.\n\tCreatedAt time.Time `json:\"created_at\"`\n\n\t// UpdatedAt is when the trail was last modified.\n\tUpdatedAt time.Time `json:\"updated_at\"`\n}\n\n// Validate checks if the trail has all required fields.\nfunc (t *Trail) Validate() error {\n\tif t.ID.IsEmpty() {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"trail ID is required\")\n\t}\n\tif t.Title == \"\" {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"trail title is required\")\n\t}\n\tif t.Description == \"\" {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"trail description is required\")\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// GetBranch returns the branch name for this trail.\n// Returns the configured Branch, or a default \"trail/<id>\" if not set.\nfunc (t *Trail) GetBranch() string {\n\tif t.Branch != \"\" {\n\t\treturn t.Branch\n\t}\n\treturn \"trail/\" + t.ID.String()\n}\n\n// TrailWithState combines a trail with its current execution state.\ntype TrailWithState struct {\n\tTrail\n\tState TrailState `json:\"state\"`\n}\n"
}
