  "content": "package trail\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/jsonutil\"\n\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/filemode\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/object\"\n)\n\n// TrailsBranchName is the orphan branch where trail definitions are stored.\nconst TrailsBranchName = \"entire/trails\"\n\n// MetadataFileName is the name of the metadata file within each trail directory.\nconst MetadataFileName = \"metadata.json\"\n\n// Store provides read/write access to trails stored in the entire/trails branch.\ntype Store struct {\n\trepo *git.Repository\n}\n\n// NewStore creates a new trail store for the given repository.\nfunc NewStore(repo *git.Repository) *Store {\n\treturn &Store{repo: repo}\n}\n\n// EnsureBranch creates the entire/trails orphan branch if it doesn't exist.\nfunc (s *Store) EnsureBranch(ctx context.Context) error {\n\t_ = ctx // Reserved for future use\n\n\trefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(TrailsBranchName)\n\t_, err := s.repo.Reference(refName, true)\n\tif err == nil {\n\t\treturn nil // Branch exists\n\t}\n\n\t// Create orphan branch with empty tree\n\temptyTreeHash, err := checkpoint.BuildTreeFromEntries(s.repo, make(map[string]object.TreeEntry))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create empty tree: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tauthorName, authorEmail := checkpoint.GetGitAuthorFromRepo(s.repo)\n\tcommitHash, err := s.createCommit(emptyTreeHash, plumbing.ZeroHash, \"Initialize trails branch\", authorName, authorEmail)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create initial commit: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tnewRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, commitHash)\n\tif err := s.repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to set branch reference: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// Create writes a new trail to the entire/trails branch.\nfunc (s *Store) Create(ctx context.Context, trail *Trail) error {\n\tif err := trail.Validate(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"invalid trail: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Ensure branch exists\n\tif err := s.EnsureBranch(ctx); err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\t// Get current branch tip and flatten tree\n\tref, entries, err := s.getBranchEntries()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\t// Check if trail already exists\n\ttrailPath := trail.ID.String() + \"/\" + MetadataFileName\n\tif _, exists := entries[trailPath]; exists {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"trail %s already exists\", trail.ID)\n\t}\n\n\t// Set timestamps if not set\n\tnow := time.Now().UTC()\n\tif trail.CreatedAt.IsZero() {\n\t\ttrail.CreatedAt = now\n\t}\n\tif trail.UpdatedAt.IsZero() {\n\t\ttrail.UpdatedAt = now\n\t}\n\n\t// Marshal trail to JSON\n\ttrailJSON, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(trail, \"\", \"  \")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to marshal trail: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Create blob\n\tblobHash, err := checkpoint.CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, trailJSON)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create trail blob: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Add to entries\n\tentries[trailPath] = object.TreeEntry{\n\t\tName: trailPath,\n\t\tMode: filemode.Regular,\n\t\tHash: blobHash,\n\t}\n\n\t// Build and commit\n\tnewTreeHash, err := checkpoint.BuildTreeFromEntries(s.repo, entries)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to build tree: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tauthorName, authorEmail := checkpoint.GetGitAuthorFromRepo(s.repo)\n\tcommitMsg := fmt.Sprintf(\"Create trail: %s\\n\\n%s\", trail.ID.Short(), trail.Title)\n\tnewCommitHash, err := s.createCommit(newTreeHash, ref.Hash(), commitMsg, authorName, authorEmail)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create commit: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\trefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(TrailsBranchName)\n\tnewRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, newCommitHash)\n\tif err := s.repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to update branch reference: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// Get retrieves a trail by ID from the entire/trails branch.\n// Returns nil, nil if the trail doesn't exist.\nfunc (s *Store) Get(ctx context.Context, id TrailID) (*Trail, error) {\n\t_ = ctx // Reserved for future use\n\n\ttree, err := s.getBranchTree()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil //nolint:nilnil,nilerr // No branch means no trail\n\t}\n\n\t// Try to get the trail directory\n\ttrailTree, err := tree.Tree(id.String())\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil //nolint:nilnil,nilerr // Trail directory not found\n\t}\n\n\t// Read metadata.json\n\tmetadataFile, err := trailTree.File(MetadataFileName)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil //nolint:nilnil,nilerr // metadata.json not found\n\t}\n\n\tcontent, err := metadataFile.Contents()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read trail metadata: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tvar trail Trail\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &trail); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse trail metadata: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn &trail, nil\n}\n\n// List returns all trails from the entire/trails branch.\nfunc (s *Store) List(ctx context.Context) ([]*Trail, error) {\n\t_ = ctx // Reserved for future use\n\n\ttree, err := s.getBranchTree()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn []*Trail{}, nil //nolint:nilerr // No branch means empty list\n\t}\n\n\tvar trails []*Trail\n\n\t// Each entry at root level should be a trail directory\n\tfor _, entry := range tree.Entries {\n\t\tif entry.Mode != filemode.Dir {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Validate that entry name is a valid trail ID\n\t\tif err := ValidateTrailID(entry.Name); err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue // Skip invalid directories\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Get the trail directory\n\t\ttrailTree, err := s.repo.TreeObject(entry.Hash)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Read metadata.json\n\t\tmetadataFile, err := trailTree.File(MetadataFileName)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tcontent, err := metadataFile.Contents()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tvar trail Trail\n\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &trail); err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\ttrails = append(trails, &trail)\n\t}\n\n\treturn trails, nil\n}\n\n// Update modifies an existing trail in the entire/trails branch.\nfunc (s *Store) Update(ctx context.Context, trail *Trail) error {\n\tif err := trail.Validate(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"invalid trail: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Get current branch tip and flatten tree\n\tref, entries, err := s.getBranchEntries()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\t// Check if trail exists\n\ttrailPath := trail.ID.String() + \"/\" + MetadataFileName\n\tif _, exists := entries[trailPath]; !exists {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"trail %s not found\", trail.ID)\n\t}\n\n\t// Update timestamp\n\ttrail.UpdatedAt = time.Now().UTC()\n\n\t// Marshal trail to JSON\n\ttrailJSON, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(trail, \"\", \"  \")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to marshal trail: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Create blob\n\tblobHash, err := checkpoint.CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, trailJSON)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create trail blob: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Update entry\n\tentries[trailPath] = object.TreeEntry{\n\t\tName: trailPath,\n\t\tMode: filemode.Regular,\n\t\tHash: blobHash,\n\t}\n\n\t// Build and commit\n\tnewTreeHash, err := checkpoint.BuildTreeFromEntries(s.repo, entries)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to build tree: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tauthorName, authorEmail := checkpoint.GetGitAuthorFromRepo(s.repo)\n\tcommitMsg := fmt.Sprintf(\"Update trail: %s\\n\\n%s\", trail.ID.Short(), trail.Title)\n\tnewCommitHash, err := s.createCommit(newTreeHash, ref.Hash(), commitMsg, authorName, authorEmail)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create commit: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\trefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(TrailsBranchName)\n\tnewRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, newCommitHash)\n\tif err := s.repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to update branch reference: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// Delete removes a trail from the entire/trails branch.\nfunc (s *Store) Delete(ctx context.Context, id TrailID) error {\n\t_ = ctx // Reserved for future use\n\n\t// Get current branch tip and flatten tree\n\tref, entries, err := s.getBranchEntries()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\t// Find and remove all entries for this trail\n\ttrailPrefix := id.String() + \"/\"\n\tfound := false\n\tfor key := range entries {\n\t\tif len(key) >= len(trailPrefix) && key[:len(trailPrefix)] == trailPrefix {\n\t\t\tdelete(entries, key)\n\t\t\tfound = true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif !found {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"trail %s not found\", id)\n\t}\n\n\t// Build and commit\n\tnewTreeHash, err := checkpoint.BuildTreeFromEntries(s.repo, entries)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to build tree: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tauthorName, authorEmail := checkpoint.GetGitAuthorFromRepo(s.repo)\n\tcommitMsg := fmt.Sprintf(\"Delete trail: %s\", id.Short())\n\tnewCommitHash, err := s.createCommit(newTreeHash, ref.Hash(), commitMsg, authorName, authorEmail)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create commit: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\trefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(TrailsBranchName)\n\tnewRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, newCommitHash)\n\tif err := s.repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to update branch reference: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// getBranchEntries returns the trails branch reference and flattened tree entries.\nfunc (s *Store) getBranchEntries() (*plumbing.Reference, map[string]object.TreeEntry, error) {\n\trefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(TrailsBranchName)\n\tref, err := s.repo.Reference(refName, true)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get trails branch reference: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tparentCommit, err := s.repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get commit object: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tbaseTree, err := parentCommit.Tree()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get commit tree: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tentries := make(map[string]object.TreeEntry)\n\tif err := checkpoint.FlattenTree(s.repo, baseTree, \"\", entries); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, err\n\t}\n\n\treturn ref, entries, nil\n}\n\n// getBranchTree returns the tree object for the entire/trails branch.\nfunc (s *Store) getBranchTree() (*object.Tree, error) {\n\trefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(TrailsBranchName)\n\tref, err := s.repo.Reference(refName, true)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"trails branch not found: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tcommit, err := s.repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get commit object: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\ttree, err := commit.Tree()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get commit tree: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn tree, nil\n}\n\n// createCommit creates a commit with the given tree and parent.\nfunc (s *Store) createCommit(treeHash, parentHash plumbing.Hash, message, authorName, authorEmail string) (plumbing.Hash, error) {\n\tcommit := &object.Commit{\n\t\tTreeHash: treeHash,\n\t\tMessage:  message,\n\t\tAuthor: object.Signature{\n\t\t\tName:  authorName,\n\t\t\tEmail: authorEmail,\n\t\t\tWhen:  time.Now(),\n\t\t},\n\t\tCommitter: object.Signature{\n\t\t\tName:  authorName,\n\t\t\tEmail: authorEmail,\n\t\t\tWhen:  time.Now(),\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\t// Add parent if not zero (not an orphan commit)\n\tif parentHash != plumbing.ZeroHash {\n\t\tcommit.ParentHashes = []plumbing.Hash{parentHash}\n\t}\n\n\tobj := s.repo.Storer.NewEncodedObject()\n\tif err := commit.Encode(obj); err != nil {\n\t\treturn plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to encode commit: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\thash, err := s.repo.Storer.SetEncodedObject(obj)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to store commit: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn hash, nil\n}\n"
}

  "content": "package trail\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"errors\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing\"\n)\n\n// RefPrefix is the prefix for all trail state refs.\nconst RefPrefix = \"refs/entire/trails/\"\n\n// State ref suffixes\nconst (\n\tRefSuffixClaimed   = \"/claimed\"\n\tRefSuffixCompleted = \"/completed\"\n\tRefSuffixFailed    = \"/failed\"\n)\n\n// ErrAlreadyClaimed is returned when attempting to claim a trail that is already claimed.\nvar ErrAlreadyClaimed = errors.New(\"trail is already claimed\")\n\n// ErrNotClaimed is returned when attempting to complete/fail a trail that is not claimed.\nvar ErrNotClaimed = errors.New(\"trail is not claimed\")\n\n// StateManager manages trail execution state using git refs.\n// State is tracked via lightweight refs (no commits for state changes):\n//   - refs/entire/trails/<id>/claimed   -> branch HEAD when claimed\n//   - refs/entire/trails/<id>/completed -> branch HEAD when done\n//   - refs/entire/trails/<id>/failed    -> branch HEAD on error\ntype StateManager struct {\n\trepo *git.Repository\n}\n\n// NewStateManager creates a new state manager for the given repository.\nfunc NewStateManager(repo *git.Repository) *StateManager {\n\treturn &StateManager{repo: repo}\n}\n\n// GetState returns the current execution state of a trail.\nfunc (m *StateManager) GetState(ctx context.Context, id TrailID) (TrailState, error) {\n\t_ = ctx // Reserved for future use\n\n\t// Check states in priority order: completed > failed > claimed > open\n\tif m.refExists(id, RefSuffixCompleted) {\n\t\treturn TrailStateCompleted, nil\n\t}\n\tif m.refExists(id, RefSuffixFailed) {\n\t\treturn TrailStateFailed, nil\n\t}\n\tif m.refExists(id, RefSuffixClaimed) {\n\t\treturn TrailStateInProgress, nil\n\t}\n\treturn TrailStateOpen, nil\n}\n\n// Claim atomically claims a trail for execution.\n// Returns ErrAlreadyClaimed if the trail is already claimed.\n// The ref points to the current HEAD of the work branch.\nfunc (m *StateManager) Claim(ctx context.Context, id TrailID, branchHead plumbing.Hash) error {\n\t_ = ctx // Reserved for future use\n\n\trefName := m.refName(id, RefSuffixClaimed)\n\n\t// Check if already claimed\n\tif _, err := m.repo.Reference(refName, true); err == nil {\n\t\treturn ErrAlreadyClaimed\n\t}\n\n\t// Create the claimed ref\n\tref := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, branchHead)\n\tif err := m.repo.Storer.SetReference(ref); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create claimed ref: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// Complete marks a trail as successfully completed.\n// The ref points to the final HEAD of the work branch.\nfunc (m *StateManager) Complete(ctx context.Context, id TrailID, branchHead plumbing.Hash) error {\n\t_ = ctx // Reserved for future use\n\n\t// Verify trail was claimed\n\tclaimedRef := m.refName(id, RefSuffixClaimed)\n\tif _, err := m.repo.Reference(claimedRef, true); err != nil {\n\t\treturn ErrNotClaimed\n\t}\n\n\t// Create completed ref\n\tcompletedRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(m.refName(id, RefSuffixCompleted), branchHead)\n\tif err := m.repo.Storer.SetReference(completedRef); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create completed ref: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Remove claimed ref\n\tif err := m.repo.Storer.RemoveReference(claimedRef); err != nil {\n\t\t// Non-fatal: completed ref was created successfully\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// Fail marks a trail as failed.\n// The ref points to the HEAD at time of failure.\nfunc (m *StateManager) Fail(ctx context.Context, id TrailID, branchHead plumbing.Hash) error {\n\t_ = ctx // Reserved for future use\n\n\t// Verify trail was claimed\n\tclaimedRef := m.refName(id, RefSuffixClaimed)\n\tif _, err := m.repo.Reference(claimedRef, true); err != nil {\n\t\treturn ErrNotClaimed\n\t}\n\n\t// Create failed ref\n\tfailedRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(m.refName(id, RefSuffixFailed), branchHead)\n\tif err := m.repo.Storer.SetReference(failedRef); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create failed ref: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Remove claimed ref\n\tif err := m.repo.Storer.RemoveReference(claimedRef); err != nil {\n\t\t// Non-fatal: failed ref was created successfully\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// Release releases a claimed trail without marking it completed or failed.\n// This allows another runner to pick up the trail.\nfunc (m *StateManager) Release(ctx context.Context, id TrailID) error {\n\t_ = ctx // Reserved for future use\n\n\tclaimedRef := m.refName(id, RefSuffixClaimed)\n\tif _, err := m.repo.Reference(claimedRef, true); err != nil {\n\t\treturn ErrNotClaimed\n\t}\n\n\tif err := m.repo.Storer.RemoveReference(claimedRef); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to remove claimed ref: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// Reset removes all state refs for a trail, returning it to open state.\n// This is useful for retrying a failed trail.\nfunc (m *StateManager) Reset(ctx context.Context, id TrailID) error {\n\t_ = ctx // Reserved for future use\n\n\t// Remove all state refs\n\tfor _, suffix := range []string{RefSuffixClaimed, RefSuffixCompleted, RefSuffixFailed} {\n\t\trefName := m.refName(id, suffix)\n\t\tif _, err := m.repo.Reference(refName, true); err == nil {\n\t\t\tif removeErr := m.repo.Storer.RemoveReference(refName); removeErr != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to remove ref %s: %w\", refName, removeErr)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// GetClaimedRef returns the commit hash that the claimed ref points to.\n// Returns plumbing.ZeroHash if not claimed.\nfunc (m *StateManager) GetClaimedRef(ctx context.Context, id TrailID) (plumbing.Hash, error) {\n\t_ = ctx // Reserved for future use\n\n\tref, err := m.repo.Reference(m.refName(id, RefSuffixClaimed), true)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn plumbing.ZeroHash, nil //nolint:nilerr // No ref means not claimed\n\t}\n\treturn ref.Hash(), nil\n}\n\n// GetCompletedRef returns the commit hash that the completed ref points to.\n// Returns plumbing.ZeroHash if not completed.\nfunc (m *StateManager) GetCompletedRef(ctx context.Context, id TrailID) (plumbing.Hash, error) {\n\t_ = ctx // Reserved for future use\n\n\tref, err := m.repo.Reference(m.refName(id, RefSuffixCompleted), true)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn plumbing.ZeroHash, nil //nolint:nilerr // No ref means not completed\n\t}\n\treturn ref.Hash(), nil\n}\n\n// GetFailedRef returns the commit hash that the failed ref points to.\n// Returns plumbing.ZeroHash if not failed.\nfunc (m *StateManager) GetFailedRef(ctx context.Context, id TrailID) (plumbing.Hash, error) {\n\t_ = ctx // Reserved for future use\n\n\tref, err := m.repo.Reference(m.refName(id, RefSuffixFailed), true)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn plumbing.ZeroHash, nil //nolint:nilerr // No ref means not failed\n\t}\n\treturn ref.Hash(), nil\n}\n\n// refName returns the full reference name for a trail state.\nfunc (m *StateManager) refName(id TrailID, suffix string) plumbing.ReferenceName {\n\treturn plumbing.ReferenceName(RefPrefix + id.String() + suffix)\n}\n\n// refExists checks if a ref exists for the given trail and suffix.\nfunc (m *StateManager) refExists(id TrailID, suffix string) bool {\n\t_, err := m.repo.Reference(m.refName(id, suffix), true)\n\treturn err == nil\n}\n"
}

  "content": "package trail\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"sort\"\n\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5\"\n)\n\n// Discovery provides methods to discover and filter trails.\ntype Discovery struct {\n\tstore *Store\n\tstate *StateManager\n}\n\n// NewDiscovery creates a new discovery instance.\nfunc NewDiscovery(repo *git.Repository) *Discovery {\n\treturn &Discovery{\n\t\tstore: NewStore(repo),\n\t\tstate: NewStateManager(repo),\n\t}\n}\n\n// ListFilter specifies filtering options for listing trails.\ntype ListFilter struct {\n\t// States filters to trails matching any of these states.\n\t// If empty, returns all trails.\n\tStates []TrailState\n\n\t// Labels filters to trails having all of these labels.\n\tLabels []string\n\n\t// Assignees filters to trails assigned to any of these users.\n\tAssignees []string\n}\n\n// ListWithState returns all trails with their current execution state.\nfunc (d *Discovery) ListWithState(ctx context.Context, filter *ListFilter) ([]TrailWithState, error) {\n\ttrails, err := d.store.List(ctx)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\n\tvar result []TrailWithState\n\n\tfor _, trail := range trails {\n\t\tstate, err := d.state.GetState(ctx, trail.ID)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t// Skip trails we can't get state for\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Apply state filter\n\t\tif filter != nil && len(filter.States) > 0 {\n\t\t\tif !containsState(filter.States, state) {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Apply label filter (AND logic - must have all labels)\n\t\tif filter != nil && len(filter.Labels) > 0 {\n\t\t\tif !hasAllLabels(trail.Labels, filter.Labels) {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Apply assignee filter (OR logic - any assignee matches)\n\t\tif filter != nil && len(filter.Assignees) > 0 {\n\t\t\tif !hasAnyAssignee(trail.Assignees, filter.Assignees) {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tresult = append(result, TrailWithState{\n\t\t\tTrail: *trail,\n\t\t\tState: state,\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\n\t// Sort by created date (newest first)\n\tsort.Slice(result, func(i, j int) bool {\n\t\treturn result[i].CreatedAt.After(result[j].CreatedAt)\n\t})\n\n\treturn result, nil\n}\n\n// FindOpen returns trails that are available for execution.\nfunc (d *Discovery) FindOpen(ctx context.Context) ([]TrailWithState, error) {\n\treturn d.ListWithState(ctx, &ListFilter{\n\t\tStates: []TrailState{TrailStateOpen},\n\t})\n}\n\n// FindInProgress returns trails that are currently being executed.\nfunc (d *Discovery) FindInProgress(ctx context.Context) ([]TrailWithState, error) {\n\treturn d.ListWithState(ctx, &ListFilter{\n\t\tStates: []TrailState{TrailStateInProgress},\n\t})\n}\n\n// FindCompleted returns trails that have been successfully completed.\nfunc (d *Discovery) FindCompleted(ctx context.Context) ([]TrailWithState, error) {\n\treturn d.ListWithState(ctx, &ListFilter{\n\t\tStates: []TrailState{TrailStateCompleted},\n\t})\n}\n\n// FindFailed returns trails that failed during execution.\nfunc (d *Discovery) FindFailed(ctx context.Context) ([]TrailWithState, error) {\n\treturn d.ListWithState(ctx, &ListFilter{\n\t\tStates: []TrailState{TrailStateFailed},\n\t})\n}\n\n// GetWithState retrieves a single trail with its current state.\nfunc (d *Discovery) GetWithState(ctx context.Context, id TrailID) (*TrailWithState, error) {\n\ttrail, err := d.store.Get(ctx, id)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tif trail == nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // Trail not found\n\t}\n\n\tstate, err := d.state.GetState(ctx, trail.ID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\n\treturn &TrailWithState{\n\t\tTrail: *trail,\n\t\tState: state,\n\t}, nil\n}\n\n// containsState checks if a state is in the list of states.\nfunc containsState(states []TrailState, state TrailState) bool {\n\tfor _, s := range states {\n\t\tif s == state {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\n// hasAllLabels checks if the trail has all the required labels.\nfunc hasAllLabels(trailLabels, requiredLabels []string) bool {\n\tlabelSet := make(map[string]bool)\n\tfor _, l := range trailLabels {\n\t\tlabelSet[l] = true\n\t}\n\tfor _, l := range requiredLabels {\n\t\tif !labelSet[l] {\n\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn true\n}\n\n// hasAnyAssignee checks if the trail has any of the specified assignees.\nfunc hasAnyAssignee(trailAssignees, filterAssignees []string) bool {\n\tassigneeSet := make(map[string]bool)\n\tfor _, a := range trailAssignees {\n\t\tassigneeSet[a] = true\n\t}\n\tfor _, a := range filterAssignees {\n\t\tif assigneeSet[a] {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n"
}

{

  "content": "package trail\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"os/exec\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths\"\n)\n\n// WorktreeDir is the directory where trail worktrees are created.\nconst WorktreeDir = \".worktrees\"\n\n// WorktreeManager manages git worktrees for trail execution.\ntype WorktreeManager struct {\n\trepoRoot string\n}\n\n// NewWorktreeManager creates a new worktree manager.\n// The repoRoot should be the path to the main git repository.\nfunc NewWorktreeManager(repoRoot string) *WorktreeManager {\n\treturn &WorktreeManager{repoRoot: repoRoot}\n}\n\n// WorktreePath returns the path where a trail's worktree should be created.\nfunc (m *WorktreeManager) WorktreePath(id TrailID) string {\n\treturn filepath.Join(m.repoRoot, WorktreeDir, \"trail-\"+id.String())\n}\n\n// Create creates a new git worktree for a trail.\n// If the branch doesn't exist, it will be created from baseBranch.\nfunc (m *WorktreeManager) Create(ctx context.Context, id TrailID, branch, baseBranch string) (string, error) {\n\tworktreePath := m.WorktreePath(id)\n\n\t// Ensure the worktrees directory exists\n\tworktreesDir := filepath.Join(m.repoRoot, WorktreeDir)\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(worktreesDir, 0o755); err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create worktrees directory: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Check if worktree already exists\n\tif _, err := os.Stat(worktreePath); err == nil {\n\t\treturn worktreePath, nil // Already exists\n\t}\n\n\t// Check if the branch exists\n\tbranchExists, err := m.branchExists(ctx, branch)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to check branch existence: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif branchExists {\n\t\t// Worktree for existing branch\n\t\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, \"git\", \"worktree\", \"add\", worktreePath, branch)\n\t\tcmd.Dir = m.repoRoot\n\t\tif output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create worktree: %s: %w\", strings.TrimSpace(string(output)), err)\n\t\t}\n\t} else {\n\t\t// Create new branch from base\n\t\tif baseBranch == \"\" {\n\t\t\t// Get default branch\n\t\t\tbaseBranch, err = m.getDefaultBranch(ctx)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to determine base branch: %w\", err)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Create worktree with new branch\n\t\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, \"git\", \"worktree\", \"add\", \"-b\", branch, worktreePath, baseBranch)\n\t\tcmd.Dir = m.repoRoot\n\t\tif output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create worktree with new branch: %s: %w\", strings.TrimSpace(string(output)), err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn worktreePath, nil\n}\n\n// Remove removes a trail's worktree.\nfunc (m *WorktreeManager) Remove(ctx context.Context, id TrailID) error {\n\tworktreePath := m.WorktreePath(id)\n\n\t// Check if worktree exists\n\tif _, err := os.Stat(worktreePath); os.IsNotExist(err) {\n\t\treturn nil // Already removed\n\t}\n\n\t// Remove the worktree\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, \"git\", \"worktree\", \"remove\", \"--force\", worktreePath)\n\tcmd.Dir = m.repoRoot\n\tif output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to remove worktree: %s: %w\", strings.TrimSpace(string(output)), err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// Exists checks if a worktree exists for the given trail.\nfunc (m *WorktreeManager) Exists(id TrailID) bool {\n\tworktreePath := m.WorktreePath(id)\n\t_, err := os.Stat(worktreePath)\n\treturn err == nil\n}\n\n// GetCurrentBranch returns the current branch name in a worktree.\nfunc (m *WorktreeManager) GetCurrentBranch(ctx context.Context, id TrailID) (string, error) {\n\tworktreePath := m.WorktreePath(id)\n\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, \"git\", \"rev-parse\", \"--abbrev-ref\", \"HEAD\")\n\tcmd.Dir = worktreePath\n\toutput, err := cmd.Output()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get current branch: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn strings.TrimSpace(string(output)), nil\n}\n\n// GetHeadCommit returns the HEAD commit hash in a worktree.\nfunc (m *WorktreeManager) GetHeadCommit(ctx context.Context, id TrailID) (string, error) {\n\tworktreePath := m.WorktreePath(id)\n\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, \"git\", \"rev-parse\", \"HEAD\")\n\tcmd.Dir = worktreePath\n\toutput, err := cmd.Output()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get HEAD commit: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn strings.TrimSpace(string(output)), nil\n}\n\n// branchExists checks if a branch exists in the repository.\nfunc (m *WorktreeManager) branchExists(ctx context.Context, branch string) (bool, error) {\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, \"git\", \"rev-parse\", \"--verify\", \"refs/heads/\"+branch)\n\tcmd.Dir = m.repoRoot\n\tif err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {\n\t\t// Branch doesn't exist\n\t\treturn false, nil\n\t}\n\treturn true, nil\n}\n\n// getDefaultBranch returns the repository's default branch name.\nfunc (m *WorktreeManager) getDefaultBranch(ctx context.Context) (string, error) {\n\t// Try to get the default branch from origin\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, \"git\", \"symbolic-ref\", \"refs/remotes/origin/HEAD\")\n\tcmd.Dir = m.repoRoot\n\tif output, err := cmd.Output(); err == nil {\n\t\tref := strings.TrimSpace(string(output))\n\t\t// Extract branch name from refs/remotes/origin/main\n\t\tparts := strings.Split(ref, \"/\")\n\t\tif len(parts) > 0 {\n\t\t\treturn parts[len(parts)-1], nil\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Fall back to checking common default branch names\n\tfor _, branch := range []string{\"main\", \"master\"} {\n\t\texists, err := m.branchExists(ctx, branch)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif exists {\n\t\t\treturn branch, nil\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"could not determine default branch\")\n}\n\n// List returns all trail worktrees.\nfunc (m *WorktreeManager) List() ([]string, error) {\n\tworktreesDir := filepath.Join(m.repoRoot, WorktreeDir)\n\n\tentries, err := os.ReadDir(worktreesDir)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif os.IsNotExist(err) {\n\t\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read worktrees directory: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tvar worktrees []string\n\tfor _, entry := range entries {\n\t\tif entry.IsDir() && strings.HasPrefix(entry.Name(), \"trail-\") {\n\t\t\tworktrees = append(worktrees, filepath.Join(worktreesDir, entry.Name()))\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn worktrees, nil\n}\n\n// EnsureWorktreesIgnored ensures the .worktrees directory is in .gitignore.\nfunc EnsureWorktreesIgnored(repoRoot string) error {\n\tgitignorePath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, \".gitignore\")\n\n\t// Read existing .gitignore\n\tcontent, err := os.ReadFile(gitignorePath)\n\tif err != nil && !os.IsNotExist(err) {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read .gitignore: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Check if already ignored\n\tlines := strings.Split(string(content), \"\\n\")\n\tfor _, line := range lines {\n\t\ttrimmed := strings.TrimSpace(line)\n\t\tif trimmed == WorktreeDir || trimmed == WorktreeDir+\"/\" {\n\t\t\treturn nil // Already ignored\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Add to .gitignore\n\tvar newContent string\n\tif len(content) > 0 && !strings.HasSuffix(string(content), \"\\n\") {\n\t\tnewContent = string(content) + \"\\n\" + WorktreeDir + \"/\\n\"\n\t} else {\n\t\tnewContent = string(content) + WorktreeDir + \"/\\n\"\n\t}\n\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(gitignorePath, []byte(newContent), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to update .gitignore: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// GetRepoRoot returns the repository root, using the paths package.\nfunc GetRepoRoot() (string, error) {\n\treturn paths.RepoRoot()\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/trail/runner.go",
  "content": "package trail\n\nimport (\n\t\"bytes\"\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"errors\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"log/slog\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"os/exec\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging\"\n\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing\"\n)\n\n// RunnerConfig holds configuration for the trail runner.\ntype RunnerConfig struct {\n\t// PollInterval is how often to check for new trails in daemon mode.\n\tPollInterval time.Duration\n\n\t// MaxConcurrent is the maximum number of trails to run concurrently.\n\t// Currently only 1 is supported.\n\tMaxConcurrent int\n\n\t// Daemon enables continuous polling mode.\n\tDaemon bool\n\n\t// Agent is the agent CLI to use (e.g., \"claude\").\n\tAgent string\n\n\t// Model is the optional model override for the agent.\n\tModel string\n\n\t// Timeout is the maximum time to wait for a single trail execution.\n\tTimeout time.Duration\n\n\t// DryRun prevents actual execution, just prints what would be done.\n\tDryRun bool\n}\n\n// DefaultRunnerConfig returns the default runner configuration.\nfunc DefaultRunnerConfig() RunnerConfig {\n\treturn RunnerConfig{\n\t\tPollInterval:  30 * time.Second,\n\t\tMaxConcurrent: 1,\n\t\tDaemon:        false,\n\t\tAgent:         \"claude\",\n\t\tModel:         \"\",\n\t\tTimeout:       30 * time.Minute,\n\t\tDryRun:        false,\n\t}\n}\n\n// Runner executes trails by discovering open tasks, claiming them,\n// creating worktrees, running agents, and updating state.\ntype Runner struct {\n\trepo      *git.Repository\n\trepoRoot  string\n\tstore     *Store\n\tstate     *StateManager\n\tdiscovery *Discovery\n\tworktree  *WorktreeManager\n\tconfig    RunnerConfig\n}\n\n// NewRunner creates a new trail runner.\nfunc NewRunner(repo *git.Repository, repoRoot string, config RunnerConfig) *Runner {\n\treturn &Runner{\n\t\trepo:      repo,\n\t\trepoRoot:  repoRoot,\n\t\tstore:     NewStore(repo),\n\t\tstate:     NewStateManager(repo),\n\t\tdiscovery: NewDiscovery(repo),\n\t\tworktree:  NewWorktreeManager(repoRoot),\n\t\tconfig:    config,\n\t}\n}\n\n// RunResult holds the result of running a single trail.\ntype RunResult struct {\n\tTrail    *Trail\n\tSuccess  bool\n\tOutput   string\n\tError    error\n\tDuration time.Duration\n}\n\n// Start runs the trail runner in daemon mode, continuously polling for new trails.\nfunc (r *Runner) Start(ctx context.Context) error {\n\tlogging.Info(ctx, \"trail runner started\",\n\t\tslog.Duration(\"poll_interval\", r.config.PollInterval),\n\t\tslog.Bool(\"daemon\", r.config.Daemon),\n\t)\n\n\tfor {\n\t\t// Run one iteration\n\t\tresults, err := r.RunOnce(ctx)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tlogging.Error(ctx, \"runner iteration failed\", slog.String(\"error\", err.Error()))\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tfor _, result := range results {\n\t\t\tif result.Success {\n\t\t\t\tlogging.Info(ctx, \"trail completed successfully\",\n\t\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"trail_id\", result.Trail.ID.String()),\n\t\t\t\t\tslog.Duration(\"duration\", result.Duration),\n\t\t\t\t)\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\tlogging.Error(ctx, \"trail failed\",\n\t\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"trail_id\", result.Trail.ID.String()),\n\t\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"error\", result.Error.Error()),\n\t\t\t\t)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif !r.config.Daemon {\n\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Wait before next poll\n\t\tselect {\n\t\tcase <-ctx.Done():\n\t\t\tlogging.Info(ctx, \"trail runner stopping\")\n\t\t\treturn ctx.Err()\n\t\tcase <-time.After(r.config.PollInterval):\n\t\t\t// Continue to next iteration\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// RunOnce performs a single iteration: find open trails and run them.\nfunc (r *Runner) RunOnce(ctx context.Context) ([]RunResult, error) {\n\t// Find open trails\n\topenTrails, err := r.discovery.FindOpen(ctx)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to find open trails: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif len(openTrails) == 0 {\n\t\tlogging.Debug(ctx, \"no open trails found\")\n\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t}\n\n\tlogging.Info(ctx, \"found open trails\", slog.Int(\"count\", len(openTrails)))\n\n\tvar results []RunResult\n\n\t// Run trails up to max concurrent (currently only 1)\n\tfor i := 0; i < min(len(openTrails), r.config.MaxConcurrent); i++ {\n\t\ttrail := openTrails[i].Trail\n\t\tresult := r.RunTrail(ctx, &trail)\n\t\tresults = append(results, result)\n\t}\n\n\treturn results, nil\n}\n\n// RunTrail executes a single trail.\nfunc (r *Runner) RunTrail(ctx context.Context, trail *Trail) RunResult {\n\tstart := time.Now()\n\tresult := RunResult{Trail: trail}\n\n\tctx = logging.WithComponent(ctx, \"trail-runner\")\n\n\tlogging.Info(ctx, \"starting trail execution\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"trail_id\", trail.ID.String()),\n\t\tslog.String(\"title\", trail.Title),\n\t)\n\n\tif r.config.DryRun {\n\t\tlogging.Info(ctx, \"dry run - skipping execution\")\n\t\tresult.Success = true\n\t\tresult.Output = \"dry run\"\n\t\tresult.Duration = time.Since(start)\n\t\treturn result\n\t}\n\n\t// Create worktree and get initial branch head\n\tworktreePath, branchHead, err := r.setupWorktree(ctx, trail)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tresult.Error = fmt.Errorf(\"failed to setup worktree: %w\", err)\n\t\tresult.Duration = time.Since(start)\n\t\treturn result\n\t}\n\n\t// Claim the trail\n\tif err := r.state.Claim(ctx, trail.ID, branchHead); err != nil {\n\t\tif errors.Is(err, ErrAlreadyClaimed) {\n\t\t\tlogging.Info(ctx, \"trail already claimed, skipping\",\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"trail_id\", trail.ID.String()),\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t}\n\t\tresult.Error = err\n\t\tresult.Duration = time.Since(start)\n\t\t// Clean up worktree if we couldn't claim\n\t\t_ = r.worktree.Remove(ctx, trail.ID)\n\t\treturn result\n\t}\n\n\t// Run the agent\n\toutput, agentErr := r.runAgent(ctx, trail, worktreePath)\n\tresult.Output = output\n\n\t// Get final branch head\n\tfinalHead, _ := r.worktree.GetHeadCommit(ctx, trail.ID)\n\tfinalHeadHash := plumbing.NewHash(finalHead)\n\tif finalHeadHash.IsZero() {\n\t\tfinalHeadHash = branchHead\n\t}\n\n\t// Update state based on result\n\tif agentErr != nil {\n\t\tresult.Error = agentErr\n\t\tresult.Success = false\n\t\tif err := r.state.Fail(ctx, trail.ID, finalHeadHash); err != nil {\n\t\t\tlogging.Error(ctx, \"failed to mark trail as failed\",\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"trail_id\", trail.ID.String()),\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"error\", err.Error()),\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t}\n\t} else {\n\t\tresult.Success = true\n\t\tif err := r.state.Complete(ctx, trail.ID, finalHeadHash); err != nil {\n\t\t\tlogging.Error(ctx, \"failed to mark trail as completed\",\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"trail_id\", trail.ID.String()),\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"error\", err.Error()),\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Clean up worktree\n\tif err := r.worktree.Remove(ctx, trail.ID); err != nil {\n\t\tlogging.Warn(ctx, \"failed to remove worktree\",\n\t\t\tslog.String(\"trail_id\", trail.ID.String()),\n\t\t\tslog.String(\"error\", err.Error()),\n\t\t)\n\t}\n\n\tresult.Duration = time.Since(start)\n\n\tlogging.Info(ctx, \"trail execution finished\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"trail_id\", trail.ID.String()),\n\t\tslog.Bool(\"success\", result.Success),\n\t\tslog.Duration(\"duration\", result.Duration),\n\t)\n\n\treturn result\n}\n\n// setupWorktree creates a worktree for the trail and returns the path and branch head.\nfunc (r *Runner) setupWorktree(ctx context.Context, trail *Trail) (string, plumbing.Hash, error) {\n\tbranch := trail.GetBranch()\n\n\t// Ensure .worktrees is in .gitignore\n\tif err := EnsureWorktreesIgnored(r.repoRoot); err != nil {\n\t\tlogging.Warn(ctx, \"failed to ensure worktrees ignored\",\n\t\t\tslog.String(\"error\", err.Error()),\n\t\t)\n\t}\n\n\t// Create the worktree\n\tworktreePath, err := r.worktree.Create(ctx, trail.ID, branch, trail.BaseBranch)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", plumbing.ZeroHash, err\n\t}\n\n\t// Get the branch head\n\theadCommit, err := r.worktree.GetHeadCommit(ctx, trail.ID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get branch head: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn worktreePath, plumbing.NewHash(headCommit), nil\n}\n\n// runAgent executes the agent CLI with the trail description.\nfunc (r *Runner) runAgent(ctx context.Context, trail *Trail, worktreePath string) (string, error) {\n\t// Build the prompt from trail description\n\tprompt := trail.Description\n\n\t// Build command arguments\n\targs := []string{}\n\n\t// Add model if specified\n\tif r.config.Model != \"\" {\n\t\targs = append(args, \"--model\", r.config.Model)\n\t}\n\n\t// Add prompt\n\targs = append(args, \"-p\", prompt)\n\n\t// Create context with timeout\n\tif r.config.Timeout > 0 {\n\t\tvar cancel context.CancelFunc\n\t\tctx, cancel = context.WithTimeout(ctx, r.config.Timeout)\n\t\tdefer cancel()\n\t}\n\n\tlogging.Debug(ctx, \"running agent\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"agent\", r.config.Agent),\n\t\tslog.String(\"worktree\", worktreePath),\n\t)\n\n\t//nolint:gosec // args are constructed from trusted config\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, r.config.Agent, args...)\n\tcmd.Dir = worktreePath\n\tcmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),\n\t\t// Ensure entire hooks are enabled in the worktree\n\t\t\"ENTIRE_ENABLED=1\",\n\t)\n\n\tvar stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer\n\tcmd.Stdout = &stdout\n\tcmd.Stderr = &stderr\n\n\terr := cmd.Run()\n\n\toutput := stdout.String()\n\tif stderr.Len() > 0 {\n\t\tif output != \"\" {\n\t\t\toutput += \"\\n\"\n\t\t}\n\t\toutput += stderr.String()\n\t}\n\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif ctx.Err() == context.DeadlineExceeded {\n\t\t\treturn output, fmt.Errorf(\"agent timed out after %s\", r.config.Timeout)\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn output, fmt.Errorf(\"agent failed: %s: %w\", strings.TrimSpace(stderr.String()), err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn output, nil\n}\n\n// RunTrailByID executes a trail by its ID.\nfunc (r *Runner) RunTrailByID(ctx context.Context, id TrailID) (RunResult, error) {\n\ttrail, err := r.store.Get(ctx, id)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn RunResult{}, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get trail: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tif trail == nil {\n\t\treturn RunResult{}, fmt.Errorf(\"trail not found: %s\", id)\n\t}\n\n\treturn r.RunTrail(ctx, trail), nil\n}\n"
}

     1→package trail
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"sort"
     6→
     7→	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5"
     8→)
     9→
    10→// Discovery provides methods to discover and filter trails.
    11→type Discovery struct {
    12→	store *Store
    13→	state *StateManager
    14→}
    15→
    16→// NewDiscovery creates a new discovery instance.
    17→func NewDiscovery(repo *git.Repository) *Discovery {
    18→	return &Discovery{
    19→		store: NewStore(repo),
    20→		state: NewStateManager(repo),
    21→	}
    22→}
    23→
    24→// ListFilter specifies filtering options for listing trails.
    25→type ListFilter struct {
    26→	// States filters to trails matching any of these states.
    27→	// If empty, returns all trails.
    28→	States []TrailState
    29→
    30→	// Labels filters to trails having all of these labels.
    31→	Labels []string
    32→
    33→	// Assignees filters to trails assigned to any of these users.
    34→	Assignees []string
    35→}
    36→
    37→// ListWithState returns all trails with their current execution state.
    38→func (d *Discovery) ListWithState(ctx context.Context, filter *ListFilter) ([]TrailWithState, error) {
    39→	trails, err := d.store.List(ctx)
    40→	if err != nil {
    41→		return nil, err
    42→	}
    43→
    44→	var result []TrailWithState
    45→
    46→	for _, trail := range trails {
    47→		state, err := d.state.GetState(ctx, trail.ID)
    48→		if err != nil {
    49→			// Skip trails we can't get state for
    50→			continue
    51→		}
    52→
    53→		// Apply state filter
    54→		if filter != nil && len(filter.States) > 0 {
    55→			if !containsState(filter.States, state) {
    56→				continue
    57→			}
    58→		}
    59→
    60→		// Apply label filter (AND logic - must have all labels)
    61→		if filter != nil && len(filter.Labels) > 0 {
    62→			if !hasAllLabels(trail.Labels, filter.Labels) {
    63→				continue
    64→			}
    65→		}
    66→
    67→		// Apply assignee filter (OR logic - any assignee matches)
    68→		if filter != nil && len(filter.Assignees) > 0 {
    69→			if !hasAnyAssignee(trail.Assignees, filter.Assignees) {
    70→				continue
    71→			}
    72→		}
    73→
    74→		result = append(result, TrailWithState{
    75→			Trail: *trail,
    76→			State: state,
    77→		})
    78→	}
    79→
    80→	// Sort by created date (newest first)
    81→	sort.Slice(result, func(i, j int) bool {
    82→		return result[i].CreatedAt.After(result[j].CreatedAt)
    83→	})
    84→
    85→	return result, nil
    86→}
    87→
    88→// FindOpen returns trails that are available for execution.
    89→func (d *Discovery) FindOpen(ctx context.Context) ([]TrailWithState, error) {
    90→	return d.ListWithState(ctx, &ListFilter{
    91→		States: []TrailState{TrailStateOpen},
    92→	})
    93→}
    94→
    95→// FindInProgress returns trails that are currently being executed.
    96→func (d *Discovery) FindInProgress(ctx context.Context) ([]TrailWithState, error) {
    97→	return d.ListWithState(ctx, &ListFilter{
    98→		States: []TrailState{TrailStateInProgress},
    99→	})
   100→}
   101→
   102→// FindCompleted returns trails that have been successfully completed.
   103→func (d *Discovery) FindCompleted(ctx context.Context) ([]TrailWithState, error) {
   104→	return d.ListWithState(ctx, &ListFilter{
   105→		States: []TrailState{TrailStateCompleted},
   106→	})
   107→}
   108→
   109→// FindFailed returns trails that failed during execution.
   110→func (d *Discovery) FindFailed(ctx context.Context) ([]TrailWithState, error) {
   111→	return d.ListWithState(ctx, &ListFilter{
   112→		States: []TrailState{TrailStateFailed},
   113→	})
   114→}
   115→
   116→// GetWithState retrieves a single trail with its current state.
   117→func (d *Discovery) GetWithState(ctx context.Context, id TrailID) (*TrailWithState, error) {
   118→	trail, err := d.store.Get(ctx, id)
   119→	if err != nil {
   120→		return nil, err
   121→	}
   122→	if trail == nil {
   123→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // Trail not found
   124→	}
   125→
   126→	state, err := d.state.GetState(ctx, trail.ID)
   127→	if err != nil {
   128→		return nil, err
   129→	}
   130→
   131→	return &TrailWithState{
   132→		Trail: *trail,
   133→		State: state,
   134→	}, nil
   135→}
   136→
   137→// containsState checks if a state is in the list of states.
   138→func containsState(states []TrailState, state TrailState) bool {
   139→	for _, s := range states {
   140→		if s == state {
   141→			return true
   142→		}
   143→	}
   144→	return false
   145→}
   146→
   147→// hasAllLabels checks if the trail has all the required labels.
   148→func hasAllLabels(trailLabels, requiredLabels []string) bool {
   149→	labelSet := make(map[string]bool)
   150→	for _, l := range trailLabels {
   151→		labelSet[l] = true
   152→	}
   153→	for _, l := range requiredLabels {
   154→		if !labelSet[l] {
   155→			return false
   156→		}
   157→	}
   158→	return true
   159→}
   160→
   161→// hasAnyAssignee checks if the trail has any of the specified assignees.
   162→func hasAnyAssignee(trailAssignees, filterAssignees []string) bool {
   163→	assigneeSet := make(map[string]bool)
   164→	for _, a := range trailAssignees {
   165→		assigneeSet[a] = true
   166→	}
   167→	for _, a := range filterAssignees {
   168→		if assigneeSet[a] {
   169→			return true
   170→		}
   171→	}
   172→	return false
   173→}

\"Implement login form\"\n  entire trail create \"Fix bug #123\" --branch fix/bug-123\n  entire trail create \"Add tests\" --base main --labels urgent,tests`,\n\t\tRunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args labels)\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tcmd.Flags().StringVar(&branch, \"branch\", \"\", \"Branch name for this trail (default: trail/<id>)\")\n\tcmd.Flags().StringVar(&baseBranch, \"base\", \"\", \"Base branch to create from (default: main)\")\n\tcmd.Flags().StringSliceVar(&labels, \"labels\", nil, \"Labels for categorization\")\n\n\treturn cmd\n}\n\nfunc newTrailDeleteCmd() *cobra.Command {\n\tvar force bool\n\n\tcmd := &cobra.Command{\n\t\tUse:   \"delete <trail-id>\",\n\t\tShort: \"Delete a trail\",\n\t\tArgs:  cobra.ExactArgs(1),\n\t\tRunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error *cobra.Command {\n\tcmd := &cobra.Command{\n\t\tUse:   \"reset <trail-id>\",\n\t\tShort: \"Reset a trail's execution state (return to open)\",\n\t\tLong: `Reset a trail's execution state, allowing it to be re-executed.\n\nThis removes all state refs (claimed, completed, failed) for the trail,\nreturning it to the 'open' state. cmd\n}\n\nfunc runTrailList(cmd *cobra.Command, statusFilter string, jsonOutput bool) error {\n\trepo, err := openRepository()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to open repository: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tdiscovery := trail.NewDiscovery(repo)\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\n\t// Build filter\n\tvar filter *trail.ListFilter\n\tif statusFilter != \"\" {\n\t\tstate, err := {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"No trails found.\")\n\t\tfmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"\\nCreate a trail with: entire trail create \\\"Title\\\"\")\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Print table\n\tfmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"ID          STATUS        BRANCH                    TITLE\")\n\tfmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), strings.Repeat(\"-\", 80))\n\n\tfor _, t := range trails {\n\t\tstatus := formatTrailState(t.State)\n\t\tbranch := t.GetBranch()\n\t\tif len(branch) > 24 {\n\t\t\tbranch = branch[:21] + \"...\"\n\t\t}\n\t\ttitle := t.Title\n\t\tif len(title) > 30 {\n\t\t\ttitle = title[:27] + \"...\"\n\t\t}\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"%-11s %-13s %-25s %s\\n\",\n\t\t\tt.ID.Short(), status, branch, title)\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc runTrailShow(cmd *cobra.Command, idStr string, jsonOutput bool) error {\n\tid, err := trail.NewTrailID(idStr)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err //nolint:wrapcheck // validation error\n\t}\n\n\trepo, err := openRepository()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to open repository: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tdiscovery := trail.NewDiscovery(repo)\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\n\tt, err := discovery.GetWithState(ctx, id)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get trail: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tif t == nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"trail not found: %s\", id)\n\t}\n\n\tif jsonOutput {\n\t\tdata, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(t, \"\", \"  \")\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err //nolint:wrapcheck // simple pass-through\n\t\t}\n\t\tfmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), string(data))\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Print details\n\tfmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"Trail: %s\\n\", t.ID)\n\tfmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"Title: %s\\n\", t.Title)\n\tfmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"State: %s\\n\", formatTrailState(t.State))\n\tfmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"Branch: %s\\n\", t.GetBranch())\n\tif t.BaseBranch != \"\" {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"Base Branch: %s\\n\", t.BaseBranch)\n\t}\n\tfmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"Created: %s\\n\", t.CreatedAt.Format(time.RFC3339))\n\tfmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"Updated: %s\\n\", t.UpdatedAt.Format(time.RFC3339))\n\n\tif len(t.Labels) > 0 {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"Labels: %s\\n\", strings.Join(t.Labels, \", \"))\n\t}\n\tif len(t.Assignees) > 0 {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"Assignees: %s\\n\", strings.Join(t.Assignees, \", \"))\n\t}\n\n\tfmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"\\nDescription:\\n%s\\n\", t.Description)\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc runTrailCreate(cmd *cobra.Command, title, branch, baseBranch string, labels []string) error {\n\tvar description string\n\n\t// Interactive mode if no title provided\n\tif title == \"\" {\n\t\tvar err error\n\t\ttitle, description, branch, baseBranch, err = runTrailCreateInteractive()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t} else {\n\t\t// Get description interactively\n\t\tform := NewAccessibleForm(\n\t\t\thuh.NewGroup(\n\t\t\t\thuh.NewText().\n\t\t\t\t\tTitle(\"Description\").\n\t\t\t\t\tDescription(\"Describe the task for the agent\").\n\t\t\t\t\tValue(&description).\n\t\t\t\t\tCharLimit(4000),\n\t\t\t),\n\t\t)\n\t\tif err := form.Run(); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"cancelled: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif description == \"\" {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"description is required\")\n\t}\n\n\t// Generate trail ID\n\tid, err := trail.GenerateTrailID()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to generate trail ID: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Create trail\n\tt := &trail.Trail{\n\t\tID:          id,\n\t\tTitle:       title,\n\t\tDescription: description,\n\t\tBranch:      branch,\n\t\tBaseBranch:  baseBranch,\n\t\tLabels:      labels,\n\t}\n\n\trepo, err := openRepository()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to open repository: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tstore := trail.NewStore(repo)\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\n\tif err := store.Create(ctx, t); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create trail: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tfmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"Created trail: %s\\n\", id)\n\tfmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"Branch: %s\\n\", t.GetBranch())\n\tfmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"\\nRun the trail with: entire trail run %s\\n\", id)\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc runTrailCreateInteractive() (title, description, branch, baseBranch string, err error) {\n\tform := NewAccessibleForm(\n\t\thuh.NewGroup(\n\t\t\thuh.NewInput().\n\t\t\t\tTitle(\"Title\").\n\t\t\t\tDescription(\"Brief title for the task\").\n\t\t\t\tValue(&title),\n\t\t\thuh.NewText().\n\t\t\t\tTitle(\"Description\").\n\t\t\t\tDescription(\"Describe the task for the agent\").\n\t\t\t\tValue(&description).\n\t\t\t\tCharLimit(4000),\n\t\t\thuh.NewInput().\n\t\t\t\tTitle(\"Branch\").\n\t\t\t\tDescription(\"Branch name (leave empty for default)\").\n\t\t\t\tValue(&branch),\n\t\t\thuh.NewInput().\n\t\t\t\tTitle(\"Base Branch\").\n\t\t\t\tDescription(\"Base branch to create from (leave empty for main)\").\n\t\t\t\tValue(&baseBranch),\n\t\t),\n\t)\n\n\tif err := form.Run(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\", \"\", \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"cancelled: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif title == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\", \"\", \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"title is required\")\n\t}\n\n\treturn title, description, branch, baseBranch, nil\n}\n\nfunc runTrailDelete(cmd *cobra.Command, idStr string, force bool) error {\n\tid, err := trail.NewTrailID(idStr)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err //nolint:wrapcheck // validation error\n\t}\n\n\trepo, err := openRepository()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to open repository: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tstore := trail.NewStore(repo)\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\n\t// Check if trail exists\n\tt, err := store.Get(ctx, id)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get trail: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tif t == nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"trail not found: %s\", id)\n\t}\n\n\t// Confirm deletion\n\tif !force {\n\t\tvar confirm bool\n\t\tform := NewAccessibleForm(\n\t\t\thuh.NewGroup(\n\t\t\t\thuh.NewConfirm().\n\t\t\t\t\tTitle(fmt.Sprintf(\"Delete trail %s?\", id.Short())).\n\t\t\t\t\tDescription(t.Title).\n\t\t\t\t\tValue(&confirm),\n\t\t\t),\n\t\t)\n\t\tif err := form.Run(); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"cancelled: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif !confirm {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"Cancelled.\")\n\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Also reset state refs\n\tstate := trail.NewStateManager(repo)\n\tif err := state.Reset(ctx, id); err != nil {\n\t\t// Non-fatal, continue with deletion\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(cmd.ErrOrStderr(), \"Warning: failed to reset state refs: %v\\n\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif err := store.Delete(ctx, id); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to delete trail: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tfmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"Deleted trail: %s\\n\", id)\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc runTrailReset(cmd *cobra.Command, idStr string) error {\n\tid, err := trail.NewTrailID(idStr)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err //nolint:wrapcheck // validation error\n\t}\n\n\trepo, err := openRepository()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to open repository: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tstate := trail.NewStateManager(repo)\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\n\tif err := state.Reset(ctx, id); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to reset trail state: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tfmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"Reset trail: %s (now open)\\n\", id)\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc parseTrailState(s string) (trail.TrailState, error) {\n\tswitch strings.ToLower(s) {\n\tcase \"open\":\n\t\treturn trail.TrailStateOpen, nil\n\tcase \"in_progress\", \"inprogress\", \"claimed\":\n\t\treturn trail.TrailStateInProgress, nil\n\tcase \"completed\", \"done\":\n\t\treturn trail.TrailStateCompleted, nil\n\tcase \"failed\", \"error\":\n\t\treturn trail.TrailStateFailed, nil\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"invalid status: %s (valid: open, in_progress, completed, failed)\", s)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc formatTrailState(state trail.TrailState) string {\n\tswitch state {\n\tcase trail.TrailStateOpen:\n\t\treturn \"open\"\n\tcase trail.TrailStateInProgress:\n\t\treturn \"in_progress\"\n\tcase trail.TrailStateCompleted:\n\t\treturn \"completed\"\n\tcase trail.TrailStateFailed:\n\t\treturn \"failed\"\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn string(state)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc openRepository() (*git.Repository, error) {\n\trepoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"not in a git repository: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn git.PlainOpen(repoRoot)\n}\n"
}

--daemon --poll-interval 30s\n\n  # Run a specific trail\n  entire trail run abc123def456`,\n\t\tRunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {\n\t\t\treturn      bool\n\t)\n\n\tcmd := &cobra.Command{\n\t\tUse:   \"start\",\n\t\tShort: \"Start the trail runner\",\n\t\tLong: `Start the trail runner to process open trails.\n\nIn daemon mode, the runner continuously polls for new trails.\nWithout daemon mode, it processes available trails once and exits.`,\n\t\tRunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {\n\t\t\treturn runTrailRunnerStart(cmd, daemon, pollInterval, agent, model, timeout, dryRun)\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tcmd.Flags().BoolVar(&daemon, \"daemon\", false, \"Run in daemon mode (continuous polling)\")\n\tcmd.Flags().DurationVar(&pollInterval, \"poll-interval\", 30*time.Second, \"Polling interval in daemon mode\")\n\tcmd.Flags().StringVar(&agent, \"agent\", \"claude\", \"Agent CLI to use\")\n\tcmd.Flags().StringVar(&model, \"model\", \"\", \"Model override for the agent\")\n\tcmd.Flags().DurationVar(&timeout, \"timeout\", 30*time.Minute, \"Timeout per trail `Process available open trails once and exit.`,\n\t\tRunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {\n\t\t\treturn runTrailRunnerRunOnce(cmd, agent, model, timeout, dryRun)\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tcmd.Flags().StringVar(&agent, \"agent\", \"claude\", \"Agent CLI to use\")\n\tcmd.Flags().StringVar(&model, \"model\", \"\", \"Model override for the executing\")\n\n\treturn cmd\n}\n\nfunc newTrailRunCmd() *cobra.Command {\n\tvar (\n\t\tagent   string\n\t\tmodel   string\n\t\ttimeout time.Duration\n\t\tdryRun  bool\n\t)\n\n\tcmd := &cobra.Command{\n\t\tUse:   \"run <trail-id>\",\n\t\tShort: \"Run a specific trail\",\n\t\tLong: `Run a specific trail by ID.\n\nThis command claims the trail, creates a worktree, runs the agent,\nand agent\")\n\tcmd.Flags().DurationVar(&timeout, \"timeout\", 30*time.Minute, \"Timeout per trail execution\")\n\tcmd.Flags().BoolVar(&dryRun, \"dry-run\", false, \"Print what would be done without executing\")\n\n\treturn cmd\n}\n\nfunc runTrailRunnerStart(cmd *cobra.Command, daemon bool, pollInterval time.Duration, agent, model string, timeout time.Duration, dryRun bool) error {\n\trepo, err := openRepository()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to open repository: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\trepoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get repository root: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tconfig := trail.RunnerConfig{\n\t\tPollInterval:  pollInterval,\n\t\tMaxConcurrent: 1,\n\t\tDaemon:        daemon,\n\t\tAgent:         agent,\n\t\tModel:         model,\n\t\tTimeout:       timeout,\n\t\tDryRun:        dryRun,\n\t}\n\n\trunner := trail.NewRunner(repo, repoRoot, config)\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\n\tif daemon {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"Starting trail runner in daemon mode (poll interval: %s)\\n\", pollInterval)\n\t} else {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"Starting trail runner...\")\n\t}\n\n\tif err := runner.Start(ctx); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"runner failed: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc runTrailRunnerRunOnce(cmd *cobra.Command, agent, model string, timeout time.Duration, dryRun bool) error {\n\trepo, err := openRepository()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to open repository: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\trepoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get repository root: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tconfig := trail.RunnerConfig{\n\t\tMaxConcurrent: 1,\n\t\tDaemon:        false,\n\t\tAgent:         agent,\n\t\tModel:         model,\n\t\tTimeout:       timeout,\n\t\tDryRun:        dryRun,\n\t}\n\n\trunner := trail.NewRunner(repo, repoRoot, config)\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\n\tresults, err := runner.RunOnce(ctx)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"runner failed: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif len(results) == 0 {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"No open trails to run.\")\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, result := range results {\n\t\tif result.Success {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"Trail %s completed successfully (%s)\\n\",\n\t\t\t\tresult.Trail.ID.Short(), result.Duration.Round(time.Second))\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"Trail %s failed: %v\\n\",\n\t\t\t\tresult.Trail.ID.Short(), result.Error)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc runTrailRun(cmd *cobra.Command, idStr, agent, model string, timeout time.Duration, dryRun bool) error {\n\tid, err := trail.NewTrailID(idStr)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err //nolint:wrapcheck // validation error\n\t}\n\n\trepo, err := openRepository()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to open repository: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\trepoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get repository root: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tconfig := trail.RunnerConfig{\n\t\tMaxConcurrent: 1,\n\t\tDaemon:        false,\n\t\tAgent:         agent,\n\t\tModel:         model,\n\t\tTimeout:       timeout,\n\t\tDryRun:        dryRun,\n\t}\n\n\trunner := trail.NewRunner(repo, repoRoot, config)\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\n\tfmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"Running trail: %s\\n\", id)\n\n\tresult, err := runner.RunTrailByID(ctx, id)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to run trail: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif result.Success {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"\\nTrail completed successfully (%s)\\n\",\n\t\t\tresult.Duration.Round(time.Second))\n\t} else {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"\\nTrail failed: %v\\n\", result.Error)\n\t\treturn NewSilentError(result.Error)\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n"

  "content": "package trail\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"testing\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/object\"\n)\n\nfunc TestTrailID_Validation(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\ttests := []struct {\n\t\tname    string\n\t\tid      string\n\t\twantErr bool\n\t}{\n\t\t{\"valid 12 hex chars\", \"a1b2c3d4e5f6\", false},\n\t\t{\"valid all zeros\", \"000000000000\", false},\n\t\t{\"valid all f's\", \"ffffffffffff\", false},\n\t\t{\"too short\", \"a1b2c3d4e5f\", true},\n\t\t{\"too long\", \"a1b2c3d4e5f6a\", true},\n\t\t{\"uppercase hex\", \"A1B2C3D4E5F6\", true},\n\t\t{\"non-hex chars\", \"g1b2c3d4e5f6\", true},\n\t\t{\"empty\", \"\", true},\n\t\t{\"with dashes\", \"a1b2-c3d4-e5f6\", true},\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, tt := range tests {\n\t\tt.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\t\tt.Parallel()\n\t\t\t_, err := NewTrailID(tt.id)\n\t\t\tif (err != nil) != tt.wantErr {\n\t\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"NewTrailID(%q) error = %v, wantErr %v\", tt.id, err, tt.wantErr)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t})\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestTrailID_Generate(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\tid1, err := GenerateTrailID()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"GenerateTrailID() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tid2, err := GenerateTrailID()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"GenerateTrailID() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif id1 == id2 {\n\t\tt.Error(\"GenerateTrailID() generated duplicate IDs\")\n\t}\n\n\tif len(id1) != 12 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"GenerateTrailID() length = %d, want 12\", len(id1))\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestTrailID_Short(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\tid := TrailID(\"a1b2c3d4e5f6\")\n\tif got := id.Short(); got != \"a1b2c3d4\" {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Short() = %q, want %q\", got, \"a1b2c3d4\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestTrail_Validate(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\ttests := []struct {\n\t\tname    string\n\t\ttrail   Trail\n\t\twantErr bool\n\t}{\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tname: \"valid trail\",\n\t\t\ttrail: Trail{\n\t\t\t\tID:          \"a1b2c3d4e5f6\",\n\t\t\t\tTitle:       \"Test Trail\",\n\t\t\t\tDescription: \"Test description\",\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\twantErr: false,\n\t\t},\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tname: \"missing ID\",\n\t\t\ttrail: Trail{\n\t\t\t\tTitle:       \"Test Trail\",\n\t\t\t\tDescription: \"Test description\",\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\twantErr: true,\n\t\t},\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tname: \"missing title\",\n\t\t\ttrail: Trail{\n\t\t\t\tID:          \"a1b2c3d4e5f6\",\n\t\t\t\tDescription: \"Test description\",\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\twantErr: true,\n\t\t},\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tname: \"missing description\",\n\t\t\ttrail: Trail{\n\t\t\t\tID:    \"a1b2c3d4e5f6\",\n\t\t\t\tTitle: \"Test Trail\",\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\twantErr: true,\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, tt := range tests {\n\t\tt.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\t\tt.Parallel()\n\t\t\terr := tt.trail.Validate()\n\t\t\tif (err != nil) != tt.wantErr {\n\t\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"Validate() error = %v, wantErr %v\", err, tt.wantErr)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t})\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestTrail_GetBranch(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\ttests := []struct {\n\t\tname   string\n\t\ttrail  Trail\n\t\twant   string\n\t}{\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tname: \"custom branch\",\n\t\t\ttrail: Trail{\n\t\t\t\tID:     \"a1b2c3d4e5f6\",\n\t\t\t\tBranch: \"feature/custom\",\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\twant: \"feature/custom\",\n\t\t},\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tname: \"default branch\",\n\t\t\ttrail: Trail{\n\t\t\t\tID: \"a1b2c3d4e5f6\",\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\twant: \"trail/a1b2c3d4e5f6\",\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, tt := range tests {\n\t\tt.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\t\tt.Parallel()\n\t\t\tif got := tt.trail.GetBranch(); got != tt.want {\n\t\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"GetBranch() = %q, want %q\", got, tt.want)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t})\n\t}\n}\n\n// Helper to create a test repository\nfunc createTestRepo(t *testing.T) (*git.Repository, string) {\n\tt.Helper()\n\n\ttmpDir, err := os.MkdirTemp(\"\", \"trail-test-*\")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to create temp dir: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tt.Cleanup(func() { os.RemoveAll(tmpDir) })\n\n\trepo, err := git.PlainInit(tmpDir, false)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to init repo: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Create initial commit\n\tworktree, err := repo.Worktree()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to get worktree: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Create a file and commit\n\ttestFile := tmpDir + \"/README.md\"\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte(\"# Test\\n\"), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to write test file: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif _, err := worktree.Add(\"README.md\"); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to add file: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif _, err := worktree.Commit(\"Initial commit\", &git.CommitOptions{\n\t\tAuthor: &object.Signature{\n\t\t\tName:  \"Test\",\n\t\t\tEmail: \"test@example.com\",\n\t\t\tWhen:  time.Now(),\n\t\t},\n\t}); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to commit: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn repo, tmpDir\n}\n\nfunc TestStore_CreateAndGet(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\trepo, _ := createTestRepo(t)\n\tstore := NewStore(repo)\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\n\t// Create a trail\n\tid, err := GenerateTrailID()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"GenerateTrailID() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\ttrail := &Trail{\n\t\tID:          id,\n\t\tTitle:       \"Test Trail\",\n\t\tDescription: \"This is a test trail\",\n\t\tLabels:      []string{\"test\", \"example\"},\n\t}\n\n\tif err := store.Create(ctx, trail); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Create() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Get the trail\n\tgot, err := store.Get(ctx, id)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Get() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif got == nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"Get() returned nil\")\n\t}\n\n\tif got.ID != id {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"ID = %q, want %q\", got.ID, id)\n\t}\n\tif got.Title != \"Test Trail\" {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Title = %q, want %q\", got.Title, \"Test Trail\")\n\t}\n\tif got.Description != \"This is a test trail\" {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Description = %q, want %q\", got.Description, \"This is a test trail\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestStore_List(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\trepo, _ := createTestRepo(t)\n\tstore := NewStore(repo)\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\n\t// Initially empty\n\ttrails, err := store.List(ctx)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"List() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif len(trails) != 0 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"List() returned %d trails, want 0\", len(trails))\n\t}\n\n\t// Create two trails\n\tfor i := 0; i < 2; i++ {\n\t\tid, _ := GenerateTrailID()\n\t\ttrail := &Trail{\n\t\t\tID:          id,\n\t\t\tTitle:       \"Trail \" + string(rune('A'+i)),\n\t\t\tDescription: \"Description\",\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := store.Create(ctx, trail); err != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Create() error = %v\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// List should return 2\n\ttrails, err = store.List(ctx)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"List() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif len(trails) != 2 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"List() returned %d trails, want 2\", len(trails))\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestStore_Delete(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\trepo, _ := createTestRepo(t)\n\tstore := NewStore(repo)\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\n\t// Create a trail\n\tid, _ := GenerateTrailID()\n\ttrail := &Trail{\n\t\tID:          id,\n\t\tTitle:       \"Test Trail\",\n\t\tDescription: \"Description\",\n\t}\n\tif err := store.Create(ctx, trail); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Create() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Delete it\n\tif err := store.Delete(ctx, id); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Delete() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Should no longer exist\n\tgot, err := store.Get(ctx, id)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Get() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif got != nil {\n\t\tt.Error(\"Get() returned trail after delete\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestStateManager_ClaimAndComplete(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\trepo, _ := createTestRepo(t)\n\tstate := NewStateManager(repo)\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\n\tid, _ := GenerateTrailID()\n\thead := plumbing.NewHash(\"aaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaa\")\n\n\t// Initially open\n\tgot, err := state.GetState(ctx, id)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"GetState() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif got != TrailStateOpen {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"GetState() = %q, want %q\", got, TrailStateOpen)\n\t}\n\n\t// Claim\n\tif err := state.Claim(ctx, id, head); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Claim() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Should be in progress\n\tgot, err = state.GetState(ctx, id)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"GetState() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif got != TrailStateInProgress {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"GetState() = %q, want %q\", got, TrailStateInProgress)\n\t}\n\n\t// Claim again should fail\n\tif err := state.Claim(ctx, id, head); err != ErrAlreadyClaimed {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Claim() error = %v, want ErrAlreadyClaimed\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Complete\n\tif err := state.Complete(ctx, id, head); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Complete() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Should be completed\n\tgot, err = state.GetState(ctx, id)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"GetState() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif got != TrailStateCompleted {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"GetState() = %q, want %q\", got, TrailStateCompleted)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestStateManager_Fail(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\trepo, _ := createTestRepo(t)\n\tstate := NewStateManager(repo)\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\n\tid, _ := GenerateTrailID()\n\thead := plumbing.NewHash(\"aaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaa\")\n\n\t// Claim\n\tif err := state.Claim(ctx, id, head); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Claim() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Fail\n\tif err := state.Fail(ctx, id, head); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Fail() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Should be failed\n\tgot, err := state.GetState(ctx, id)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"GetState() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif got != TrailStateFailed {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"GetState() = %q, want %q\", got, TrailStateFailed)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestStateManager_Reset(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\trepo, _ := createTestRepo(t)\n\tstate := NewStateManager(repo)\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\n\tid, _ := GenerateTrailID()\n\thead := plumbing.NewHash(\"aaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaa\")\n\n\t// Claim and complete\n\tif err := state.Claim(ctx, id, head); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Claim() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif err := state.Complete(ctx, id, head); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Complete() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Reset\n\tif err := state.Reset(ctx, id); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Reset() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Should be open again\n\tgot, err := state.GetState(ctx, id)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"GetState() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif got != TrailStateOpen {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"GetState() = %q, want %q\", got, TrailStateOpen)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestDiscovery_ListWithState(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\trepo, _ := createTestRepo(t)\n\tstore := NewStore(repo)\n\tstate := NewStateManager(repo)\n\tdiscovery := NewDiscovery(repo)\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\n\t// Create some trails\n\tid1, _ := GenerateTrailID()\n\tid2, _ := GenerateTrailID()\n\tid3, _ := GenerateTrailID()\n\n\ttrails := []*Trail{\n\t\t{ID: id1, Title: \"Trail 1\", Description: \"Open trail\", Labels: []string{\"urgent\"}},\n\t\t{ID: id2, Title: \"Trail 2\", Description: \"In progress trail\"},\n\t\t{ID: id3, Title: \"Trail 3\", Description: \"Completed trail\"},\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, tr := range trails {\n\t\tif err := store.Create(ctx, tr); err != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Create() error = %v\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Set states\n\thead := plumbing.NewHash(\"aaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaa\")\n\t_ = state.Claim(ctx, id2, head)\n\t_ = state.Claim(ctx, id3, head)\n\t_ = state.Complete(ctx, id3, head)\n\n\t// List all\n\tall, err := discovery.ListWithState(ctx, nil)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"ListWithState() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif len(all) != 3 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"ListWithState(nil) returned %d trails, want 3\", len(all))\n\t}\n\n\t// Filter by state\n\topen, err := discovery.FindOpen(ctx)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"FindOpen() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif len(open) != 1 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"FindOpen() returned %d trails, want 1\", len(open))\n\t}\n\n\t// Filter by label\n\twithLabel, err := discovery.ListWithState(ctx, &ListFilter{Labels: []string{\"urgent\"}})\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"ListWithState(labels) error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif len(withLabel) != 1 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"ListWithState(labels) returned %d trails, want 1\", len(withLabel))\n\t}\n}\n"
}

     2→//
     3→// Trails are task definitions stored in the entire/trails orphan branch.
     4→// Execution state is tracked via lightweight git refs (no commits for state changes).

    35→// If the branch doesn't exist, it will be created from baseBranch.
    36→func (m *WorktreeManager) Create(ctx context.Context, id TrailID, branch, baseBranch string) (string, error) {
    37→	worktreePath := m.WorktreePath(id)
    38→
    39→	// Ensure the worktrees directory exists
    40→	worktreesDir := filepath.Join(m.repoRoot, WorktreeDir)
    41→	if err := os.MkdirAll(worktreesDir, 0o755); err != nil {
    42→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to create worktrees directory: %w", err)
    43→	}
    44→
    45→	// Check if worktree already exists
    46→	if _, err := os.Stat(worktreePath); err == nil {
    47→		return worktreePath, nil // Already exists
    48→	}
    49→
    50→	// Check if the branch exists
    51→	branchExists, err := m.branchExists(ctx, branch)
    52→	if err != nil {
    53→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to check branch existence: %w", err)
    54→	}
    55→
    56→	if branchExists {
    57→		// Worktree for existing branch
    58→		cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "worktree", "add", worktreePath, branch)
    59→		cmd.Dir = m.repoRoot
    60→		if output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
    61→			return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to create worktree: %s: %w", strings.TrimSpace(string(output)), err)
    62→		}
    63→	} else {
    64→		// Create new branch from base
    65→		if baseBranch == "" {
    66→			// Get default branch
    67→			baseBranch, err = m.getDefaultBranch(ctx)
    68→			if err != nil {
    69→				return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to determine base branch: %w", err)
    70→			}
    71→		}
    72→
    73→		// Create worktree with new branch
    74→		cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "worktree", "add", "-b", branch, worktreePath, baseBranch)
    75→		cmd.Dir = m.repoRoot
    76→		if output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
    77→			return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to create worktree with new branch: %s: %w", strings.TrimSpace(string(output)), err)
    78→		}
    79→	}
    80→
    81→	return worktreePath, nil
    82→}
    83→

   109→func (m *StateManager) Fail(ctx context.Context, id TrailID, branchHead plumbing.Hash) error {
   110→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use

   112→	// Verify trail was claimed

   126→		// Non-fatal: failed ref was created successfully
   127→		return nil
   128→	}

   130→	return nil
   131→}

> AGENT

All tests pass. The Trail Runner implementation is complete with all lint and test checks passing.

**Summary of what was implemented:**

**Core package** (`cmd/entire/cli/trail/`):
- `trail.go` - TrailID, Trail, TrailState types with validation
- `store.go` - CRUD operations for trails on `entire/trails` orphan branch
- `state.go` - Ref-based state management (`refs/entire/trails/<id>/claimed|completed|failed`)
- `discovery.go` - List and filter trails by state
- `worktree.go` - Git worktree management for isolated execution
- `runner.go` - Execution loop (discover → claim → worktree → agent → complete)
- `trail_test.go` - Unit tests

**CLI commands** (`cmd/entire/cli/`):
- `trail_cmd.go` - Trail management commands:
  - `entire trail list [--status <state>]`
  - `entire trail show <id>`
  - `entire trail create "Title" [--branch <name>] [--base <branch>]`
  - `entire trail delete <id>`
  - `entire trail reset <id>`

- `trail_runner_cmd.go` - Runner commands:
  - `entire trail-runner start [--daemon] [--poll-interval 30s]`
  - `entire trail-runner run-once`
  - `entire trail run <id>`
