    18→// ProofConfig defines a single proof (validation check) to run.
    19→type ProofConfig struct {
    20→	// Type is the proof identifier (e.g., "test", "lint", "typecheck")
    21→	Type string `json:"type"`
    22→	// Command is the shell command to execute
    23→	Command string `json:"command"`
    24→	// Required indicates if this proof must pass for validation to succeed
    25→	Required bool `json:"required"`
    26→}
    27→
    28→// ValidationConfig holds the validation configuration from settings.
    29→type ValidationConfig struct {
    30→	// Enabled controls whether validation runs automatically
    31→	Enabled bool `json:"enabled"`
    32→	// Proofs is the list of proof checks to run
    33→	Proofs []ProofConfig `json:"proofs"`
    34→}
    35→
    36→// ProofResult holds the result of running a single proof.
    37→type ProofResult struct {
    38→	Type     string        `json:"type"`
    39→	Command  string        `json:"command"`
    40→	Passed   bool          `json:"passed"`
    41→	Required bool          `json:"required"`
    42→	Output   string        `json:"output,omitempty"`
    43→	Error    string        `json:"error,omitempty"`
    44→	Duration time.Duration `json:"duration"`
    45→}
    46→
    47→// ValidationResult holds the complete validation results.
    48→type ValidationResult struct {
    49→	Passed    bool          `json:"passed"`
    50→	Proofs    []ProofResult `json:"proofs"`
    51→	Duration  time.Duration `json:"duration"`
    52→	Timestamp time.Time     `json:"timestamp"`
    53→}
    54→
    55→// DefaultValidationConfig returns the default validation configuration.
    56→func DefaultValidationConfig() *ValidationConfig {
    57→	return &ValidationConfig{
    58→		Enabled: false,
    59→		Proofs: []ProofConfig{
    60→			{Type: "test", Command: "mise run test", Required: true},
    61→			{Type: "lint", Command: "mise run lint", Required: true},
    62→		},
    63→	}
    64→}
    65→
    66→func newValidateCmd() *cobra.Command {
    67→	var (
    68→		jsonOutput bool
    69→		proofTypes []string
    70→	)
    71→
    72→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
    73→		Use:    "validate",
    74→		Short:  "Run validation proofs on the codebase",
    75→		Hidden: true, // Hidden experimental command
    76→		Long: `Run configured validation proofs to verify code quality.
    77→
    78→This command runs the validation proofs configured in .entire/settings.json
    79→under the "validation" key. Each proof is a command that must exit with
    80→status 0 to pass.
    81→
    82→Example configuration in .entire/settings.json:
    83→  {
    84→    "validation": {
    85→      "enabled": true,
    86→      "proofs": [
    87→        {"type": "test", "command": "mise run test", "required": true},
    88→        {"type": "lint", "command": "mise run lint", "required": true},
    89→        {"type": "typecheck", "command": "go build ./...", "required": true}
    90→      ]
    91→    }
    92→  }
    93→
    94→This is an experimental feature for autonomous validation workflows.`,
    95→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
    96→			return runValidate(cmd.OutOrStdout(), cmd.ErrOrStderr(), jsonOutput, proofTypes)
    97→		},
    98→	}
    99→
   100→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&jsonOutput, "json", false, "Output results as JSON")
   101→	cmd.Flags().StringSliceVar(&proofTypes, "proof", nil, "Run only specific proof types (can be repeated)")
   102→
   103→	return cmd
   104→}
   105→
   106→func runValidate(stdout, stderr io.Writer, jsonOutput bool, filterProofs []string) error {
   107→	// Check if we're in a git repository
   108→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   109→	if err != nil {
   110→		return fmt.Errorf("not in a git repository: %w", err)
   111→	}
   112→
   113→	// Load validation config
   114→	config, err := loadValidationConfig()
   115→	if err != nil {
   116→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load validation config: %w", err)
   117→	}
   118→
   119→	if len(config.Proofs) == 0 {
   120→		if jsonOutput {
   121→			result := &ValidationResult{
   122→				Passed:    true,
   123→				Proofs:    []ProofResult{},
   124→				Timestamp: time.Now(),
   125→			}
   126→			return outputJSON(stdout, result)
   127→		}
   128→		fmt.Fprintln(stdout, "No validation proofs configured.")
   129→		fmt.Fprintln(stdout, "\nAdd proofs to .entire/settings.json:")
   130→		fmt.Fprintln(stdout, `  "validation": {`)
   131→		fmt.Fprintln(stdout, `    "enabled": true,`)
   132→		fmt.Fprintln(stdout, `    "proofs": [`)
   133→		fmt.Fprintln(stdout, `      {"type": "test", "command": "mise run test", "required": true}`)
   134→		fmt.Fprintln(stdout, `    ]`)
   135→		fmt.Fprintln(stdout, `  }`)
   136→		return nil
   137→	}
   138→
   139→	// Filter proofs if specific types requested
   140→	proofsToRun := config.Proofs
   141→	if len(filterProofs) > 0 {
   142→		proofsToRun = filterProofsByType(config.Proofs, filterProofs)
   143→		if len(proofsToRun) == 0 {
   144→			return fmt.Errorf("no matching proofs found for types: %v", filterProofs)
   145→		}
   146→	}
   147→
   148→	// Run validation
   149→	result := runProofs(stdout, stderr, repoRoot, proofsToRun, jsonOutput)
   150→
   151→	if jsonOutput {
   152→		return outputJSON(stdout, result)
   153→	}
   154→
   155→	// Print summary
   156→	printValidationSummary(stdout, result)
   157→
   158→	if !result.Passed {
   159→		return NewSilentError(errors.New("validation failed"))
   160→	}
   161→	return nil
   162→}
   163→
   164→func loadValidationConfig() (*ValidationConfig, error) {
   165→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   166→	if err != nil {
   167→		return DefaultValidationConfig(), nil //nolint:nilerr // use defaults on error
   168→	}
   169→
   170→	// Check if validation is configured in strategy_options
   171→	if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   172→		return DefaultValidationConfig(), nil
   173→	}
   174→
   175→	validationRaw, ok := settings.StrategyOptions["validation"]
   176→	if !ok {
   177→		return DefaultValidationConfig(), nil
   178→	}
   179→
   180→	// Convert to JSON and back to parse into ValidationConfig
   181→	data, err := json.Marshal(validationRaw)
   182→	if err != nil {
   183→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("marshaling validation config: %w", err)
   184→	}
   185→
   186→	var config ValidationConfig
   187→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &config); err != nil {
   188→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("parsing validation config: %w", err)
   189→	}
   190→
   191→	return &config, nil
   192→}
   193→
   194→func filterProofsByType(proofs []ProofConfig, types []string) []ProofConfig {
   195→	typeSet := make(map[string]bool)
   196→	for _, t := range types {
   197→		typeSet[strings.ToLower(t)] = true
   198→	}
   199→
   200→	var filtered []ProofConfig
   201→	for _, p := range proofs {
   202→		if typeSet[strings.ToLower(p.Type)] {
   203→			filtered = append(filtered, p)
   204→		}
   205→	}
   206→	return filtered
   207→}
   208→
   209→func runProofs(stdout, stderr io.Writer, repoRoot string, proofs []ProofConfig, quiet bool) *ValidationResult {
   210→	start := time.Now()
   211→	result := &ValidationResult{
   212→		Passed:    true,
   213→		Proofs:    make([]ProofResult, 0, len(proofs)),
   214→		Timestamp: start,
   215→	}
   216→
   217→	for i, proof := range proofs {
   218→		if !quiet {
   219→			fmt.Fprintf(stdout, "\n[%d/%d] Running %s: %s\n", i+1, len(proofs), proof.Type, proof.Command)
   220→		}
   221→
   222→		proofResult := runSingleProof(repoRoot, proof)
   223→		result.Proofs = append(result.Proofs, proofResult)
   224→
   225→		if !quiet {
   226→			if proofResult.Passed {
   227→				fmt.Fprintf(stdout, "  ✓ PASSED (%s)\n", proofResult.Duration.Round(time.Millisecond))
   228→			} else {
   229→				fmt.Fprintf(stdout, "  ✗ FAILED (%s)\n", proofResult.Duration.Round(time.Millisecond))
   230→				if proofResult.Output != "" {
   231→					// Print last 20 lines of output
   232→					lines := strings.Split(strings.TrimSpace(proofResult.Output), "\n")
   233→					if len(lines) > 20 {
   234→						fmt.Fprintf(stderr, "  ... (%d lines truncated)\n", len(lines)-20)
   235→						lines = lines[len(lines)-20:]
   236→					}
   237→					for _, line := range lines {
   238→						fmt.Fprintf(stderr, "  │ %s\n", line)
   239→					}
   240→				}
   241→				if proofResult.Error != "" {
   242→					fmt.Fprintf(stderr, "  Error: %s\n", proofResult.Error)
   243→				}
   244→			}
   245→		}
   246→
   247→		// Update overall pass status
   248→		if !proofResult.Passed && proof.Required {
   249→			result.Passed = false
   250→		}
   251→	}
   252→
   253→	result.Duration = time.Since(start)
   254→	return result
   255→}
   256→
   257→func runSingleProof(repoRoot string, proof ProofConfig) ProofResult {
   258→	start := time.Now()
   259→	result := ProofResult{
   260→		Type:     proof.Type,
   261→		Command:  proof.Command,
   262→		Required: proof.Required,
   263→	}
   264→
   265→	// Create command with shell
   266→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Minute)
   267→	defer cancel()
   268→
   269→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "sh", "-c", proof.Command) //nolint:gosec // command is user-configured in settings.json
   270→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   271→
   272→	// Capture output
   273→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   274→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   275→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   276→
   277→	err := cmd.Run()
   278→	result.Duration = time.Since(start)
   279→
   280→	// Combine stdout and stderr for output
   281→	output := stdout.String()
   282→	if stderr.Len() > 0 {
   283→		if output != "" {
   284→			output += "\n"
   285→		}
   286→		output += stderr.String()
   287→	}
   288→	result.Output = strings.TrimSpace(output)
   289→
   290→	if err != nil {
   291→		result.Passed = false
   292→		if ctx.Err() == context.DeadlineExceeded {
   293→			result.Error = "command timed out after 10 minutes"
   294→		} else {
   295→			result.Error = err.Error()
   296→		}
   297→	} else {
   298→		result.Passed = true
   299→	}
   300→
   301→	return result
   302→}
   303→
   304→func printValidationSummary(w io.Writer, result *ValidationResult) {
   305→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\n"+strings.Repeat("─", 50))
   306→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Validation Summary")
   307→	fmt.Fprintln(w, strings.Repeat("─", 50))
   308→
   309→	passed := 0
   310→	failed := 0
   311→	for _, p := range result.Proofs {
   312→		status := "✓"
   313→		if !p.Passed {
   314→			status = "✗"
   315→			failed++
   316→		} else {
   317→			passed++
   318→		}
   319→		reqLabel := ""
   320→		if !p.Required {
   321→			reqLabel = " (optional)"
   322→		}
   323→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  %s %s%s\n", status, p.Type, reqLabel)
   324→	}
   325→
   326→	fmt.Fprintln(w, strings.Repeat("─", 50))
   327→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "Total: %d passed, %d failed (%.1fs)\n", passed, failed, result.Duration.Seconds())
   328→
   329→	if result.Passed {
   330→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "\n✓ All required proofs passed")
   331→	} else {
   332→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "\n✗ Validation failed - required proofs did not pass")
   333→	}
   334→}
   335→
   336→func outputJSON(w io.Writer, result *ValidationResult) error {
   337→	enc := json.NewEncoder(w)
   338→	enc.SetIndent("", "  ")
   339→	if err := enc.Encode(result); err != nil {
   340→		return fmt.Errorf("encoding validation result: %w", err)
   341→	}
   342→	return nil
   343→}
   344→

    20→// RunnerConfig holds configuration for the trail runner.
    21→type RunnerConfig struct {
    22→	// PollInterval is how often to check for new trails in daemon mode.
    23→	PollInterval time.Duration
    24→
    25→	// MaxConcurrent is the maximum number of trails to run concurrently.
    26→	// Currently only 1 is supported.
    27→	MaxConcurrent int
    28→
    29→	// Daemon enables continuous polling mode.
    30→	Daemon bool
    31→
    32→	// Agent is the agent CLI to use (e.g., "claude").
    33→	Agent string
    34→
    35→	// Model is the optional model override for the agent.
    36→	Model string
    37→
    38→	// Timeout is the maximum time to wait for a single trail execution.
    39→	Timeout time.Duration
    40→
    41→	// DryRun prevents actual execution, just prints what would be done.
    42→	DryRun bool
    43→}
    44→
    45→// DefaultRunnerConfig returns the default runner configuration.
    46→func DefaultRunnerConfig() RunnerConfig {
    47→	return RunnerConfig{
    48→		PollInterval:  30 * time.Second,
    49→		MaxConcurrent: 1,
    50→		Daemon:        false,
    51→		Agent:         "claude",
    52→		Model:         "",
    53→		Timeout:       30 * time.Minute,
    54→		DryRun:        false,
    55→	}
    56→}
    57→
    58→// Runner executes trails by discovering open tasks, claiming them,
    59→// creating worktrees, running agents, and updating state.
    60→type Runner struct {
    61→	repo      *git.Repository
    62→	repoRoot  string
    63→	store     *Store
    64→	state     *StateManager
    65→	discovery *Discovery
    66→	worktree  *WorktreeManager
    67→	config    RunnerConfig
    68→}
    69→
    70→// NewRunner creates a new trail runner.
    71→func NewRunner(repo *git.Repository, repoRoot string, config RunnerConfig) *Runner {
    72→	return &Runner{
    73→		repo:      repo,
    74→		repoRoot:  repoRoot,
    75→		store:     NewStore(repo),
    76→		state:     NewStateManager(repo),
    77→		discovery: NewDiscovery(repo),
    78→		worktree:  NewWorktreeManager(repoRoot),
    79→		config:    config,
    80→	}
    81→}
    82→
    83→// RunResult holds the result of running a single trail.
    84→type RunResult struct {
    85→	Trail    *Trail
    86→	Success  bool
    87→	Output   string
    88→	Error    error
    89→	Duration time.Duration
    90→}
    91→
    92→// Start runs the trail runner in daemon mode, continuously polling for new trails.
    93→func (r *Runner) Start(ctx context.Context) error {
    94→	logging.Info(ctx, "trail runner started",
    95→		slog.Duration("poll_interval", r.config.PollInterval),
    96→		slog.Bool("daemon", r.config.Daemon),
    97→	)
    98→
    99→	for {
   100→		// Run one iteration
   101→		results, err := r.RunOnce(ctx)
   102→		if err != nil {
   103→			logging.Error(ctx, "runner iteration failed", slog.String("error", err.Error()))
   104→		}
   105→
   106→		for _, result := range results {
   107→			if result.Success {
   108→				logging.Info(ctx, "trail completed successfully",
   109→					slog.String("trail_id", result.Trail.ID.String()),
   110→					slog.Duration("duration", result.Duration),
   111→				)
   112→			} else {
   113→				logging.Error(ctx, "trail failed",
   114→					slog.String("trail_id", result.Trail.ID.String()),
   115→					slog.String("error", result.Error.Error()),
   116→				)
   117→			}
   118→		}
   119→
   120→		if !r.config.Daemon {
   121→			return nil
   122→		}
   123→
   124→		// Wait before next poll
   125→		select {
   126→		case <-ctx.Done():
   127→			logging.Info(ctx, "trail runner stopping")
   128→			return fmt.Errorf("trail runner stopped: %w", ctx.Err())
   129→		case <-time.After(r.config.PollInterval):
   130→			// Continue to next iteration
   131→		}
   132→	}
   133→}
   134→
   135→// RunOnce performs a single iteration: find open trails and run them.
   136→func (r *Runner) RunOnce(ctx context.Context) ([]RunResult, error) {
   137→	// Find open trails
   138→	openTrails, err := r.discovery.FindOpen(ctx)
   139→	if err != nil {
   140→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to find open trails: %w", err)
   141→	}
   142→
   143→	if len(openTrails) == 0 {
   144→		logging.Debug(ctx, "no open trails found")
   145→		return nil, nil
   146→	}
   147→
   148→	logging.Info(ctx, "found open trails", slog.Int("count", len(openTrails)))
   149→
   150→	var results []RunResult
   151→
   152→	// Run trails up to max concurrent (currently only 1)
   153→	maxToRun := min(len(openTrails), r.config.MaxConcurrent)
   154→	for i := range maxToRun {
   155→		trail := openTrails[i].Trail
   156→		result := r.RunTrail(ctx, &trail)
   157→		results = append(results, result)
   158→	}
   159→
   160→	return results, nil
   161→}
   162→
   163→// RunTrail executes a single trail.
   164→func (r *Runner) RunTrail(ctx context.Context, trail *Trail) RunResult {
   165→	start := time.Now()
   166→	result := RunResult{Trail: trail}
   167→
   168→	ctx = logging.WithComponent(ctx, "trail-runner")
   169→
   170→	logging.Info(ctx, "starting trail execution",
   171→		slog.String("trail_id", trail.ID.String()),
   172→		slog.String("title", trail.Title),
   173→	)
   174→
   175→	if r.config.DryRun {
   176→		logging.Info(ctx, "dry run - skipping execution")
   177→		result.Success = true
   178→		result.Output = "dry run"
   179→		result.Duration = time.Since(start)
   180→		return result
   181→	}
   182→
   183→	// Create worktree and get initial branch head
   184→	worktreePath, branchHead, err := r.setupWorktree(ctx, trail)
   185→	if err != nil {
   186→		result.Error = fmt.Errorf("failed to setup worktree: %w", err)
   187→		result.Duration = time.Since(start)
   188→		return result
   189→	}
   190→
   191→	// Claim the trail
   192→	if err := r.state.Claim(ctx, trail.ID, branchHead); err != nil {
   193→		if errors.Is(err, ErrAlreadyClaimed) {
   194→			logging.Info(ctx, "trail already claimed, skipping",
   195→				slog.String("trail_id", trail.ID.String()),
   196→			)
   197→		}
   198→		result.Error = err
   199→		result.Duration = time.Since(start)
   200→		// Clean up worktree if we couldn't claim
   201→		if removeErr := r.worktree.Remove(ctx, trail.ID); removeErr != nil {
   202→			logging.Warn(ctx, "failed to remove worktree after claim failure",
   203→				slog.String("trail_id", trail.ID.String()),
   204→				slog.String("error", removeErr.Error()),
   205→			)
   206→		}
   207→		return result
   208→	}
   209→
   210→	// Run the agent
   211→	output, agentErr := r.runAgent(ctx, trail, worktreePath)
   212→	result.Output = output
   213→
   214→	// Get final branch head
   215→	finalHead, headErr := r.worktree.GetHeadCommit(ctx, trail.ID)
   216→	finalHeadHash := plumbing.NewHash(finalHead)
   217→	if headErr != nil || finalHeadHash.IsZero() {
   218→		// Fall back to initial branch head if we can't get the current
   219→		finalHeadHash = branchHead
   220→	}
   221→
   222→	// Update state based on result
   223→	if agentErr != nil {
   224→		result.Error = agentErr
   225→		result.Success = false
   226→		if err := r.state.Fail(ctx, trail.ID, finalHeadHash); err != nil {
   227→			logging.Error(ctx, "failed to mark trail as failed",
   228→				slog.String("trail_id", trail.ID.String()),
   229→				slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   230→			)
   231→		}
   232→	} else {
   233→		result.Success = true
   234→		if err := r.state.Complete(ctx, trail.ID, finalHeadHash); err != nil {
   235→			logging.Error(ctx, "failed to mark trail as completed",
   236→				slog.String("trail_id", trail.ID.String()),
   237→				slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   238→			)
   239→		}
   240→	}
   241→
   242→	// Clean up worktree
   243→	if err := r.worktree.Remove(ctx, trail.ID); err != nil {
   244→		logging.Warn(ctx, "failed to remove worktree",
   245→			slog.String("trail_id", trail.ID.String()),
   246→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   247→		)
   248→	}
   249→
   250→	result.Duration = time.Since(start)
   251→
   252→	logging.Info(ctx, "trail execution finished",
   253→		slog.String("trail_id", trail.ID.String()),
   254→		slog.Bool("success", result.Success),
   255→		slog.Duration("duration", result.Duration),
   256→	)
   257→
   258→	return result
   259→}
   260→
   261→// setupWorktree creates a worktree for the trail and returns the path and branch head.
   262→func (r *Runner) setupWorktree(ctx context.Context, trail *Trail) (string, plumbing.Hash, error) {
   263→	branch := trail.GetBranch()
   264→
   265→	// Ensure .worktrees is in .gitignore
   266→	if err := EnsureWorktreesIgnored(r.repoRoot); err != nil {
   267→		logging.Warn(ctx, "failed to ensure worktrees ignored",
   268→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   269→		)
   270→	}
   271→
   272→	// Create the worktree
   273→	worktreePath, err := r.worktree.Create(ctx, trail.ID, branch, trail.BaseBranch)
   274→	if err != nil {
   275→		return "", plumbing.ZeroHash, err
   276→	}
   277→
   278→	// Get the branch head
   279→	headCommit, err := r.worktree.GetHeadCommit(ctx, trail.ID)
   280→	if err != nil {
   281→		return "", plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to get branch head: %w", err)
   282→	}
   283→
   284→	return worktreePath, plumbing.NewHash(headCommit), nil
   285→}
   286→
   287→// runAgent executes the agent CLI with the trail description.
   288→func (r *Runner) runAgent(ctx context.Context, trail *Trail, worktreePath string) (string, error) {
   289→	// Build the prompt from trail description
   290→	prompt := trail.Description
   291→
   292→	// Build command arguments
   293→	args := []string{}
   294→
   295→	// Add model if specified
   296→	if r.config.Model != "" {
   297→		args = append(args, "--model", r.config.Model)
   298→	}
   299→
   300→	// Add prompt
   301→	args = append(args, "-p", prompt)
   302→
   303→	// Create context with timeout
   304→	if r.config.Timeout > 0 {
   305→		var cancel context.CancelFunc
   306→		ctx, cancel = context.WithTimeout(ctx, r.config.Timeout)
   307→		defer cancel()
   308→	}
   309→
   310→	logging.Debug(ctx, "running agent",
   311→		slog.String("agent", r.config.Agent),
   312→		slog.String("worktree", worktreePath),
   313→	)
   314→
   315→	//nolint:gosec // args are constructed from trusted config
   316→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, r.config.Agent, args...)
   317→	cmd.Dir = worktreePath
   318→	cmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   319→		// Ensure entire hooks are enabled in the worktree
   320→		"ENTIRE_ENABLED=1",
   321→	)
   322→
   323→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   324→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   325→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   326→
   327→	err := cmd.Run()
   328→
   329→	output := stdout.String()
   330→	if stderr.Len() > 0 {
   331→		if output != "" {
   332→			output += "\n"
   333→		}
   334→		output += stderr.String()
   335→	}
   336→
   337→	if err != nil {
   338→		if ctx.Err() == context.DeadlineExceeded {
   339→			return output, fmt.Errorf("agent timed out after %s", r.config.Timeout)
   340→		}
   341→		return output, fmt.Errorf("agent failed: %s: %w", strings.TrimSpace(stderr.String()), err)
   342→	}
   343→
   344→	return output, nil
   345→}
   346→
   347→// RunTrailByID executes a trail by its ID.
   348→func (r *Runner) RunTrailByID(ctx context.Context, id TrailID) (RunResult, error) {
   349→	trail, err := r.store.Get(ctx, id)
   350→	if err != nil {
   351→		return RunResult{}, fmt.Errorf("failed to get trail: %w", err)
   352→	}
   353→	if trail == nil {
   354→		return RunResult{}, fmt.Errorf("trail not found: %s", id)
   355→	}
   356→
   357→	return r.RunTrail(ctx, trail), nil
   358→}

err != nil {\n\t\tif errors.Is(err, ErrAlreadyClaimed) {\n\t\t\tlogging.Info(ctx, \"trail already claimed, skipping\",\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"trail_id\", trail.ID.String()),\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t}\n\t\tresult.Error = err\n\t\tresult.Duration = time.Since(start)\n\t\t// Clean up worktree if we couldn't claim\n\t\tif removeErr := r.worktree.Remove(ctx, trail.ID); err != nil {\n\t\t\tlogging.Error(ctx, \"failed to mark trail as completed\",\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"trail_id\", trail.ID.String()),\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"error\", err.Error()),\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Clean up worktree\n\tif err := r.worktree.Remove(ctx, trail.ID); err != nil {\n\t\tlogging.Warn(ctx, \"failed to remove worktree\",\n\t\t\tslog.String(\"trail_id\", trail.ID.String()),\n\t\t\tslog.String(\"error\", err.Error()),\n\t\t)\n\t}\n\n\tresult.Duration = time.Since(start)\n\n\tlogging.Info(ctx, \"trail execution finished\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"trail_id\", trail.ID.String()),\n\t\tslog.Bool(\"success\", result.Success),\n\t\tslog.Duration(\"duration\", result.Duration),\n\t)\n\n\treturn result\n}",
  "new_string": "// RunTrail executes a single trail.\nfunc (r *Runner) RunTrail(ctx context.Context, trail *Trail) RunResult {\n\tstart := time.Now()\n\tresult := RunResult{Trail: trail}\n\n\tctx = logging.WithComponent(ctx, \"trail-runner\")\n\n\tlogging.Info(ctx, \"starting trail execution\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"trail_id\", trail.ID.String()),\n\t\tslog.String(\"title\", trail.Title),\n\t)\n\n\tif r.config.DryRun {\n\t\tlogging.Info(ctx, \"dry run - skipping execution\")\n\t\tresult.Success = true\n\t\tresult.Output = \"dry run\"\n\t\tresult.Duration = time.Since(start)\n\t\treturn result\n\t}\n\n\t// Create worktree and get initial branch head\n\tworktreePath, branchHead, err := r.setupWorktree(ctx, trail)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tresult.Error = fmt.Errorf(\"failed to setup worktree: %w\", err)\n\t\tresult.Duration = time.Since(start)\n\t\treturn result\n\t}\n\n\t// Claim the trail\n\tif err := r.state.Claim(ctx, trail.ID, branchHead); err != nil {\n\t\tif errors.Is(err, ErrAlreadyClaimed) {\n\t\t\tlogging.Info(ctx, \"trail already claimed, skipping\",\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"trail_id\", trail.ID.String()),\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t}\n\t\tresult.Error = err\n\t\tresult.Duration = time.Since(start)\n\t\t// Clean up worktree if we couldn't claim\n\t\tif removeErr := r.worktree.Remove(ctx, trail.ID); removeErr != nil {\n\t\t\tlogging.Warn(ctx, \"failed to remove worktree after claim failure\",\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"trail_id\", trail.ID.String()),\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"error\", removeErr.Error()),\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn result\n\t}\n\n\t// Run agent with validation loop\n\tmaxAttempts := r.config.MaxAttempts\n\tif maxAttempts < 1 {\n\t\tmaxAttempts = 1\n\t}\n\n\tvar allOutput strings.Builder\n\tvar lastValidationOutput string\n\n\tfor attempt := 1; attempt <= maxAttempts; attempt++ {\n\t\tresult.Attempts = attempt\n\n\t\tlogging.Info(ctx, \"running agent\",\n\t\t\tslog.String(\"trail_id\", trail.ID.String()),\n\t\t\tslog.Int(\"attempt\", attempt),\n\t\t\tslog.Int(\"max_attempts\", maxAttempts),\n\t\t)\n\n\t\t// Build prompt - include validation feedback if this is a retry\n\t\tprompt := trail.Description\n\t\tif attempt > 1 && lastValidationOutput != \"\" {\n\t\t\tprompt = fmt.Sprintf(\"%s\\n\\n---\\n\\nPrevious attempt failed validation. Please fix the issues and try again.\\n\\nValidation output:\\n%s\",\n\t\t\t\ttrail.Description, lastValidationOutput)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Run the agent\n\t\toutput, agentErr := r.runAgentWithPrompt(ctx, prompt, worktreePath)\n\t\tallOutput.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf(\"\\n=== Attempt %d ===\\n\", attempt))\n\t\tallOutput.WriteString(output)\n\n\t\tif agentErr != nil {\n\t\t\tresult.Error = agentErr\n\t\t\tresult.Success = false\n\t\t\tresult.Output = allOutput.String()\n\t\t\tlogging.Error(ctx, \"agent failed\",\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"trail_id\", trail.ID.String()),\n\t\t\t\tslog.Int(\"attempt\", attempt),\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"error\", agentErr.Error()),\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Run validation\n\t\tlogging.Info(ctx, \"running validation\",\n\t\t\tslog.String(\"trail_id\", trail.ID.String()),\n\t\t\tslog.Int(\"attempt\", attempt),\n\t\t)\n\n\t\tvalidationPassed, validationOutput, validationErr := r.runValidation(ctx, worktreePath)\n\t\tallOutput.WriteString(\"\\n--- Validation ---\\n\")\n\t\tallOutput.WriteString(validationOutput)\n\n\t\tif validationErr != nil {\n\t\t\tlogging.Warn(ctx, \"validation command failed\",\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"trail_id\", trail.ID.String()),\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"error\", validationErr.Error()),\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t\t// Treat validation command failure as validation failure\n\t\t\tvalidationPassed = false\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tresult.ValidationPassed = validationPassed\n\n\t\tif validationPassed {\n\t\t\tlogging.Info(ctx, \"validation passed\",\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"trail_id\", trail.ID.String()),\n\t\t\t\tslog.Int(\"attempt\", attempt),\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t\tresult.Success = true\n\t\t\tresult.Output = allOutput.String()\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tlogging.Info(ctx, \"validation failed\",\n\t\t\tslog.String(\"trail_id\", trail.ID.String()),\n\t\t\tslog.Int(\"attempt\", attempt),\n\t\t)\n\n\t\tlastValidationOutput = validationOutput\n\n\t\t// If this was the last attempt, mark as failed\n\t\tif attempt == maxAttempts {\n\t\t\tresult.Error = errors.New(\"validation failed after all attempts\")\n\t\t\tresult.Success = false\n\t\t\tresult.Output = allOutput.String()\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Get final branch head\n\tfinalHead, headErr := r.worktree.GetHeadCommit(ctx, trail.ID)\n\tfinalHeadHash := plumbing.NewHash(finalHead)\n\tif headErr != nil || finalHeadHash.IsZero() {\n\t\t// Fall back to initial branch head if we can't get the current\n\t\tfinalHeadHash = branchHead\n\t}\n\n\t// Update state based on result\n\tif result.Success {\n\t\tif err := r.state.Complete(ctx, trail.ID, finalHeadHash); err != nil {\n\t\t\tlogging.Error(ctx, \"failed to mark trail as completed\",\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"trail_id\", trail.ID.String()),\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"error\", err.Error()),\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t}\n\t} else {\n\t\tif err := r.state.Fail(ctx, trail.ID, finalHeadHash); err != nil {\n\t\t\tlogging.Error(ctx, \"failed to mark trail as failed\",\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"trail_id\", trail.ID.String()),\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"error\", err.Error()),\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Clean up worktree\n\tif err := r.worktree.Remove(ctx, trail.ID); err != nil {\n\t\tlogging.Warn(ctx, \"failed to remove worktree\",\n\t\t\tslog.String(\"trail_id\", trail.ID.String()),\n\t\t\tslog.String(\"error\", err.Error()),\n\t\t)\n\t}\n\n\tresult.Duration = time.Since(start)\n\n\tlogging.Info(ctx, \"trail execution finished\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"trail_id\", trail.ID.String()),\n\t\tslog.Bool(\"success\", result.Success),\n\t\tslog.Int(\"attempts\", result.Attempts),\n\t\tslog.Duration(\"duration\", result.Duration),\n\t)\n\n\treturn result\n}"
}

prompt\n\targs = append(args, \"-p\", prompt)\n\n\t// Create context with timeout\n\tif r.config.Timeout > 0 {\n\t\tvar cancel context.CancelFunc\n\t\tctx, cancel = context.WithTimeout(ctx, r.config.Timeout)\n\t\tdefer cancel()\n\t}\n\n\tlogging.Debug(ctx, \"running agent\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"agent\", r.config.Agent),\n\t\tslog.String(\"worktree\", worktreePath),\n\t)\n\n\t//nolint:gosec // args are constructed from trusted config\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, r.config.Agent, args...)\n\tcmd.Dir = worktreePath\n\tcmd.Env = &stderr\n\n\terr := cmd.Run()\n\n\toutput := stdout.String()\n\tif stderr.Len() > 0 {\n\t\tif output != \"\" {\n\t\t\toutput += \"\\n\"\n\t\t}\n\t\toutput += stderr.String()\n\t}\n\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif ctx.Err() == context.DeadlineExceeded {\n\t\t\treturn output, fmt.Errorf(\"agent timed out after %s\", r.config.Timeout)\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn output, fmt.Errorf(\"agent failed: %s: %w\", strings.TrimSpace(stderr.String()), err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn output, nil\n}\n\n// runValidation runs \"entire validate --json\" and returns whether validation passed.\nfunc (r *Runner) runValidation(ctx context.Context, worktreePath string) (bool, string, error) {\n\t// Create context with timeout for validation (10 minutes)\n\tctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 10*time.Minute)\n\tdefer cancel()\n\n\tentireCLI := r.config.EntireCLI\n\tif entireCLI == \"\" {\n\t\tentireCLI = \"entire\"\n\t}\n\n\t//nolint:gosec // entireCLI is from trusted config\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, entireCLI, \"validate\", \"--json\")\n\tcmd.Dir = worktreePath\n\n\tvar stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer\n\tcmd.Stdout = &stdout\n\tcmd.Stderr = &stderr\n\n\terr := cmd.Run()\n\n\t// Combine output for logging\n\toutput := stdout.String()\n\tif stderr.Len() > 0 {\n\t\tif output != \"\" {\n\t\t\toutput += \"\\n\"\n\t\t}\n\t\toutput += stderr.String()\n\t}\n\n\t// Try to parse JSON result\n\tvar result validationResult\n\tif jsonErr := json.Unmarshal(stdout.Bytes(), &result); jsonErr == nil {\n\t\treturn result.Passed, output, nil\n\t}\n\n\t// If we couldn't parse JSON but command succeeded, assume passed\n\tif err == nil {\n\t\treturn true, output, nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Command failed\n\tif ctx.Err() == context.DeadlineExceeded {\n\t\treturn false, output, fmt.Errorf(\"validation timed out\")\n\t}\n\n\treturn false, output, fmt.Errorf(\"validation failed: %w\", err)\n}"

   136→func runTrailRunnerStart(cmd *cobra.Command, daemon bool, pollInterval time.Duration, agent, model string, timeout time.Duration, dryRun bool) error {
   137→	repo, err := openRepository()
   138→	if err != nil {
   139→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   140→	}
   141→
   142→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   143→	if err != nil {
   144→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repository root: %w", err)
   145→	}
   146→
   147→	config := trail.RunnerConfig{
   148→		PollInterval:  pollInterval,
   149→		MaxConcurrent: 1,
   150→		Daemon:        daemon,
   151→		Agent:         agent,
   152→		Model:         model,
   153→		Timeout:       timeout,
   154→		DryRun:        dryRun,
   155→	}
   156→
   157→	runner := trail.NewRunner(repo, repoRoot, config)
   158→	ctx := context.Background()
   159→
   160→	if daemon {
   161→		fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Starting trail runner in daemon mode (poll interval: %s)\n", pollInterval)
   162→	} else {
   163→		fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Starting trail runner...")
   164→	}
   165→
   166→	if err := runner.Start(ctx); err != nil {
   167→		return fmt.Errorf("runner failed: %w", err)
   168→	}
   169→
   170→	return nil
   171→}
   172→
   173→func runTrailRunnerRunOnce(cmd *cobra.Command, agent, model string, timeout time.Duration, dryRun bool) error {
   174→	repo, err := openRepository()
   175→	if err != nil {
   176→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   177→	}
   178→
   179→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   180→	if err != nil {
   181→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repository root: %w", err)
   182→	}
   183→
   184→	config := trail.RunnerConfig{
   185→		MaxConcurrent: 1,
   186→		Daemon:        false,
   187→		Agent:         agent,
   188→		Model:         model,
   189→		Timeout:       timeout,
   190→		DryRun:        dryRun,
   191→	}
   192→
   193→	runner := trail.NewRunner(repo, repoRoot, config)
   194→	ctx := context.Background()
   195→
   196→	results, err := runner.RunOnce(ctx)
   197→	if err != nil {
   198→		return fmt.Errorf("runner failed: %w", err)
   199→	}
   200→
   201→	if len(results) == 0 {
   202→		fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "No open trails to run.")
   203→		return nil
   204→	}
   205→
   206→	for _, result := range results {
   207→		if result.Success {
   208→			fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Trail %s completed successfully (%s)\n",
   209→				result.Trail.ID.Short(), result.Duration.Round(time.Second))
   210→		} else {
   211→			fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Trail %s failed: %v\n",
   212→				result.Trail.ID.Short(), result.Error)
   213→		}
   214→	}
   215→
   216→	return nil
   217→}
   218→
   219→func runTrailRun(cmd *cobra.Command, idStr, agent, model string, timeout time.Duration, dryRun bool) error {
   220→	id, err := trail.NewTrailID(idStr)
   221→	if err != nil {
   222→		return err //nolint:wrapcheck // validation error
   223→	}
   224→
   225→	repo, err := openRepository()
   226→	if err != nil {
   227→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   228→	}
   229→
   230→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   231→	if err != nil {
   232→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repository root: %w", err)
   233→	}
   234→
   235→	config := trail.RunnerConfig{
   236→		MaxConcurrent: 1,
   237→		Daemon:        false,
   238→		Agent:         agent,
   239→		Model:         model,
   240→		Timeout:       timeout,
   241→		DryRun:        dryRun,
   242→	}
   243→
   244→	runner := trail.NewRunner(repo, repoRoot, config)
   245→	ctx := context.Background()
   246→
   247→	fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Running trail: %s\n", id)
   248→
   249→	result, err := runner.RunTrailByID(ctx, id)
   250→	if err != nil {
   251→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to run trail: %w", err)
   252→	}
   253→
   254→	if result.Success {
   255→		fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "\nTrail completed successfully (%s)\n",
   256→			result.Duration.Round(time.Second))
   257→	} else {
   258→		fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "\nTrail failed: %v\n", result.Error)
   259→		return NewSilentError(result.Error)
   260→	}
   261→
   262→	return nil
   263→}
   264→

> AGENT

All tests pass. Here's a summary of the changes made:

## Changes to Trail Runner

**Modified `runner.go`:**
1. Added `MaxAttempts` and `EntireCLI` to `RunnerConfig` (defaults: 3 attempts, "entire" CLI)
2. Added `Attempts` and `ValidationPassed` fields to `RunResult`
3. Rewrote `RunTrail` to include a validation loop:
   - Runs the agent
   - Runs `entire validate --json` to check if the work is complete
   - If validation passes → marks trail as completed
   - If validation fails → re-runs the agent with feedback about failures (up to `MaxAttempts` times)
   - If all attempts exhausted → marks trail as failed
4. Added `runValidation()` function that executes `entire validate --json` and parses the result
5. Renamed `runAgent` to `runAgentWithPrompt` to accept a dynamic prompt (used for retry feedback)

**Modified `trail_runner_cmd.go`:**
1. Added `--max-attempts` flag to all runner commands (default: 3)
2. Updated output messages to include attempt count

## Example Flow

```
Trail execution starts
├── Attempt 1:
│   ├── Run agent with trail description
│   ├── Run `entire validate --json`
│   └── Validation failed (tests failing)
├── Attempt 2:
│   ├── Run agent with: trail description + "Previous attempt failed validation. Please fix..."
│   ├── Run `entire validate --json`
│   └── Validation passed!
└── Mark trail as completed
```

> DEVELOPER

which agent would now be run?

> AGENT

Looking at the code, the agent is configured via the `--agent` flag, which defaults to `"claude"`:

```go
// In trail_runner_cmd.go
cmd.Flags().StringVar(&agent, "agent", "claude", "Agent CLI to use")
```

So by default, it runs the `claude` CLI (Claude Code). The runner executes it like:

```bash
claude --model <model> -p "<prompt>"
```

You can override it with `--agent` to use a different agent CLI:

```bash
entire trail run abc123 --agent gemini   # Use Gemini CLI
entire trail run abc123 --agent claude   # Use Claude (default)
```

Is there a different agent you'd like it to use by default, or should it be using `entire` itself (to run validation within the same tool)?

From d13528a136daf027913009d3cbe248be8080319d Mon Sep 17 00:00:00 2001
From: Daniel Adams <daniel@entire.io>
Date: Wed, 11 Feb 2026 16:59:14 +0100
Subject: [PATCH 01/44] Add wingman automated code review feature

Adds `entire wingman` command group (enable/disable/status) and a
background review loop that analyzes agent code changes via Claude,
writes suggestions to .entire/REVIEW.md, and auto-applies them when
the session is idle. Includes lock file to prevent concurrent spawns
and base commit capture for deterministic diffs.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.6 <noreply@anthropic.com>
Entire-Checkpoint: 71d4670c34ac

+// IsWingmanEnabled checks if wingman auto-review is enabled in settings.
+// Returns false by default if settings cannot be loaded or the key is missing.
+func IsWingmanEnabled() bool {
+	s, err := Load()
+	if err != nil {
+		return false
+	}
+	return s.IsWingmanEnabled()
+}
+
+// IsWingmanEnabled checks if wingman auto-review is enabled in this settings instance.
+func (s *EntireSettings) IsWingmanEnabled() bool {
+	if s.StrategyOptions == nil {
+		return false
+	}
+	wingmanOpts, ok := s.StrategyOptions["wingman"].(map[string]any)
+	if !ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	enabled, ok := wingmanOpts["enabled"].(bool)
+	if !ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	return enabled
+}
+
 // IsPushSessionsDisabled checks if push_sessions is disabled in settings.
 // Returns true if push_sessions is explicitly set to false.
 func (s *EntireSettings) IsPushSessionsDisabled() bool {
diff --git a/cmd/entire/cli/wingman.go b/cmd/entire/cli/wingman.go
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..a509990f0
--- /dev/null
+++ b/cmd/entire/cli/wingman.go
@@ -0,0 +1,291 @@
+package cli
+
+import (
+	"context"
+	"crypto/sha256"
+	"encoding/hex"
+	"encoding/json"
+	"errors"
+	"fmt"
+	"os"
+	"os/exec"
+	"path/filepath"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+	"time"
+
+	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
+	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/settings"
+	"github.com/spf13/cobra"
+)
+
+// WingmanPayload is the data passed to the detached review subprocess.
+type WingmanPayload struct {
+	SessionID     string   `json:"session_id"`
+	RepoRoot      string   `json:"repo_root"`
+	BaseCommit    string   `json:"base_commit"`
+	ModifiedFiles []string `json:"modified_files"`
+	NewFiles      []string `json:"new_files"`
+	DeletedFiles  []string `json:"deleted_files"`
+	Prompts       []string `json:"prompts"`
+	CommitMessage string   `json:"commit_message"`
+}
+
+// WingmanState tracks deduplication and review state.
+type WingmanState struct {
+	SessionID     string    `json:"session_id"`
+	FilesHash     string    `json:"files_hash"`
+	ReviewedAt    time.Time `json:"reviewed_at"`
+	ReviewApplied bool      `json:"review_applied"`
+}
+
+const (
+	wingmanStateFile  = ".entire/wingman-state.json"
+	wingmanReviewFile = ".entire/REVIEW.md"
+	wingmanLockFile   = ".entire/wingman.lock"
+)
+
+func newWingmanCmd() *cobra.Command {
+	cmd := &cobra.Command{
+		Use:   "wingman",
+		Short: "Automated code review for agent sessions",
+		Long: `Wingman provides automated code review after agent turns.
+
+When enabled, wingman automatically reviews code changes made by agents,
+writes suggestions to .entire/REVIEW.md, and optionally triggers the agent
+to apply them.`,
+		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
+			return cmd.Help()
+		},
+	}
+
+	cmd.AddCommand(newWingmanEnableCmd())
+	cmd.AddCommand(newWingmanDisableCmd())
+	cmd.AddCommand(newWingmanStatusCmd())
+	cmd.AddCommand(newWingmanReviewCmd())
+
+	return cmd
+}
+
+func newWingmanEnableCmd() *cobra.Command {
+	return &cobra.Command{
+		Use:   "enable",
+		Short: "Enable wingman auto-review",
+		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
+			if _, err := paths.RepoRoot(); err != nil {
+				fmt.Fprintln(cmd.ErrOrStderr(), "Not a git repository. Please run from within a git repository.")
+				return NewSilentError(errors.New("not a git repository"))
+			}
+
+			s, err := settings.Load()
+			if err != nil {
+				return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
+			}
+
+			if !s.Enabled {
+				fmt.Fprintln(cmd.ErrOrStderr(), "Entire is not enabled. Run 'entire enable' first.")
+				return NewSilentError(errors.New("entire not enabled"))
+			}
+
+			if s.StrategyOptions == nil {
+				s.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]any)
+			}
+			s.StrategyOptions["wingman"] = map[string]any{"enabled": true}
+
+			if err := settings.Save(s); err != nil {
+				return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
+			}
+
+			fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Wingman enabled. Code changes will be automatically reviewed after agent turns.")
+			return nil
+		},
+	}
+}
+
+func newWingmanDisableCmd() *cobra.Command {
+	return &cobra.Command{
+		Use:   "disable",
+		Short: "Disable wingman auto-review",
+		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
+			s, err := settings.Load()
+			if err != nil {
+				return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
+			}
+
+			if s.StrategyOptions == nil {
+				s.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]any)
+			}
+			s.StrategyOptions["wingman"] = map[string]any{"enabled": false}
+
+			if err := settings.Save(s); err != nil {
+				return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
+			}
+
+			// Clean up pending review file if it exists
+			reviewPath, err := paths.AbsPath(wingmanReviewFile)
+			if err == nil {
+				_ = os.Remove(reviewPath)
+			}
+
+			fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Wingman disabled.")
+			return nil
+		},
+	}
+}
+
+func newWingmanStatusCmd() *cobra.Command {
+	return &cobra.Command{
+		Use:   "status",
+		Short: "Show wingman status",
+		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
+			s, err := settings.Load()
+			if err != nil {
+				return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
+			}
+
+			if s.IsWingmanEnabled() {
+				fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Wingman: enabled")
+			} else {
+				fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Wingman: disabled")
+			}
+
+			// Show last review info if available
+			state, err := loadWingmanState()
+			if err == nil && state != nil {
+				fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Last review: %s\n", state.ReviewedAt.Format(time.RFC3339))
+				if state.ReviewApplied {
+					fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Status: applied")
+				} else {
+					fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Status: pending")
+				}
+			}
+
+			// Check for pending REVIEW.md
+			reviewPath, err := paths.AbsPath(wingmanReviewFile)
+			if err == nil {
+				if _, statErr := os.Stat(reviewPath); statErr == nil {
+					fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Pending review: .entire/REVIEW.md")
+				}
+			}
+
+			return nil
+		},
+	}
+}
+
+// triggerWingmanReview checks preconditions and spawns the detached review process.
+func triggerWingmanReview(payload WingmanPayload) {
+	repoRoot := payload.RepoRoot
+
+	// Check if a pending REVIEW.md already exists
+	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
+	if _, err := os.Stat(reviewPath); err == nil {
+		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Pending review exists, skipping\n")
+		return
+	}
+
+	// Lock file prevents concurrent review spawns. Multiple rapid stop hooks
+	// could race past the dedup check (which reads stale state) before any
+	// review completes and updates the state file.
+	lockPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanLockFile)
+	if _, err := os.Stat(lockPath); err == nil {
+		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review already in progress, skipping\n")
+		return
+	}
+
+	// Dedup check: compute hash of sorted file paths
+	allFiles := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))
+	allFiles = append(allFiles, payload.ModifiedFiles...)
+	allFiles = append(allFiles, payload.NewFiles...)
+	allFiles = append(allFiles, payload.DeletedFiles...)
+	filesHash := computeFilesHash(allFiles)
+
+	state, _ := loadWingmanState() //nolint:errcheck // best-effort dedup
+	if state != nil && state.FilesHash == filesHash && state.SessionID == payload.SessionID {
+		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Already reviewed these changes, skipping\n")
+		return
+	}
+
+	// Capture HEAD at trigger time so the detached review diffs against
+	// the correct commit even if HEAD moves during the initial delay.
+	payload.BaseCommit = resolveHEAD(repoRoot)
+
+	// Write lock file synchronously before spawning to prevent races
+	//nolint:gosec // G306: lock file is not secrets
+	_ = os.WriteFile(lockPath, []byte(payload.SessionID), 0o644) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort lock
+
+	// Marshal payload
+	payloadJSON, err := json.Marshal(payload)
+	if err != nil {
+		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Failed to marshal payload: %v\n", err)
+		_ = os.Remove(lockPath)
+		return
+	}
+
+	// Spawn detached review process
+	spawnDetachedWingmanReview(string(payloadJSON))
+	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review starting in background...\n")
+}
+
+// resolveHEAD returns the current HEAD commit hash, or empty string on error.
+func resolveHEAD(repoRoot string) string {
+	cmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), "git", "rev-parse", "HEAD")
+	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
+	out, err := cmd.Output()
+	if err != nil {
+		return ""
+	}
+	return strings.TrimSpace(string(out))
+}
+
+// computeFilesHash returns a SHA256 hex digest of the sorted file paths.
+func computeFilesHash(files []string) string {
+	sorted := make([]string, len(files))
+	copy(sorted, files)
+	sort.Strings(sorted)
+	h := sha256.Sum256([]byte(strings.Join(sorted, "\n")))
+	return hex.EncodeToString(h[:])
+}
+
+// loadWingmanState loads the wingman state from .entire/wingman-state.json.
+func loadWingmanState() (*WingmanState, error) {
+	statePath, err := paths.AbsPath(wingmanStateFile)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, fmt.Errorf("resolving wingman state path: %w", err)
+	}
+
+	data, err := os.ReadFile(statePath) //nolint:gosec // path is repo-relative
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, fmt.Errorf("reading wingman state: %w", err)
+	}
+
+	var state WingmanState
+	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &state); err != nil {
+		return nil, fmt.Errorf("parsing wingman state: %w", err)
+	}
+	return &state, nil
+}
+
+// saveWingmanState saves the wingman state to .entire/wingman-state.json.
+func saveWingmanState(state *WingmanState) error {
+	statePath, err := paths.AbsPath(wingmanStateFile)
+	if err != nil {
+		return fmt.Errorf("resolving wingman state path: %w", err)
+	}
+
+	dir := filepath.Dir(statePath)
+	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o750); err != nil {
+		return fmt.Errorf("creating wingman state directory: %w", err)
+	}
+
+	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(state, "", "  ")
+	if err != nil {
+		return fmt.Errorf("marshaling wingman state: %w", err)
+	}
+
+	//nolint:gosec // G306: state file is config, not secrets
+	if err := os.WriteFile(statePath, data, 0o644); err != nil {
+		return fmt.Errorf("writing wingman state: %w", err)
+	}
+	return nil
+}

+package cli
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+// maxDiffSize is the maximum size of the diff included in the review prompt.
+// Large diffs degrade review quality, so we truncate.
+const maxDiffSize = 100 * 1024 // 100KB
+
+// reviewPromptTemplate is the prompt sent to Claude for code review.
+//
+// Security note: User content (diff, prompts) is wrapped in XML tags to provide
+// clear boundary markers, similar to the summarization prompt pattern.
+const reviewPromptTemplate = `You are a senior code reviewer. Review the following code changes and provide actionable feedback.
+
+## Context
+
+The developer's intent (from their prompts):
+<prompts>
+%s
+</prompts>
+
+## Code Changes
+
+Files changed: %s
+
+<diff>
+%s
+</diff>
+
+## Instructions
+
+Review the code changes above. Focus on:
+- Bugs or logic errors
+- Security vulnerabilities
+- Performance issues
+- Missing error handling
+- Code style and readability issues
+
+For each issue found, provide:
+1. Severity: CRITICAL, WARNING, or SUGGESTION
+2. File path and line number (from the diff)
+3. Description of the issue
+4. Suggested fix
+
+Format your response as Markdown with this structure:
+
+# Code Review
+
+## Summary
+Brief overview of the changes and overall assessment.
+
+## Issues
+
+### [SEVERITY] Short description
+**File:** ` + "`path/to/file.go:42`" + `
+**Description:** What the issue is and why it matters.
+**Suggestion:**
+` + "```" + `
+// suggested fix
+` + "```" + `
+
+If no issues are found, say so and optionally provide suggestions for improvement.
+Do NOT include any preamble or explanation outside the Markdown structure above.`
+
+// buildReviewPrompt constructs the review prompt from the payload and diff.
```

diff --git a/cmd/entire/cli/agent/prompter.go b/cmd/entire/cli/agent/prompter.go
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..7c7de085b
--- /dev/null
+++ b/cmd/entire/cli/agent/prompter.go
@@ -0,0 +1,63 @@
+package agent
+
+import "context"
+
+// PromptOptions configures how an agent CLI is invoked for a prompt.
+type PromptOptions struct {
+	// Model overrides the default model for this invocation.
+	// If empty, uses the agent's default.
+	Model string
+
+	// WorkDir is the working directory for the CLI subprocess.
+	// If empty, uses os.TempDir() for isolation.
+	WorkDir string
+
+	// AllowedTools restricts which tools the agent can use (e.g., "Read,Glob,Grep").
+	// If empty, no tool restriction is applied.
+	AllowedTools string
+
+	// PermissionMode controls the agent's permission behavior.
+	// Values: "bypassPermissions", "acceptEdits", etc.
+	// If empty, uses the agent's default.
+	PermissionMode string
+
+	// OutputFormat controls the output format (e.g., "json", "text").
+	// If empty, defaults to "json".
+	OutputFormat string
+
+	// IsolateFromGit strips GIT_* and agent-specific env vars to prevent
+	// interference with the parent's git state. Defaults to true when nil.
+	IsolateFromGit *bool
+
+	// ExtraEnv adds additional environment variables to the subprocess.
+	ExtraEnv []string
+}
+
+// PromptResult contains the response from an agent prompt invocation.
+type PromptResult struct {
+	// Text is the extracted text response from the agent.
+	Text string
+}
+
+// Prompter is an optional interface for agents that support CLI-based prompting.
+// Agents implementing this interface can be invoked with a prompt string and return
+// a text response. This powers features like wingman review and summarization.
+//
+// This follows the same optional interface pattern as TranscriptAnalyzer,
+// TokenCalculator, etc. Use a type assertion to check if an agent supports it:
+//
+//	if prompter, ok := ag.(agent.Prompter); ok {
+//	    result, err := prompter.Prompt(ctx, prompt, opts)
+//	}
+type Prompter interface {
+	Agent
+
+	// Prompt sends a prompt to the agent CLI and returns the text response.
+	// The implementation handles CLI invocation, environment isolation,
+	// output parsing, and error handling.
+	Prompt(ctx context.Context, prompt string, opts PromptOptions) (*PromptResult, error)
+
+	// CLICommand returns the CLI executable name for this agent (e.g., "claude").
+	// Used for error messages and logging.
+	CLICommand() string
+}

+// reviewHasActionableIssues reads a REVIEW.md and checks if it contains
+// actionable issue markers ([CRITICAL], [WARNING], or [SUGGESTION]).
+// Returns false if the file can't be read or contains no issues.
+func reviewHasActionableIssues(reviewPath string) bool {
+	data, err := os.ReadFile(reviewPath) //nolint:gosec // path is from repo-relative constant
+	if err != nil {
+		return false
+	}
+
+	content := string(data)
+
+	// The review format uses "### [SEVERITY] description" for each issue.
+	// Check for any of the severity markers in heading context.
+	for _, marker := range []string{"[CRITICAL]", "[WARNING]", "[SUGGESTION]"} {
+		if strings.Contains(content, marker) {
+			return true
+		}
+	}
+
+	return false
+}
+
 // outputWingmanStopNotification outputs a systemMessage notification about
 // wingman status at the end of a stop hook. This makes wingman activity visible
 // in the agent terminal without injecting context into the agent's conversation.

From 94a7206f68848d3bcf91fd4517c17abb60863da7 Mon Sep 17 00:00:00 2001
From: Daniel Adams <daniel@entire.io>
Date: Wed, 18 Feb 2026 16:23:39 +0100
Subject: [PATCH 36/44] feat: add shared CLI helpers for agent subprocess
 invocation

Consolidate duplicated code from wingman and summarize into
agent/cli_helpers.go: CLIResponse struct, StripGitEnv(), and
FormatExecError(). This eliminates identical implementations
of stripGitEnv and CLI response types across packages.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.6 <noreply@anthropic.com>
Entire-Checkpoint: fd5d1beba299
---
 cmd/entire/cli/agent/cli_helpers.go      |  44 ++++++++++
 cmd/entire/cli/agent/cli_helpers_test.go | 102 +++++++++++++++++++++++
 2 files changed, 146 insertions(+)

diff --git a/cmd/entire/cli/agent/cli_helpers.go b/cmd/entire/cli/agent/cli_helpers.go
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..f758334e1
--- /dev/null
+++ b/cmd/entire/cli/agent/cli_helpers.go
@@ -0,0 +1,44 @@
+package agent
+
+import (
+	"errors"
+	"fmt"
+	"os/exec"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+// CLIResponse represents the JSON response from agent CLIs that support --output-format json.
+// Used by both wingman review and summarization.
+type CLIResponse struct {
+	Result string `json:"result"`
+}
+
+// StripGitEnv returns a copy of env with all GIT_* variables removed and
+// agent-specific nesting-detection variables unset (e.g., CLAUDECODE).
+// GIT_* removal prevents a subprocess from discovering or modifying the
+// parent's git repo. CLAUDECODE removal prevents the Claude CLI from
+// refusing to start due to nested-session detection.
+func StripGitEnv(env []string) []string {
+	filtered := make([]string, 0, len(env))
+	for _, e := range env {
+		if strings.HasPrefix(e, "GIT_") || strings.HasPrefix(e, "CLAUDECODE=") {
+			continue
+		}
+		filtered = append(filtered, e)
+	}
+	return filtered
+}
+
+// FormatExecError returns a human-readable error message for exec failures.
+// Handles both "command not found" (exec.Error) and non-zero exit (exec.ExitError).
+func FormatExecError(err error, cliName, stderr string) error {
+	var execErr *exec.Error
+	if errors.As(err, &execErr) {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%s CLI not found: %w", cliName, err)
+	}
+	var exitErr *exec.ExitError
+	if errors.As(err, &exitErr) {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%s CLI failed (exit %d): %s", cliName, exitErr.ExitCode(), stderr)
+	}
+	return fmt.Errorf("failed to run %s CLI: %w", cliName, err)
+}

+package agent
+
+import (
+	"context"
+	"errors"
+	"os/exec"
+	"strings"
+	"testing"
+)
+
+func TestStripGitEnv(t *testing.T) {
+	t.Parallel()
+
+	env := []string{
+		"HOME=/Users/test",
+		"GIT_DIR=/repo/.git",
+		"PATH=/usr/bin",
+		"GIT_WORK_TREE=/repo",
+		"GIT_INDEX_FILE=/repo/.git/index",
+		"SHELL=/bin/zsh",
+		"CLAUDECODE=1",
+	}
+
+	filtered := StripGitEnv(env)
+
+	expected := []string{
+		"HOME=/Users/test",
+		"PATH=/usr/bin",
+		"SHELL=/bin/zsh",
+	}
+
+	if len(filtered) != len(expected) {
+		t.Fatalf("got %d entries, want %d", len(filtered), len(expected))

     1→package agent
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"fmt"
     5→	"slices"
     6→	"sync"
     7→)
     8→
     9→var (
    10→	registryMu sync.RWMutex
    11→	registry   = make(map[AgentName]Factory)
    12→)
    13→
    14→// Factory creates a new agent instance
    15→type Factory func() Agent
    16→
    17→// Register adds an agent factory to the registry.
    18→// Called from init() in each agent implementation.
    19→func Register(name AgentName, factory Factory) {
    20→	registryMu.Lock()
    21→	defer registryMu.Unlock()
    22→	registry[name] = factory
    23→}
    24→
    25→// Get retrieves an agent by name.
    26→//
    27→
    28→func Get(name AgentName) (Agent, error) {
    29→	registryMu.RLock()
    30→	defer registryMu.RUnlock()
    31→
    32→	factory, ok := registry[name]
    33→	if !ok {
    34→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("unknown agent: %s (available: %v)", name, List())
    35→	}
    36→	return factory(), nil
    37→}
    38→
    39→// List returns all registered agent names in sorted order.
    40→func List() []AgentName {
    41→	registryMu.RLock()
    42→	defer registryMu.RUnlock()
    43→
    44→	names := make([]AgentName, 0, len(registry))
    45→	for name := range registry {
    46→		names = append(names, name)
    47→	}
    48→	slices.Sort(names)
    49→	return names
    50→}
    51→
    52→// DetectAll returns all agents whose DetectPresence reports true.
    53→// Agents are checked in sorted name order (via List()) for deterministic results.
    54→// Returns an empty slice when no agent is detected.
    55→func DetectAll() []Agent {
    56→	names := List() // sorted, lock-safe
    57→
    58→	var detected []Agent
    59→	for _, name := range names {
    60→		ag, err := Get(name)
    61→		if err != nil {
    62→			continue
    63→		}
    64→		if present, err := ag.DetectPresence(); err == nil && present {
    65→			detected = append(detected, ag)
    66→		}
    67→	}
    68→	return detected
    69→}
    70→
    71→// Detect attempts to auto-detect which agent is being used.
    72→// Iterates registered agents in sorted name order for deterministic results.
    73→// Returns the first agent whose DetectPresence reports true.
    74→func Detect() (Agent, error) {
    75→	detected := DetectAll()
    76→	if len(detected) == 0 {
    77→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no agent detected (available: %v)", List())
    78→	}
    79→	return detected[0], nil
    80→}
    81→
    82→// AgentName is the registry key type for agents (e.g., "claude-code", "gemini").
    83→//
    84→//nolint:revive // stuttering is intentional - distinguishes from AgentType when both are used
    85→type AgentName string
    86→
    87→// AgentType is the display name type stored in metadata/trailers (e.g., "Claude Code", "Gemini CLI").
    88→//
    89→//nolint:revive // stuttering is intentional - distinguishes from AgentName when both are used
    90→type AgentType string
    91→
    92→// Agent name constants (registry keys)
    93→const (
    94→	AgentNameClaudeCode AgentName = "claude-code"
    95→	AgentNameGemini     AgentName = "gemini"
    96→	AgentNameOpenCode   AgentName = "opencode"
    97→)
    98→
    99→// Agent type constants (type identifiers stored in metadata/trailers)
   100→const (
   101→	AgentTypeClaudeCode AgentType = "Claude Code"
   102→	AgentTypeGemini     AgentType = "Gemini CLI"
   103→	AgentTypeOpenCode   AgentType = "OpenCode"
   104→	AgentTypeUnknown    AgentType = "Agent" // Fallback for backwards compatibility
   105→)
   106→
   107→// DefaultAgentName is the registry key for the default agent.
   108→const DefaultAgentName AgentName = AgentNameClaudeCode
   109→
   110→// GetByAgentType retrieves an agent by its type identifier.
   111→//
   112→// Note: This uses a linear search that instantiates agents until a match is found.
   113→// This is acceptable because:
   114→//   - Agent count is small (~2-20 agents)
   115→//   - Agent factories are lightweight (empty struct allocation)
   116→//   - Called infrequently (commit hooks, rewind, debug commands - not hot paths)
   117→//   - Cost is ~400ns worst case vs milliseconds for I/O operations
   118→//
   119→// Only optimize if agent count exceeds 100 or profiling shows this as a bottleneck.
   120→func GetByAgentType(agentType AgentType) (Agent, error) {
   121→	registryMu.RLock()
   122→	defer registryMu.RUnlock()
   123→
   124→	for _, factory := range registry {
   125→		ag := factory()
   126→		if ag.Type() == agentType {
   127→			return ag, nil
   128→		}
   129→	}
   130→
   131→	return nil, fmt.Errorf("unknown agent type: %s", agentType)
   132→}
   133→
   134→// AllProtectedDirs returns the union of ProtectedDirs from all registered agents.
   135→func AllProtectedDirs() []string {
   136→	// Copy factories under the lock, then release before calling external code.
   137→	registryMu.RLock()
   138→	factories := make([]Factory, 0, len(registry))
   139→	for _, f := range registry {
   140→		factories = append(factories, f)
   141→	}
   142→	registryMu.RUnlock()
   143→
   144→	seen := make(map[string]struct{})
   145→	var dirs []string
   146→	for _, factory := range factories {
   147→		for _, d := range factory().ProtectedDirs() {
   148→			if _, ok := seen[d]; !ok {
   149→				seen[d] = struct{}{}
   150→				dirs = append(dirs, d)
   151→			}
   152→		}
   153→	}
   154→	slices.Sort(dirs)
   155→	return dirs
   156→}
   157→
   158→// Default returns the default agent.
   159→// Returns nil if the default agent is not registered.
   160→//
   161→//nolint:errcheck // Factory pattern returns interface; error is acceptable to ignore for default
   162→func Default() Agent {
   163→	a, _ := Get(DefaultAgentName)
   164→	return a
   165→}
   166→

     1→// Package agent provides interfaces and types for integrating with coding agents.
     2→// It abstracts agent-specific behavior (hooks, log parsing, session storage) so that
     3→// the same Strategy implementations can work with any coding agent.
     4→package agent
     5→
     6→import (
     7→	"io"
     8→)
     9→
    10→// Agent defines the interface for interacting with a coding agent.
    11→// Each agent implementation (Claude Code, Cursor, Aider, etc.) converts its
    12→// native format to the normalized types defined in this package.
    13→//
    14→// The interface is organized into four groups:
    15→//
    16→//   - Identity (5 methods): Name, Type, Description, DetectPresence, ProtectedDirs
    17→//   - Event Mapping (2 methods): HookNames, ParseHookEvent
    18→//   - Transcript Storage (3 methods): ReadTranscript, ChunkTranscript, ReassembleTranscript
    19→//   - Legacy (8 methods): Will be moved to optional interfaces or removed in a future phase
    20→type Agent interface {
    21→	// --- Identity ---
    22→
    23→	// Name returns the agent registry key (e.g., "claude-code", "gemini")
    24→	Name() AgentName
    25→
    26→	// Type returns the agent type identifier (e.g., "Claude Code", "Gemini CLI")
    27→	// This is stored in metadata and trailers.
    28→	Type() AgentType
    29→
    30→	// Description returns a human-readable description for UI
    31→	Description() string
    32→
    33→	// IsPreview returns whether the agent integration is in preview or stable
    34→	IsPreview() bool
    35→
    36→	// DetectPresence checks if this agent is configured in the repository
    37→	DetectPresence() (bool, error)
    38→
    39→	// ProtectedDirs returns repo-root-relative directories that should never be
    40→	// modified or deleted during rewind or other destructive operations.
    41→	// Examples: [".claude"] for Claude, [".gemini"] for Gemini.
    42→	ProtectedDirs() []string
    43→
    44→	// --- Event Mapping ---
    45→
    46→	// HookNames returns the hook verbs this agent supports.
    47→	// These become subcommands under `entire hooks <agent>`.
    48→	// e.g., ["stop", "user-prompt-submit", "session-start", "session-end"]
    49→	HookNames() []string
    50→
    51→	// ParseHookEvent translates an agent-native hook into a normalized lifecycle Event.
    52→	// Returns nil if the hook has no lifecycle significance (e.g., pass-through hooks).
    53→	// This is the core contribution surface for new agent implementations.
    54→	ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*Event, error)
    55→
    56→	// --- Transcript Storage ---
    57→
    58→	// ReadTranscript reads the raw transcript bytes for a session.
    59→	ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error)
    60→
    61→	// ChunkTranscript splits a transcript into chunks if it exceeds maxSize.
    62→	// Returns a slice of chunks. If the transcript fits in one chunk, returns single-element slice.
    63→	// The chunking is format-aware: JSONL splits at line boundaries, JSON splits message arrays.
    64→	ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error)
    65→
    66→	// ReassembleTranscript combines chunks back into a single transcript.
    67→	// Handles format-specific reassembly (JSONL concatenation, JSON message merging).
    68→	ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error)
    69→
    70→	// --- Legacy methods (will move to optional interfaces in Phase 4) ---
    71→
    72→	// GetSessionID extracts session ID from hook input.
    73→	GetSessionID(input *HookInput) string
    74→
    75→	// GetSessionDir returns where agent stores session data for this repo.
    76→	GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error)
    77→
    78→	// ResolveSessionFile returns the path to the session transcript file.
    79→	ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string
    80→
    81→	// ReadSession reads session data from agent's storage.
    82→	ReadSession(input *HookInput) (*AgentSession, error)
    83→
    84→	// WriteSession writes session data for resumption.
    85→	WriteSession(session *AgentSession) error
    86→
    87→	// FormatResumeCommand returns command to resume a session.
    88→	FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string
    89→}
    90→
    91→// HookSupport is implemented by agents with lifecycle hooks.
    92→// This optional interface allows agents like Claude Code and Cursor to
    93→// install and manage hooks that notify Entire of agent events.
    94→type HookSupport interface {
    95→	Agent
    96→
    97→	// InstallHooks installs agent-specific hooks.
    98→	// If localDev is true, hooks point to local development build.
    99→	// If force is true, removes existing Entire hooks before installing.
   100→	// Returns the number of hooks installed.
   101→	InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error)
   102→
   103→	// UninstallHooks removes installed hooks
   104→	UninstallHooks() error
   105→
   106→	// AreHooksInstalled checks if hooks are currently installed
   107→	AreHooksInstalled() bool
   108→}
   109→
   110→// FileWatcher is implemented by agents that use file-based detection.
   111→// Agents like Aider that don't support hooks can use file watching
   112→// to detect session activity.
   113→type FileWatcher interface {
   114→	Agent
   115→
   116→	// GetWatchPaths returns paths to watch for session changes
   117→	GetWatchPaths() ([]string, error)
   118→
   119→	// OnFileChange handles a detected file change and returns session info
   120→	OnFileChange(path string) (*SessionChange, error)
   121→}
   122→
   123→// TranscriptAnalyzer provides format-specific transcript parsing.
   124→// Agents that implement this get richer checkpoints (transcript-derived file lists,
   125→// prompts, summaries). Agents that don't still participate in the checkpoint lifecycle
   126→// via git-status-based file detection and raw transcript storage.
   127→type TranscriptAnalyzer interface {
   128→	Agent
   129→
   130→	// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current position (length) of a transcript.
   131→	// For JSONL formats (Claude Code), this is the line count.
   132→	// For JSON formats (Gemini CLI), this is the message count.
   133→	// Returns 0 if the file doesn't exist or is empty.
   134→	GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error)
   135→
   136→	// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given offset.
   137→	// For JSONL formats (Claude Code), offset is the starting line number.
   138→	// For JSON formats (Gemini CLI), offset is the starting message index.
   139→	// Returns:
   140→	//   - files: list of file paths modified by the agent (from Write/Edit tools)
   141→	//   - currentPosition: the current position (line count or message count)
   142→	//   - error: any error encountered during reading
   143→	ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error)
   144→
   145→	// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompts from the transcript starting at the given offset.
   146→	ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error)
   147→
   148→	// ExtractSummary extracts a summary of the session from the transcript.
   149→	ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error)
   150→}
   151→
   152→// TranscriptPreparer is called before ReadTranscript to handle agent-specific
   153→// flush/sync requirements (e.g., Claude Code's async transcript writing).
   154→// The framework calls PrepareTranscript before ReadTranscript if implemented.
   155→type TranscriptPreparer interface {
   156→	Agent
   157→
   158→	// PrepareTranscript ensures the transcript is ready to read.
   159→	// For Claude Code, this waits for the async transcript flush to complete.
   160→	PrepareTranscript(sessionRef string) error
   161→}
   162→
   163→// TokenCalculator provides token usage calculation for a session.
   164→// The framework calls this during step save and checkpoint if implemented.
   165→type TokenCalculator interface {
   166→	Agent
   167→
   168→	// CalculateTokenUsage computes token usage from the transcript starting at the given offset.
   169→	CalculateTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) (*TokenUsage, error)
   170→}
   171→
   172→// SubagentAwareExtractor provides methods for extracting files and tokens including subagents.
   173→// Agents that support spawning subagents (like Claude Code's Task tool) should implement this
   174→// to ensure subagent contributions are included in checkpoints.
   175→type SubagentAwareExtractor interface {
   176→	Agent
   177→
   178→	// ExtractAllModifiedFiles extracts files modified by both the main agent and any spawned subagents.
   179→	// The subagentsDir parameter specifies where subagent transcripts are stored.
   180→	// Returns a deduplicated list of all modified file paths.
   181→	ExtractAllModifiedFiles(sessionRef string, fromOffset int, subagentsDir string) ([]string, error)
   182→
   183→	// CalculateTotalTokenUsage computes token usage including all spawned subagents.

This powers features like trail runner and automated review.\n//\n// This follows the same optional interface pattern as TranscriptAnalyzer,\n// TokenCalculator, etc. Use a type assertion to check if an agent supports it:\n//\n//\tif prompter, ok := ag.(agent.Prompter); ok {\n//\t    result, err := prompter.Prompt(ctx, prompt, opts)\n//\t}\ntype Prompter interface {\n\tAgent\n\n\t// Prompt sends a prompt to the agent CLI and returns the text response.\n\t// The implementation handles CLI invocation, environment isolation,\n\t// output parsing, and error handling.\n\tPrompt(ctx context.Context, prompt string, opts PromptOptions) (*PromptResult, error)\n\n\t// CLICommand returns the CLI executable name for this agent (e.g., \"claude\").\n\t// Used for error messages and logging.\n\tCLICommand() string\n}\n"
}

   105→// ReadSession reads a session from Claude's storage (JSONL transcript file).
   106→// The session data is stored in NativeData as raw JSONL bytes.
   107→// ModifiedFiles is computed by parsing the transcript.
   108→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReadSession(input *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   109→	if input.SessionRef == "" {
   110→		return nil, errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   111→	}
   112→
   113→	// Read the raw JSONL file
   114→	data, err := os.ReadFile(input.SessionRef)
   115→	if err != nil {
   116→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   117→	}
   118→
   119→	// Parse to extract computed fields
   120→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(data)
   121→	if err != nil {
   122→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   123→	}
   124→
   125→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   126→		SessionID:     input.SessionID,
   127→		AgentName:     c.Name(),
   128→		SessionRef:    input.SessionRef,
   129→		StartTime:     time.Now(),
   130→		NativeData:    data,
   131→		ModifiedFiles: ExtractModifiedFiles(lines),
   132→	}, nil
   133→}
   134→
   135→// WriteSession writes a session to Claude's storage (JSONL transcript file).
   136→// Uses the NativeData field which contains raw JSONL bytes.
   137→// The session must have been created by Claude Code (AgentName check).
   138→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) WriteSession(session *agent.AgentSession) error {
   139→	if session == nil {
   140→		return errors.New("session is nil")
   141→	}
   142→
   143→	// Verify this session belongs to Claude Code
   144→	if session.AgentName != "" && session.AgentName != c.Name() {
   145→		return fmt.Errorf("session belongs to agent %q, not %q", session.AgentName, c.Name())
   146→	}
   147→
   148→	if session.SessionRef == "" {
   149→		return errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   150→	}
   151→
   152→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   153→		return errors.New("session has no native data to write")
   154→	}
   155→
   156→	// Write the raw JSONL data
   157→	if err := os.WriteFile(session.SessionRef, session.NativeData, 0o600); err != nil {
   158→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   159→	}
   160→
   161→	return nil
   162→}

   172→// Requires NativeData to be populated (call ReadSession first).
   173→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetLastUserPrompt(session *agent.AgentSession) string {
   174→	if session == nil || len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   175→		return ""

   185→
   186→// TruncateAtUUID returns a new session truncated at the given UUID (inclusive).
   187→// This is used for rewind operations to restore transcript state.
   188→// Requires NativeData to be populated.
   189→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) TruncateAtUUID(session *agent.AgentSession, uuid string) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   190→	if session == nil {
   191→		return nil, errors.New("session is nil")
   192→	}
   193→
   194→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   195→		return nil, errors.New("session has no native data")
   196→	}
   197→
   198→	if uuid == "" {
   199→		// No truncation needed, return copy
   200→		return &agent.AgentSession{
   201→			SessionID:     session.SessionID,
   202→			AgentName:     session.AgentName,
   203→			RepoPath:      session.RepoPath,
   204→			SessionRef:    session.SessionRef,
   205→			StartTime:     session.StartTime,
   206→			NativeData:    session.NativeData,
   207→			ModifiedFiles: session.ModifiedFiles,
   208→		}, nil
   209→	}
   210→
   211→	// Parse, truncate, re-serialize
   212→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(session.NativeData)
   213→	if err != nil {
   214→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   215→	}
   216→
   217→	truncated := TruncateAtUUID(lines, uuid)
   218→
   219→	newData, err := SerializeTranscript(truncated)
   220→	if err != nil {
   221→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to serialize truncated transcript: %w", err)
   222→	}
   223→
   224→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   225→		SessionID:     session.SessionID,
   226→		AgentName:     session.AgentName,
   227→		RepoPath:      session.RepoPath,
   228→		SessionRef:    session.SessionRef,
   229→		StartTime:     session.StartTime,
   230→		NativeData:    newData,
   231→		ModifiedFiles: ExtractModifiedFiles(truncated),
   232→	}, nil
   233→}
   234→
   235→// FindCheckpointUUID finds the UUID of the message containing the tool_result
   236→// for the given tool use ID. Used for task checkpoint rewind.
   237→// Returns the UUID and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.
   238→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) FindCheckpointUUID(session *agent.AgentSession, toolUseID string) (string, bool) {
   239→	if session == nil || len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   240→		return "", false
   241→	}
   242→
   243→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(session.NativeData)
   244→	if err != nil {
   245→		return "", false
   246→	}
