   248→	return FindCheckpointUUID(lines, toolUseID)
   249→}
   250→
   251→// ReadSessionFromPath is a convenience method that reads a session directly from a file path.
   252→// This is useful when you have the path but not a HookInput.
   253→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReadSessionFromPath(transcriptPath, sessionID string) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   254→	return c.ReadSession(&agent.HookInput{
   255→		SessionID:  sessionID,
   256→		SessionRef: transcriptPath,
   257→	})
   258→}
   259→
   260→// SanitizePathForClaude converts a path to Claude's project directory format.
   261→// Claude replaces any non-alphanumeric character with a dash.
   262→var nonAlphanumericRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`[^a-zA-Z0-9]`)
   263→
   264→func SanitizePathForClaude(path string) string {
   265→	return nonAlphanumericRegex.ReplaceAllString(path, "-")
   266→}
   267→
   268→// TranscriptAnalyzer interface implementation
   269→
   270→// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current line count of a Claude Code transcript.
   271→// Claude Code uses JSONL format, so position is the number of lines.
   272→// This is a lightweight operation that only counts lines without parsing JSON.
   273→// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines (no size limit).
   274→// Returns 0 if the file doesn't exist or is empty.
   275→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {
   276→	if path == "" {
   277→		return 0, nil
   278→	}
   279→
   280→	file, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from Claude Code transcript location
   281→	if err != nil {
   282→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   283→			return 0, nil
   284→		}
   285→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to open transcript file: %w", err)
   286→	}
   287→	defer file.Close()
   288→
   289→	reader := bufio.NewReader(file)
   290→	lineCount := 0
   291→
   292→	for {
   293→		line, err := reader.ReadBytes('\n')
   294→		if err != nil {
   295→			if err == io.EOF {
   296→				if len(line) > 0 {
   297→					lineCount++ // Count final line without trailing newline
   298→				}
   299→				break
   300→			}
   301→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   302→		}
   303→		lineCount++
   304→	}
   305→
   306→	return lineCount, nil
   307→}
   308→
   309→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given line number.
   310→// For Claude Code (JSONL format), offset is the starting line number.
   311→// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines (no size limit).
   312→// Returns:
   313→//   - files: list of file paths modified by Claude (from Write/Edit tools)
   314→//   - currentPosition: total number of lines in the file
   315→//   - error: any error encountered during reading
   316→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error) {
   317→	if path == "" {
   318→		return nil, 0, nil
   319→	}
   320→
   321→	file, openErr := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from Claude Code transcript location
   322→	if openErr != nil {
   323→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to open transcript file: %w", openErr)
   324→	}
   325→	defer file.Close()
   326→
   327→	reader := bufio.NewReader(file)
   328→	var lines []TranscriptLine
   329→	lineNum := 0
   330→
   331→	for {
   332→		lineData, readErr := reader.ReadBytes('\n')
   333→		if readErr != nil && readErr != io.EOF {
   334→			return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", readErr)
   335→		}
   336→
   337→		if len(lineData) > 0 {
   338→			lineNum++
   339→			if lineNum > startOffset {
   340→				var line TranscriptLine
   341→				if parseErr := json.Unmarshal(lineData, &line); parseErr == nil {
   342→					lines = append(lines, line)
   343→				}
   344→				// Skip malformed lines silently
   345→			}
   346→		}
   347→
   348→		if readErr == io.EOF {
   349→			break
   350→		}
   351→	}
   352→
   353→	return ExtractModifiedFiles(lines), lineNum, nil
   354→}
   355→
   356→// ChunkTranscript splits a JSONL transcript at line boundaries.
   357→// Claude Code uses JSONL format (one JSON object per line), so chunking
   358→// is done at newline boundaries to preserve message integrity.
   359→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
   360→	chunks, err := agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
   361→	if err != nil {
   362→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to chunk JSONL transcript: %w", err)
   363→	}
   364→	return chunks, nil
   365→}
   366→
   367→// ReassembleTranscript concatenates JSONL chunks with newlines.
   368→//
   369→
   370→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
   371→	return agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks), nil
   372→}
   373→

   378→// Returns a deduplicated list of all modified file paths.
   379→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ExtractAllModifiedFiles(sessionRef string, fromOffset int, subagentsDir string) ([]string, error) {
   380→	return ExtractAllModifiedFiles(sessionRef, fromOffset, subagentsDir)
   381→}
   382→
   383→// CalculateTotalTokenUsage computes token usage including all spawned subagents.
   384→// Claude Code spawns subagents via the Task tool; their transcripts are stored in subagentsDir.
   385→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) CalculateTotalTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int, subagentsDir string) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   386→	return CalculateTotalTokenUsage(sessionRef, fromOffset, subagentsDir)
   387→}

(\n\t\"bytes\"\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"errors\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"os/exec\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent\"\n)\n\n// Ensure ClaudeCodeAgent implements Prompter at compile time.\nvar _ agent.Prompter = (*ClaudeCodeAgent)(nil)\n\n// cliResponse represents the JSON response from the Claude CLI --output-format json.\ntype cliResponse struct {\n\tResult string `json:\"result\"`\n}\n\n// CLICommand returns the CLI executable name for Claude Code.\nfunc (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) CLICommand() string {\n\treturn \"claude\"\n}\n\n// Prompt sends a prompt to the Claude CLI and returns the text response.\nfunc (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) Prompt(ctx context.Context, prompt string, opts agent.PromptOptions) (*agent.PromptResult, error) {\n\targs := []string{\"--print\"}\n\n\t// Add model if specified\n\tif opts.Model != \"\" {\n\t\targs = append(args, \"--model\", opts.Model)\n\t}\n\n\t// Add output format\n\toutputFormat := opts.OutputFormat\n\tif outputFormat == \"\" {\n\t\toutputFormat = \"json\"\n\t}\n\targs = append(args, \"--output-format\", outputFormat)\n\n\t// Add allowed tools\n\tif opts.AllowedTools != \"\" {\n\t\targs = append(args, \"--allowedTools\", opts.AllowedTools)\n\t}\n\n\t// Add permission mode\n\tif opts.PermissionMode != \"\" {\n\t\targs = append(args, \"--permission-mode\", opts.PermissionMode)\n\t}\n\n\t// Disable settings sources to prevent hook loops\n\targs = append(args, \"--setting-sources\", \"\")\n\n\t//nolint:gosec // args are constructed from trusted config\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, c.CLICommand(), args...)\n\n\t// Set working directory\n\tif opts.WorkDir != \"\" {\n\t\tcmd.Dir = opts.WorkDir\n\t}\n\n\t// Set up environment\n\tenv := os.Environ()\n\t// Strip GIT_* and CLAUDECODE env vars to prevent interference\n\tenv = stripGitEnv(env)\n\t// Add any extra env vars\n\tenv = append(env, opts.ExtraEnv...)\n\tcmd.Env = env\n\n\t// Pass prompt via stdin\n\tcmd.Stdin = strings.NewReader(prompt)\n\n\tvar stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer\n\tcmd.Stdout = &stdout\n\tcmd.Stderr = &stderr\n\n\terr := cmd.Run()\n\n\tresult := &agent.PromptResult{\n\t\tExitCode: 0,\n\t}\n\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tvar execErr *exec.Error\n\t\tif errors.As(err, &execErr) {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"claude CLI not found: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tvar exitErr *exec.ExitError\n\t\tif errors.As(err, &exitErr) {\n\t\t\tresult.ExitCode = exitErr.ExitCode()\n\t\t\treturn result, fmt.Errorf(\"claude CLI failed (exit %d): %s\", exitErr.ExitCode(), stderr.String())\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to run claude CLI: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Parse JSON response if using json output format\n\tif outputFormat == \"json\" {\n\t\tvar cliResp cliResponse\n\t\tif jsonErr := json.Unmarshal(stdout.Bytes(), &cliResp); jsonErr == nil {\n\t\t\tresult.Text = cliResp.Result\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t// Fall back to raw output if JSON parsing fails\n\t\t\tresult.Text = stdout.String()\n\t\t}\n\t} else {\n\t\tresult.Text = stdout.String()\n\t}\n\n\treturn result, nil\n}\n\n// stripGitEnv removes GIT_* and CLAUDECODE env vars to prevent interference\n// with the parent's git state and Claude's nested-session detection.\nfunc stripGitEnv(env []string) []string {\n\tfiltered := make([]string, 0, len(env))\n\tfor _, e := range env {\n\t\tif strings.HasPrefix(e, \"GIT_\") || strings.HasPrefix(e, \"CLAUDECODE=\") {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tfiltered = append(filtered, e)\n\t}\n\treturn filtered\n}\n"

> AGENT

I see the issue - the trail implementation from our earlier discussion wasn't committed and only exists in the conversation context. The branch `soph/validate-command` only contains the validate command, not the trail runner.

Looking at PR 302's pattern, the key insights for how the feedback loop should work:

1. **Prompter interface**: Agents implement `Prompt(ctx, prompt, opts)` and `CLICommand()` to be invocable
2. **Configuration**: Agent/model are configurable via settings (`strategy_options.wingman.agent/model`)
3. **Feedback injection**: When validation fails, the feedback is injected as additional context to the prompt

I've added the `Prompter` interface and Claude Code implementation (those are the untracked files). Should I:

1. **Recreate the trail package** using the plan from the context and integrate it with the Prompter interface
2. **Just focus on the Prompter interface** for now, so it's ready for trail runner when that's implemented

Which would you prefer?

    21→// RunnerConfig holds configuration for the trail runner.
    22→type RunnerConfig struct {
    23→	// PollInterval is how often to check for new trails in daemon mode.
    24→	PollInterval time.Duration
    25→
    26→	// MaxConcurrent is the maximum number of trails to run concurrently.
    27→	// Currently only 1 is supported.
    28→	MaxConcurrent int
    29→
    30→	// Daemon enables continuous polling mode.
    31→	Daemon bool
    32→
    33→	// Agent is the agent CLI to use (e.g., "claude").
    34→	Agent string
    35→
    36→	// Model is the optional model override for the agent.
    37→	Model string
    38→
    39→	// Timeout is the maximum time to wait for a single trail execution.
    40→	Timeout time.Duration
    41→
    42→	// DryRun prevents actual execution, just prints what would be done.
    43→	DryRun bool
    44→
    45→	// MaxAttempts is the maximum number of agent attempts before marking as failed.
    46→	// Each attempt runs the agent, then validates. If validation fails, the agent
    47→	// is run again with feedback about what failed.
    48→	MaxAttempts int
    49→
    50→	// EntireCLI is the path to the entire CLI binary for running validation.
    51→	// Defaults to "entire".
    52→	EntireCLI string
    53→}
    54→
    55→// DefaultRunnerConfig returns the default runner configuration.
    56→func DefaultRunnerConfig() RunnerConfig {
    57→	return RunnerConfig{
    58→		PollInterval:  30 * time.Second,
    59→		MaxConcurrent: 1,
    60→		Daemon:        false,
    61→		Agent:         "claude",
    62→		Model:         "",
    63→		Timeout:       30 * time.Minute,
    64→		DryRun:        false,
    65→		MaxAttempts:   3,
    66→		EntireCLI:     "entire",
    67→	}
    68→}
    69→
    70→// Runner executes trails by discovering open tasks, claiming them,
    71→// creating worktrees, running agents, and updating state.
    72→type Runner struct {
    73→	repo      *git.Repository
    74→	repoRoot  string
    75→	store     *Store
    76→	state     *StateManager
    77→	discovery *Discovery
    78→	worktree  *WorktreeManager
    79→	config    RunnerConfig
    80→}
    81→
    82→// NewRunner creates a new trail runner.
    83→func NewRunner(repo *git.Repository, repoRoot string, config RunnerConfig) *Runner {
    84→	return &Runner{
    85→		repo:      repo,
    86→		repoRoot:  repoRoot,
    87→		store:     NewStore(repo),
    88→		state:     NewStateManager(repo),
    89→		discovery: NewDiscovery(repo),
    90→		worktree:  NewWorktreeManager(repoRoot),
    91→		config:    config,
    92→	}
    93→}
    94→
    95→// RunResult holds the result of running a single trail.
    96→type RunResult struct {
    97→	Trail            *Trail
    98→	Success          bool
    99→	Output           string
   100→	Error            error
   101→	Duration         time.Duration
   102→	Attempts         int
   103→	ValidationPassed bool
   104→}
   105→
   106→// validationResult mirrors the ValidationResult from the validate command.
   107→type validationResult struct {
   108→	Passed   bool          `json:"passed"`
   109→	Duration time.Duration `json:"duration"`
   110→}
   111→
   112→// Start runs the trail runner in daemon mode, continuously polling for new trails.
   113→func (r *Runner) Start(ctx context.Context) error {
   114→	logging.Info(ctx, "trail runner started",
   115→		slog.Duration("poll_interval", r.config.PollInterval),
   116→		slog.Bool("daemon", r.config.Daemon),
   117→	)
   118→
   119→	for {
   120→		// Run one iteration
   121→		results, err := r.RunOnce(ctx)
   122→		if err != nil {
   123→			logging.Error(ctx, "runner iteration failed", slog.String("error", err.Error()))
   124→		}
   125→
   126→		for _, result := range results {
   127→			if result.Success {
   128→				logging.Info(ctx, "trail completed successfully",
   129→					slog.String("trail_id", result.Trail.ID.String()),
   130→					slog.Duration("duration", result.Duration),
   131→				)
   132→			} else {
   133→				logging.Error(ctx, "trail failed",
   134→					slog.String("trail_id", result.Trail.ID.String()),
   135→					slog.String("error", result.Error.Error()),
   136→				)
   137→			}
   138→		}
   139→
   140→		if !r.config.Daemon {
   141→			return nil
   142→		}
   143→
   144→		// Wait before next poll
   145→		select {
   146→		case <-ctx.Done():
   147→			logging.Info(ctx, "trail runner stopping")
   148→			return fmt.Errorf("trail runner stopped: %w", ctx.Err())
   149→		case <-time.After(r.config.PollInterval):
   150→			// Continue to next iteration
   151→		}
   152→	}
   153→}
   154→
   155→// RunOnce performs a single iteration: find open trails and run them.
   156→func (r *Runner) RunOnce(ctx context.Context) ([]RunResult, error) {
   157→	// Find open trails
   158→	openTrails, err := r.discovery.FindOpen(ctx)
   159→	if err != nil {
   160→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to find open trails: %w", err)
   161→	}
   162→
   163→	if len(openTrails) == 0 {
   164→		logging.Debug(ctx, "no open trails found")
   165→		return nil, nil
   166→	}
   167→
   168→	logging.Info(ctx, "found open trails", slog.Int("count", len(openTrails)))
   169→
   170→	var results []RunResult
   171→
   172→	// Run trails up to max concurrent (currently only 1)
   173→	maxToRun := min(len(openTrails), r.config.MaxConcurrent)
   174→	for i := range maxToRun {
   175→		trail := openTrails[i].Trail
   176→		result := r.RunTrail(ctx, &trail)
   177→		results = append(results, result)
   178→	}
   179→
   180→	return results, nil
   181→}
   182→
   183→// RunTrail executes a single trail.
   184→func (r *Runner) RunTrail(ctx context.Context, trail *Trail) RunResult {
   185→	start := time.Now()
   186→	result := RunResult{Trail: trail}
   187→
   188→	ctx = logging.WithComponent(ctx, "trail-runner")
   189→
   190→	logging.Info(ctx, "starting trail execution",
   191→		slog.String("trail_id", trail.ID.String()),
   192→		slog.String("title", trail.Title),
   193→	)
   194→
   195→	if r.config.DryRun {
   196→		logging.Info(ctx, "dry run - skipping execution")
   197→		result.Success = true
   198→		result.Output = "dry run"
   199→		result.Duration = time.Since(start)
   200→		return result
   201→	}
   202→
   203→	// Create worktree and get initial branch head
   204→	worktreePath, branchHead, err := r.setupWorktree(ctx, trail)
   205→	if err != nil {
   206→		result.Error = fmt.Errorf("failed to setup worktree: %w", err)
   207→		result.Duration = time.Since(start)
   208→		return result
   209→	}
   210→
   211→	// Claim the trail
   212→	if err := r.state.Claim(ctx, trail.ID, branchHead); err != nil {
   213→		if errors.Is(err, ErrAlreadyClaimed) {
   214→			logging.Info(ctx, "trail already claimed, skipping",
   215→				slog.String("trail_id", trail.ID.String()),
   216→			)
   217→		}
   218→		result.Error = err
   219→		result.Duration = time.Since(start)
   220→		// Clean up worktree if we couldn't claim
   221→		if removeErr := r.worktree.Remove(ctx, trail.ID); removeErr != nil {
   222→			logging.Warn(ctx, "failed to remove worktree after claim failure",
   223→				slog.String("trail_id", trail.ID.String()),
   224→				slog.String("error", removeErr.Error()),
   225→			)
   226→		}
   227→		return result
   228→	}
   229→
   230→	// Run agent with validation loop
   231→	maxAttempts := r.config.MaxAttempts
   232→	if maxAttempts < 1 {
   233→		maxAttempts = 1
   234→	}
   235→
   236→	var allOutput strings.Builder
   237→	var lastValidationOutput string
   238→
   239→	for attempt := 1; attempt <= maxAttempts; attempt++ {
   240→		result.Attempts = attempt
   241→
   242→		logging.Info(ctx, "running agent",
   243→			slog.String("trail_id", trail.ID.String()),
   244→			slog.Int("attempt", attempt),
   245→			slog.Int("max_attempts", maxAttempts),
   246→		)
   247→
   248→		// Build prompt - include validation feedback if this is a retry
   249→		prompt := trail.Description
   250→		if attempt > 1 && lastValidationOutput != "" {
   251→			prompt = fmt.Sprintf("%s\n\n---\n\nPrevious attempt failed validation. Please fix the issues and try again.\n\nValidation output:\n%s",
   252→				trail.Description, lastValidationOutput)
   253→		}
   254→
   255→		// Run the agent
   256→		output, agentErr := r.runAgentWithPrompt(ctx, prompt, worktreePath)
   257→		allOutput.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("\n=== Attempt %d ===\n", attempt))
   258→		allOutput.WriteString(output)
   259→
   260→		if agentErr != nil {
   261→			result.Error = agentErr
   262→			result.Success = false
   263→			result.Output = allOutput.String()
   264→			logging.Error(ctx, "agent failed",
   265→				slog.String("trail_id", trail.ID.String()),
   266→				slog.Int("attempt", attempt),
   267→				slog.String("error", agentErr.Error()),
   268→			)
   269→			break
   270→		}
   271→
   272→		// Run validation
   273→		logging.Info(ctx, "running validation",
   274→			slog.String("trail_id", trail.ID.String()),
   275→			slog.Int("attempt", attempt),
   276→		)
   277→
   278→		validationPassed, validationOutput, validationErr := r.runValidation(ctx, worktreePath)
   279→		allOutput.WriteString("\n--- Validation ---\n")
   280→		allOutput.WriteString(validationOutput)
   281→
   282→		if validationErr != nil {
   283→			logging.Warn(ctx, "validation command failed",
   284→				slog.String("trail_id", trail.ID.String()),
   285→				slog.String("error", validationErr.Error()),
   286→			)
   287→			// Treat validation command failure as validation failure
   288→			validationPassed = false
   289→		}
   290→
   291→		result.ValidationPassed = validationPassed
   292→
   293→		if validationPassed {
   294→			logging.Info(ctx, "validation passed",
   295→				slog.String("trail_id", trail.ID.String()),
   296→				slog.Int("attempt", attempt),
   297→			)
   298→			result.Success = true
   299→			result.Output = allOutput.String()
   300→			break
   301→		}
   302→
   303→		logging.Info(ctx, "validation failed",
   304→			slog.String("trail_id", trail.ID.String()),
   305→			slog.Int("attempt", attempt),
   306→		)
   307→
   308→		lastValidationOutput = validationOutput
   309→
   310→		// If this was the last attempt, mark as failed
   311→		if attempt == maxAttempts {
   312→			result.Error = errors.New("validation failed after all attempts")
   313→			result.Success = false
   314→			result.Output = allOutput.String()
   315→		}
   316→	}
   317→
   318→	// Get final branch head
   319→	finalHead, headErr := r.worktree.GetHeadCommit(ctx, trail.ID)
   320→	finalHeadHash := plumbing.NewHash(finalHead)
   321→	if headErr != nil || finalHeadHash.IsZero() {
   322→		// Fall back to initial branch head if we can't get the current
   323→		finalHeadHash = branchHead
   324→	}
   325→
   326→	// Update state based on result
   327→	if result.Success {
   328→		if err := r.state.Complete(ctx, trail.ID, finalHeadHash); err != nil {
   329→			logging.Error(ctx, "failed to mark trail as completed",
   330→				slog.String("trail_id", trail.ID.String()),
   331→				slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   332→			)
   333→		}
   334→	} else {
   335→		if err := r.state.Fail(ctx, trail.ID, finalHeadHash); err != nil {
   336→			logging.Error(ctx, "failed to mark trail as failed",
   337→				slog.String("trail_id", trail.ID.String()),
   338→				slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   339→			)
   340→		}
   341→	}
   342→
   343→	// Clean up worktree
   344→	if err := r.worktree.Remove(ctx, trail.ID); err != nil {
   345→		logging.Warn(ctx, "failed to remove worktree",
   346→			slog.String("trail_id", trail.ID.String()),
   347→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   348→		)
   349→	}
   350→
   351→	result.Duration = time.Since(start)
   352→
   353→	logging.Info(ctx, "trail execution finished",
   354→		slog.String("trail_id", trail.ID.String()),
   355→		slog.Bool("success", result.Success),
   356→		slog.Int("attempts", result.Attempts),
   357→		slog.Duration("duration", result.Duration),
   358→	)
   359→
   360→	return result
   361→}
   362→
   363→// setupWorktree creates a worktree for the trail and returns the path and branch head.
   364→func (r *Runner) setupWorktree(ctx context.Context, trail *Trail) (string, plumbing.Hash, error) {
   365→	branch := trail.GetBranch()
   366→
   367→	// Ensure .worktrees is in .gitignore
   368→	if err := EnsureWorktreesIgnored(r.repoRoot); err != nil {
   369→		logging.Warn(ctx, "failed to ensure worktrees ignored",
   370→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   371→		)
   372→	}
   373→
   374→	// Create the worktree
   375→	worktreePath, err := r.worktree.Create(ctx, trail.ID, branch, trail.BaseBranch)
   376→	if err != nil {
   377→		return "", plumbing.ZeroHash, err
   378→	}
   379→
   380→	// Get the branch head
   381→	headCommit, err := r.worktree.GetHeadCommit(ctx, trail.ID)
   382→	if err != nil {
   383→		return "", plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to get branch head: %w", err)
   384→	}
   385→
   386→	return worktreePath, plumbing.NewHash(headCommit), nil
   387→}
   388→
   389→// runAgentWithPrompt executes the agent CLI with the given prompt.
   390→func (r *Runner) runAgentWithPrompt(ctx context.Context, prompt string, worktreePath string) (string, error) {
   391→	// Build command arguments
   392→	args := []string{}
   393→
   394→	// Add model if specified
   395→	if r.config.Model != "" {
   396→		args = append(args, "--model", r.config.Model)
   397→	}
   398→
   399→	// Add prompt
   400→	args = append(args, "-p", prompt)
   401→
   402→	// Create context with timeout
   403→	if r.config.Timeout > 0 {
   404→		var cancel context.CancelFunc
   405→		ctx, cancel = context.WithTimeout(ctx, r.config.Timeout)
   406→		defer cancel()
   407→	}
   408→
   409→	logging.Debug(ctx, "running agent",
   410→		slog.String("agent", r.config.Agent),
   411→		slog.String("worktree", worktreePath),
   412→	)
   413→
   414→	//nolint:gosec // args are constructed from trusted config
   415→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, r.config.Agent, args...)
   416→	cmd.Dir = worktreePath
   417→	cmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   418→		// Ensure entire hooks are enabled in the worktree
   419→		"ENTIRE_ENABLED=1",
   420→	)
   421→
   422→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   423→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   424→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   425→
   426→	err := cmd.Run()
   427→
   428→	output := stdout.String()
   429→	if stderr.Len() > 0 {
   430→		if output != "" {
   431→			output += "\n"
   432→		}
   433→		output += stderr.String()
   434→	}
   435→
   436→	if err != nil {
   437→		if ctx.Err() == context.DeadlineExceeded {
   438→			return output, fmt.Errorf("agent timed out after %s", r.config.Timeout)
   439→		}
   440→		return output, fmt.Errorf("agent failed: %s: %w", strings.TrimSpace(stderr.String()), err)
   441→	}
   442→
   443→	return output, nil
   444→}
   445→
   446→// runValidation runs "entire validate --json" and returns whether validation passed.
   447→func (r *Runner) runValidation(ctx context.Context, worktreePath string) (bool, string, error) {
   448→	// Create context with timeout for validation (10 minutes)
   449→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 10*time.Minute)
   450→	defer cancel()
   451→
   452→	entireCLI := r.config.EntireCLI
   453→	if entireCLI == "" {
   454→		entireCLI = "entire"
   455→	}
   456→
   457→	//nolint:gosec // entireCLI is from trusted config
   458→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, entireCLI, "validate", "--json")
   459→	cmd.Dir = worktreePath
   460→
   461→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   462→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   463→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   464→
   465→	err := cmd.Run()
   466→
   467→	// Combine output for logging
   468→	output := stdout.String()
   469→	if stderr.Len() > 0 {
   470→		if output != "" {
   471→			output += "\n"
   472→		}
   473→		output += stderr.String()
   474→	}
   475→
   476→	// Try to parse JSON result
   477→	var result validationResult
   478→	if jsonErr := json.Unmarshal(stdout.Bytes(), &result); jsonErr == nil {
   479→		return result.Passed, output, nil
   480→	}
   481→
   482→	// If we couldn't parse JSON but command succeeded, assume passed
   483→	if err == nil {
   484→		return true, output, nil
   485→	}
   486→
   487→	// Command failed
   488→	if ctx.Err() == context.DeadlineExceeded {
   489→		return false, output, errors.New("validation timed out")
   490→	}
   491→
   492→	return false, output, fmt.Errorf("validation failed: %w", err)
   493→}
   494→
   495→// RunTrailByID executes a trail by its ID.
   496→func (r *Runner) RunTrailByID(ctx context.Context, id TrailID) (RunResult, error) {
   497→	trail, err := r.store.Get(ctx, id)
   498→	if err != nil {
   499→		return RunResult{}, fmt.Errorf("failed to get trail: %w", err)
   500→	}
   501→	if trail == nil {
   502→		return RunResult{}, fmt.Errorf("trail not found: %s", id)
   503→	}
   504→
   505→	return r.RunTrail(ctx, trail), nil
   506→}

  "old_string": "// runAgentWithPrompt executes the agent CLI with the given prompt.\nfunc (r *Runner) runAgentWithPrompt(ctx context.Context, prompt string, worktreePath string) (string, error) {\n\t// Build command arguments\n\targs := []string{}\n\n\t// Add model if specified\n\tif r.config.Model != \"\" {\n\t\targs = append(args, \"--model\", r.config.Model)\n\t}\n\n\t// Add prompt\n\targs = append(args, \"-p\", prompt)\n\n\t// Create context with timeout\n\tif r.config.Timeout > 0 {\n\t\tvar cancel context.CancelFunc\n\t\tctx, cancel = context.WithTimeout(ctx, r.config.Timeout)\n\t\tdefer cancel()\n\t}\n\n\tlogging.Debug(ctx, \"running agent\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"agent\", r.config.Agent),\n\t\tslog.String(\"worktree\", worktreePath),\n\t)\n\n\t//nolint:gosec // args are constructed from trusted config\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, r.config.Agent, args...)\n\tcmd.Dir = worktreePath\n\tcmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),\n\t\t// Ensure entire hooks are enabled in the worktree\n\t\t\"ENTIRE_ENABLED=1\",\n\t)\n\n\tvar stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer\n\tcmd.Stdout = &stdout\n\tcmd.Stderr = &stderr\n\n\terr := cmd.Run()\n\n\toutput := stdout.String()\n\tif stderr.Len() > 0 {\n\t\tif output != \"\" {\n\t\t\toutput += \"\\n\"\n\t\t}\n\t\toutput += stderr.String()\n\t}\n\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif ctx.Err() == context.DeadlineExceeded {\n\t\t\treturn output, fmt.Errorf(\"agent timed out after %s\", r.config.Timeout)\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn output, fmt.Errorf(\"agent failed: %s: %w\", strings.TrimSpace(stderr.String()), err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn output, nil\n}",
  "new_string": "// runAgentWithPrompt executes the agent with the given prompt using the Prompter interface.\nfunc (r *Runner) runAgentWithPrompt(ctx context.Context, prompt string, worktreePath string) (string, error) {\n\t// Get the agent from the registry\n\tag, err := agent.Get(r.config.AgentName)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get agent %q: %w\", r.config.AgentName, err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Check if the agent implements Prompter\n\tprompter, ok := ag.(agent.Prompter)\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"agent %q does not support prompting (does not implement agent.Prompter)\", r.config.AgentName)\n\t}\n\n\t// Create context with timeout\n\tif r.config.Timeout > 0 {\n\t\tvar cancel context.CancelFunc\n\t\tctx, cancel = context.WithTimeout(ctx, r.config.Timeout)\n\t\tdefer cancel()\n\t}\n\n\tlogging.Debug(ctx, \"running agent\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"agent\", string(r.config.AgentName)),\n\t\tslog.String(\"worktree\", worktreePath),\n\t)\n\n\t// Build prompt options\n\topts := agent.PromptOptions{\n\t\tModel:   r.config.Model,\n\t\tWorkDir: worktreePath,\n\t\tExtraEnv: []string{\n\t\t\t// Ensure entire hooks are enabled in the worktree\n\t\t\t\"ENTIRE_ENABLED=1\",\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\t// Run the prompt\n\tresult, err := prompter.Prompt(ctx, prompt, opts)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif ctx.Err() == context.DeadlineExceeded {\n\t\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"agent timed out after %s\", r.config.Timeout)\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"agent failed: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn result.Text, nil\n}"
}

    14→func newTrailRunnerCmd() *cobra.Command {

    21→The runner:
    22→1. Finds trails in 'open' state
    23→2. Claims them atomically (prevents concurrent execution)
    24→3. Creates a git worktree for isolated execution
    25→4. Runs the configured agent with the trail description
    26→5. Marks the trail as completed or failed
    27→6. Cleans up the worktree
    28→
    29→Examples:
    30→  # Run once (process one open trail)
    31→  entire trail-runner run-once
    32→
    33→  # Run in daemon mode (continuously poll for trails)
    34→  entire trail-runner start --daemon --poll-interval 30s
    35→
    36→  # Run a specific trail

   154→func runTrailRunnerStart(cmd *cobra.Command, daemon bool, pollInterval time.Duration, agent, model string, timeout time.Duration, dryRun bool, maxAttempts int) error {
   155→	repo, err := openRepository()
   156→	if err != nil {
   157→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   158→	}
   159→
   160→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   161→	if err != nil {
   162→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repository root: %w", err)
   163→	}
   164→
   165→	config := trail.RunnerConfig{
   166→		PollInterval:  pollInterval,
   167→		MaxConcurrent: 1,
   168→		Daemon:        daemon,
   169→		Agent:         agent,
   170→		Model:         model,
   171→		Timeout:       timeout,
   172→		DryRun:        dryRun,
   173→		MaxAttempts:   maxAttempts,
   174→	}
   175→
   176→	runner := trail.NewRunner(repo, repoRoot, config)
   177→	ctx := context.Background()
   178→
   179→	if daemon {
   180→		fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Starting trail runner in daemon mode (poll interval: %s)\n", pollInterval)
   181→	} else {
   182→		fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Starting trail runner...")
   183→	}
   184→
   185→	if err := runner.Start(ctx); err != nil {
   186→		return fmt.Errorf("runner failed: %w", err)
   187→	}
   188→
   189→	return nil
   190→}
   191→
   192→func runTrailRunnerRunOnce(cmd *cobra.Command, agent, model string, timeout time.Duration, dryRun bool, maxAttempts int) error {
   193→	repo, err := openRepository()
   194→	if err != nil {
   195→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   196→	}
   197→
   198→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   199→	if err != nil {
   200→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repository root: %w", err)
   201→	}
   202→
   203→	config := trail.RunnerConfig{
   204→		MaxConcurrent: 1,
   205→		Daemon:        false,
   206→		Agent:         agent,
   207→		Model:         model,
   208→		Timeout:       timeout,
   209→		DryRun:        dryRun,
   210→		MaxAttempts:   maxAttempts,
   211→	}
   212→
   213→	runner := trail.NewRunner(repo, repoRoot, config)
   214→	ctx := context.Background()
   215→
   216→	results, err := runner.RunOnce(ctx)
   217→	if err != nil {
   218→		return fmt.Errorf("runner failed: %w", err)
   219→	}
   220→
   221→	if len(results) == 0 {
   222→		fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "No open trails to run.")
   223→		return nil
   224→	}
   225→
   226→	for _, result := range results {
   227→		if result.Success {
   228→			fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Trail %s completed successfully (%s, %d attempts)\n",
   229→				result.Trail.ID.Short(), result.Duration.Round(time.Second), result.Attempts)
   230→		} else {
   231→			fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Trail %s failed after %d attempts: %v\n",
   232→				result.Trail.ID.Short(), result.Attempts, result.Error)
   233→		}
   234→	}
   235→
   236→	return nil
   237→}
   238→
   239→func runTrailRun(cmd *cobra.Command, idStr, agent, model string, timeout time.Duration, dryRun bool, maxAttempts int) error {
   240→	id, err := trail.NewTrailID(idStr)
   241→	if err != nil {
   242→		return err //nolint:wrapcheck // validation error
   243→	}
   244→
   245→	repo, err := openRepository()
   246→	if err != nil {
   247→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   248→	}
   249→
   250→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   251→	if err != nil {
   252→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repository root: %w", err)
   253→	}
   254→
   255→	config := trail.RunnerConfig{
   256→		MaxConcurrent: 1,
   257→		Daemon:        false,
   258→		Agent:         agent,
   259→		Model:         model,
   260→		Timeout:       timeout,
   261→		DryRun:        dryRun,
   262→		MaxAttempts:   maxAttempts,
   263→	}
   264→
   265→	runner := trail.NewRunner(repo, repoRoot, config)
   266→	ctx := context.Background()
   267→
   268→	fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Running trail: %s (max %d attempts)\n", id, maxAttempts)
   269→
   270→	result, err := runner.RunTrailByID(ctx, id)
   271→	if err != nil {
   272→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to run trail: %w", err)
   273→	}
   274→
   275→	if result.Success {
   276→		fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "\nTrail completed successfully (%s, %d attempts)\n",
   277→			result.Duration.Round(time.Second), result.Attempts)
   278→	} else {
   279→		fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "\nTrail failed after %d attempts: %v\n", result.Attempts, result.Error)
   280→		return NewSilentError(result.Error)
   281→	}
   282→
   283→	return nil
   284→}

> AGENT

All tests pass. The trail runner implementation is complete:

**Summary of changes:**

1. **`cmd/entire/cli/trail/runner.go`** - Updated to:
   - Use the `Prompter` interface from PR 302's pattern via `agent.Get()` and type assertion
   - Run `entire validate --json` after each agent attempt
   - Retry with validation feedback up to `MaxAttempts` times (default: 3)
   - Use `max()` builtin for cleaner code

2. **`cmd/entire/cli/trail_runner_cmd.go`** - Updated to use `agent.AgentName` type instead of raw strings

3. **`cmd/entire/cli/agent/prompter.go`** - New file defining the `Prompter` interface

4. **`cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/prompter.go`** - New file implementing `Prompter` for Claude Code

The trail runner validation loop now works as follows:
1. Run agent with trail description
2. Run `entire validate --json` to check if work is correct
3. If validation passes → mark trail as completed
4. If validation fails → retry with feedback ("Previous attempt failed validation. Please fix...") up to MaxAttempts times
5. If all attempts fail → mark trail as failed

> DEVELOPER

So I tried it and it failed with 3 attempts, the issue is that it's hard to understand what happened. A solution could be to make a commit after each attempt and then we get a checkpoint which has the session logs attached and we could inspect that?

  "new_string": "// commitAttempt commits all changes in the worktree to create a checkpoint.\n// This triggers Entire hooks which attach session logs to the checkpoint.\nfunc (r *Runner) commitAttempt(ctx context.Context, worktreePath, message string) error {\n\t// Stage all changes\n\taddCmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, \"git\", \"add\", \"-A\")\n\taddCmd.Dir = worktreePath\n\tif err := addCmd.Run(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"git add failed: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Check if there are changes to commit\n\tdiffCmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, \"git\", \"diff\", \"--cached\", \"--quiet\")\n\tdiffCmd.Dir = worktreePath\n\tif err := diffCmd.Run(); err == nil {\n\t\t// No changes to commit\n\t\tlogging.Debug(ctx, \"no changes to commit after attempt\")\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Commit the changes\n\tcommitCmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, \"git\", \"commit\", \"-m\", message)\n\tcommitCmd.Dir = worktreePath\n\tif err := commitCmd.Run(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"git commit failed: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tlogging.Info(ctx, \"committed attempt checkpoint\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"message\", message),\n\t)\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// RunTrailByID executes a trail by its ID.\nfunc (r *Runner) RunTrailByID(ctx context.Context, id TrailID) (RunResult, error) {"

> AGENT

All tests pass. Now after each agent attempt, the trail runner will:

1. **Run the agent** with the trail description
2. **Commit changes** with message like `"Trail a1b2c3d4 attempt 1: Fix the bug"`
3. **Entire hooks trigger** → creates a checkpoint with session logs attached
4. **Run validation** with `entire validate --json`
5. If validation fails → retry with feedback (up to MaxAttempts)

After a failed trail run, you can inspect what happened by:
- Running `entire log` in the worktree to see session logs
- Running `entire show <checkpoint-id>` to see checkpoint details
- The commit messages clearly indicate which attempt each checkpoint corresponds to

> DEVELOPER

hmm would be nice if the validation result would be part of the checkpoint, any idea how to achieve that?

> AGENT

Good point. We could reorder the flow: run validation *before* committing, then include the validation result in the commit. That way the checkpoint naturally includes the validation outcome.

The new flow would be:
1. Agent runs
2. Validation runs
3. Commit with validation result in the message/trailer
4. If failed, retry with feedback

Let me implement this:

   500→// commitAttempt commits all changes in the worktree to create a checkpoint.
   501→// This triggers Entire hooks which attach session logs to the checkpoint.
   502→func (r *Runner) commitAttempt(ctx context.Context, worktreePath, message string) error {
   503→	// Stage all changes
   504→	addCmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "add", "-A")
   505→	addCmd.Dir = worktreePath
   506→	if err := addCmd.Run(); err != nil {
   507→		return fmt.Errorf("git add failed: %w", err)
   508→	}
   509→
   510→	// Check if there are changes to commit
   511→	diffCmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "diff", "--cached", "--quiet")
   512→	diffCmd.Dir = worktreePath
   513→	if err := diffCmd.Run(); err == nil {
   514→		// No changes to commit
   515→		logging.Debug(ctx, "no changes to commit after attempt")
   516→		return nil
   517→	}
   518→
   519→	// Commit the changes
   520→	commitCmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "commit", "-m", message)
   521→	commitCmd.Dir = worktreePath
   522→	if err := commitCmd.Run(); err != nil {
   523→		return fmt.Errorf("git commit failed: %w", err)
   524→	}
   525→
   526→	logging.Info(ctx, "committed attempt checkpoint",
   527→		slog.String("message", message),
   528→	)
   529→
   530→	return nil
   531→}

  "old_string": "// commitAttempt commits all changes in the worktree to create a checkpoint.\n// This triggers Entire hooks which attach session logs to the checkpoint.\nfunc (r *Runner) commitAttempt(ctx context.Context, worktreePath, message string) error {\n\t// Stage all changes\n\taddCmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, \"git\", \"add\", \"-A\")\n\taddCmd.Dir = worktreePath\n\tif err := addCmd.Run(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"git add failed: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Check if there are changes to commit\n\tdiffCmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, \"git\", \"diff\", \"--cached\", \"--quiet\")\n\tdiffCmd.Dir = worktreePath\n\tif err := diffCmd.Run(); err == nil {\n\t\t// No changes to commit\n\t\tlogging.Debug(ctx, \"no changes to commit after attempt\")\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Commit the changes\n\tcommitCmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, \"git\", \"commit\", \"-m\", message)\n\tcommitCmd.Dir = worktreePath\n\tif err := commitCmd.Run(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"git commit failed: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tlogging.Info(ctx, \"committed attempt checkpoint\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"message\", message),\n\t)\n\n\treturn nil\n}",
  "new_string": "// commitAttempt commits all changes in the worktree to create a checkpoint.\n// This triggers Entire hooks which attach session logs to the checkpoint.\n// The validation output is written to .entire/validation.txt so it's part of the checkpoint.\nfunc (r *Runner) commitAttempt(ctx context.Context, worktreePath, message, validationOutput string) error {\n\t// Write validation output to .entire/validation.txt\n\tentireDir := filepath.Join(worktreePath, \".entire\")\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(entireDir, 0o755); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create .entire directory: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tvalidationFile := filepath.Join(entireDir, \"validation.txt\")\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(validationFile, []byte(validationOutput), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to write validation output: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Stage all changes including the validation file\n\taddCmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, \"git\", \"add\", \"-A\")\n\taddCmd.Dir = worktreePath\n\tif err := addCmd.Run(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"git add failed: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Check if there are changes to commit\n\tdiffCmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, \"git\", \"diff\", \"--cached\", \"--quiet\")\n\tdiffCmd.Dir = worktreePath\n\tif err := diffCmd.Run(); err == nil {\n\t\t// No changes to commit\n\t\tlogging.Debug(ctx, \"no changes to commit after attempt\")\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Commit the changes\n\tcommitCmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, \"git\", \"commit\", \"-m\", message)\n\tcommitCmd.Dir = worktreePath\n\tif err := commitCmd.Run(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"git commit failed: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tlogging.Info(ctx, \"committed attempt checkpoint\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"message\", message),\n\t)\n\n\treturn nil\n}"
}
```

> AGENT

All tests pass. Now the flow is:

1. **Agent runs** with trail description
2. **Validation runs** with `entire validate --json`
3. **Validation output written** to `.entire/validation.txt` in the worktree
4. **Commit created** with message including validation status (PASSED/FAILED)
5. **Entire hooks trigger** → checkpoint includes session logs AND validation result

Each checkpoint now contains:
- Session transcript (from Entire hooks)
- Validation output in `.entire/validation.txt` (committed file)
- Commit message with `Validation: PASSED` or `Validation: FAILED`

You can inspect a failed trail by:
- `entire show <checkpoint-id>` - see session logs
- `git show <commit>:.entire/validation.txt` - see validation output
- `git log --oneline` - see which attempts passed/failed

   116→func (r *Runner) Start(ctx context.Context) error {

> AGENT

The runner already handles context cancellation properly:

1. **Daemon mode** (line 148-154): Uses `select` to check for `ctx.Done()` during poll interval wait
2. **Exec commands**: All use `exec.CommandContext(ctx, ...)` which kills the command when context is cancelled:
   - Agent commands (via Prompter)
   - Validation commands
   - Git commands (add, diff, commit)

The fix I just made ensures `cmd.Context()` is used instead of `context.Background()`, so the context from `main.go` (which has the signal handler) is properly passed through.

When Ctrl+C is pressed:
- If waiting for next poll interval → immediately exits with "trail runner stopping"
- If running a trail → exec commands are killed, error propagates up, runner exits

One small improvement: we could check for cancellation before starting each trail attempt. Let me add that.

> AGENT

The trail runner now properly responds to Ctrl+C:

1. **Commands use `cmd.Context()`** - inherits the cancellable context from `main.go` which has the signal handler
2. **Daemon mode**: Uses `select` to exit immediately when cancelled during poll wait
3. **Attempt loop**: Now checks `ctx.Err()` before each attempt, so it won't start a new agent run if cancelled
4. **Exec commands**: All use `exec.CommandContext(ctx, ...)` so they get killed when context is cancelled

When you press Ctrl+C:
- If waiting for poll interval → exits immediately with "trail runner stopping"
- If between attempts → exits before starting next attempt with "cancelled: context canceled"
- If mid-execution → exec commands are killed, error propagates, then exits

   152→// Reset removes all state refs for a trail, returning it to open state.
   153→// This is useful for retrying a failed trail.
   154→func (m *StateManager) Reset(ctx context.Context, id TrailID) error {
   155→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   156→
   157→	// Remove all state refs
   158→	for _, suffix := range []string{RefSuffixClaimed, RefSuffixCompleted, RefSuffixFailed} {
   159→		refName := m.refName(id, suffix)
   160→		if _, err := m.repo.Reference(refName, true); err == nil {
   161→			if removeErr := m.repo.Storer.RemoveReference(refName); removeErr != nil {
   162→				return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove ref %s: %w", refName, removeErr)
   163→			}
   164→		}
   165→	}
   166→
   167→	return nil
   168→}
   169→
   170→// GetClaimedRef returns the commit hash that the claimed ref points to.
   171→// Returns plumbing.ZeroHash if not claimed.

    99→  entire trail create "Fix bug #123" --branch fix/bug-123
   100→  entire trail create "Add tests" --base main --labels urgent,tests`,
   101→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
   102→			var title string
   103→			if len(args) > 0 {
   104→				title = args[0]
   105→			}
   106→			return runTrailCreate(cmd, title, branch, baseBranch, labels)
   107→		},
   108→	}
   109→
   110→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&branch, "branch", "", "Branch name for this trail (default: trail/<id>)")
   111→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&baseBranch, "base", "", "Base branch to create from (default: main)")
   112→	cmd.Flags().StringSliceVar(&labels, "labels", nil, "Labels for categorization")
   113→
   114→	return cmd
   115→}

   151→func runTrailList(cmd *cobra.Command, statusFilter string, jsonOutput bool) error {
   152→	repo, err := openRepository()
   153→	if err != nil {
   154→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   155→	}
   156→
   157→	discovery := trail.NewDiscovery(repo)
   158→	ctx := context.Background()
   159→
   160→	// Build filter
   161→	var filter *trail.ListFilter
   162→	if statusFilter != "" {
   163→		state, err := parseTrailState(statusFilter)
   164→		if err != nil {
   165→			return err
   166→		}
   167→		filter = &trail.ListFilter{States: []trail.TrailState{state}}
   168→	}
   169→
   170→	trails, err := discovery.ListWithState(ctx, filter)
   171→	if err != nil {
   172→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to list trails: %w", err)
   173→	}
   174→
   175→	if jsonOutput {
   176→		data, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(trails, "", "  ")
   177→		if err != nil {
   178→			return err //nolint:wrapcheck // simple pass-through
   179→		}
   180→		fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), string(data))
   181→		return nil
   182→	}
   183→
   184→	if len(trails) == 0 {
   185→		fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "No trails found.")
   186→		fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "\nCreate a trail with: entire trail create \"Title\"")
   187→		return nil
   188→	}
   189→
   190→	// Print table
   191→	fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "ID          STATUS        BRANCH                    TITLE")
   192→	fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), strings.Repeat("-", 80))
   193→
   194→	for _, t := range trails {
   195→		status := formatTrailState(t.State)
   196→		branch := t.GetBranch()
   197→		if len(branch) > 24 {
   198→			branch = branch[:21] + "..."
   199→		}
   200→		title := t.Title
   201→		if len(title) > 30 {
   202→			title = title[:27] + "..."
   203→		}
   204→		fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "%-11s %-13s %-25s %s\n",
   205→			t.ID.Short(), status, branch, title)
   206→	}
   207→
   208→	return nil
   209→}
   210→
   211→func runTrailShow(cmd *cobra.Command, idStr string, jsonOutput bool) error {
   212→	id, err := trail.NewTrailID(idStr)
   213→	if err != nil {
   214→		return err //nolint:wrapcheck // validation error
   215→	}
   216→
   217→	repo, err := openRepository()
   218→	if err != nil {
   219→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   220→	}
   221→
   222→	discovery := trail.NewDiscovery(repo)
   223→	ctx := context.Background()
   224→
   225→	t, err := discovery.GetWithState(ctx, id)
   226→	if err != nil {
   227→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get trail: %w", err)
   228→	}
   229→	if t == nil {
   230→		return fmt.Errorf("trail not found: %s", id)
   231→	}
   232→
   233→	if jsonOutput {
   234→		data, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(t, "", "  ")
   235→		if err != nil {
   236→			return err //nolint:wrapcheck // simple pass-through
   237→		}
   238→		fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), string(data))
   239→		return nil
   240→	}
   241→
   242→	// Print details
   243→	fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Trail: %s\n", t.ID)
   244→	fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Title: %s\n", t.Title)
   245→	fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "State: %s\n", formatTrailState(t.State))
   246→	fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Branch: %s\n", t.GetBranch())
   247→	if t.BaseBranch != "" {
   248→		fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Base Branch: %s\n", t.BaseBranch)
   249→	}
   250→	fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Created: %s\n", t.CreatedAt.Format(time.RFC3339))
   251→	fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Updated: %s\n", t.UpdatedAt.Format(time.RFC3339))
   252→
   253→	if len(t.Labels) > 0 {
   254→		fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Labels: %s\n", strings.Join(t.Labels, ", "))
   255→	}
   256→	if len(t.Assignees) > 0 {
   257→		fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Assignees: %s\n", strings.Join(t.Assignees, ", "))
   258→	}
   259→
   260→	fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "\nDescription:\n%s\n", t.Description)
   261→
   262→	return nil
   263→}
   264→
   265→func runTrailCreate(cmd *cobra.Command, title, branch, baseBranch string, labels []string) error {
   266→	var description string
   267→
   268→	// Interactive mode if no title provided
   269→	if title == "" {
   270→		var err error
   271→		title, description, branch, baseBranch, err = runTrailCreateInteractive()
   272→		if err != nil {
   273→			return err
   274→		}
   275→	} else {
   276→		// Get description interactively
   277→		form := NewAccessibleForm(
   278→			huh.NewGroup(
   279→				huh.NewText().
   280→					Title("Description").
   281→					Description("Describe the task for the agent").
   282→					Value(&description).
   283→					CharLimit(4000),
   284→			),
   285→		)
   286→		if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   287→			return fmt.Errorf("cancelled: %w", err)
   288→		}
   289→	}
   290→
   291→	if description == "" {
   292→		return errors.New("description is required")
   293→	}
   294→
   295→	// Generate trail ID
   296→	id, err := trail.GenerateTrailID()
   297→	if err != nil {
   298→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate trail ID: %w", err)
   299→	}
   300→
   301→	// Create trail
   302→	t := &trail.Trail{
   303→		ID:          id,
   304→		Title:       title,
   305→		Description: description,
   306→		Branch:      branch,
   307→		BaseBranch:  baseBranch,
   308→		Labels:      labels,
   309→	}
   310→
   311→	repo, err := openRepository()
   312→	if err != nil {
   313→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   314→	}
   315→
   316→	store := trail.NewStore(repo)
   317→	ctx := context.Background()
   318→
   319→	if err := store.Create(ctx, t); err != nil {
   320→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create trail: %w", err)
   321→	}
   322→
   323→	fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Created trail: %s\n", id)
   324→	fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Branch: %s\n", t.GetBranch())
   325→	fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "\nRun the trail with: entire trail run %s\n", id)
   326→
   327→	return nil
   328→}
   329→
   330→func runTrailCreateInteractive() (title, description, branch, baseBranch string, err error) {
   331→	form := NewAccessibleForm(
   332→		huh.NewGroup(
   333→			huh.NewInput().
   334→				Title("Title").
   335→				Description("Brief title for the task").
   336→				Value(&title),
   337→			huh.NewText().
   338→				Title("Description").
   339→				Description("Describe the task for the agent").
   340→				Value(&description).
   341→				CharLimit(4000),
   342→			huh.NewInput().
   343→				Title("Branch").
   344→				Description("Branch name (leave empty for default)").
   345→				Value(&branch),
   346→			huh.NewInput().
   347→				Title("Base Branch").
   348→				Description("Base branch to create from (leave empty for main)").
   349→				Value(&baseBranch),
   350→		),
   351→	)
   352→
   353→	if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   354→		return "", "", "", "", fmt.Errorf("cancelled: %w", err)
   355→	}
   356→
   357→	if title == "" {
   358→		return "", "", "", "", errors.New("title is required")
   359→	}
   360→
   361→	return title, description, branch, baseBranch, nil
   362→}
   363→
   364→func runTrailDelete(cmd *cobra.Command, idStr string, force bool) error {
   365→	id, err := trail.NewTrailID(idStr)
   366→	if err != nil {
   367→		return err //nolint:wrapcheck // validation error
   368→	}
   369→
   370→	repo, err := openRepository()
   371→	if err != nil {
   372→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   373→	}
   374→
   375→	store := trail.NewStore(repo)
   376→	ctx := context.Background()
   377→
   378→	// Check if trail exists
   379→	t, err := store.Get(ctx, id)
   380→	if err != nil {
   381→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get trail: %w", err)
   382→	}
   383→	if t == nil {
   384→		return fmt.Errorf("trail not found: %s", id)
   385→	}
   386→
   387→	// Confirm deletion
   388→	if !force {
   389→		var confirm bool
   390→		form := NewAccessibleForm(
   391→			huh.NewGroup(
   392→				huh.NewConfirm().
   393→					Title(fmt.Sprintf("Delete trail %s?", id.Short())).
   394→					Description(t.Title).
   395→					Value(&confirm),
   396→			),
   397→		)
   398→		if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   399→			return fmt.Errorf("cancelled: %w", err)
   400→		}
   401→		if !confirm {
   402→			fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Cancelled.")
   403→			return nil
   404→		}
   405→	}
   406→
   407→	// Also reset state refs
   408→	state := trail.NewStateManager(repo)
   409→	if err := state.Reset(ctx, id); err != nil {
   410→		// Non-fatal, continue with deletion
   411→		fmt.Fprintf(cmd.ErrOrStderr(), "Warning: failed to reset state refs: %v\n", err)
   412→	}
   413→
   414→	if err := store.Delete(ctx, id); err != nil {
   415→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to delete trail: %w", err)
   416→	}
   417→
   418→	fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Deleted trail: %s\n", id)
   419→	return nil
   420→}
   421→
   422→func runTrailReset(cmd *cobra.Command, idStr string) error {
   423→	id, err := trail.NewTrailID(idStr)
   424→	if err != nil {
   425→		return err //nolint:wrapcheck // validation error
   426→	}
   427→
   428→	repo, err := openRepository()
   429→	if err != nil {
   430→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   431→	}
   432→
   433→	state := trail.NewStateManager(repo)
   434→	ctx := context.Background()
   435→
   436→	if err := state.Reset(ctx, id); err != nil {
   437→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to reset trail state: %w", err)
   438→	}
   439→
   440→	fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Reset trail: %s (now open)\n", id)
   441→	return nil
   442→}
   443→
   444→func parseTrailState(s string) (trail.TrailState, error) {
   445→	switch strings.ToLower(s) {
   446→	case "open":
   447→		return trail.TrailStateOpen, nil
   448→	case "in_progress", "inprogress", "claimed":
   449→		return trail.TrailStateInProgress, nil
   450→	case "completed", "done":
   451→		return trail.TrailStateCompleted, nil
   452→	case "failed", "error":
   453→		return trail.TrailStateFailed, nil
   454→	default:
   455→		return "", fmt.Errorf("invalid status: %s (valid: open, in_progress, completed, failed)", s)
   456→	}
   457→}
   458→
   459→func formatTrailState(state trail.TrailState) string {
   460→	switch state {
   461→	case trail.TrailStateOpen:
   462→		return "open"
   463→	case trail.TrailStateInProgress:
   464→		return "in_progress"
   465→	case trail.TrailStateCompleted:
   466→		return "completed"
   467→	case trail.TrailStateFailed:
   468→		return "failed"
   469→	default:
   470→		return string(state)
   471→	}
   472→}
   473→

This removes all state refs (claimed, completed, failed) for the trail,

Usage:
  entire trail reset <trail-id> [flags]

Flags:

> AGENT

The command already exists and works. Here's the workflow for resetting a failed trail:

```bash
# List trails to find failed ones
entire trail list --status failed

# Reset a specific trail to requeue it
entire trail reset <trail-id>

# Verify it's now open
entire trail show <trail-id>

# Run it again
entire trail run <trail-id>
# or let the runner pick it up
entire trail-runner run-once
```

Is there anything you'd like to add to this functionality, such as:
- A `--all` flag to reset all failed trails at once?
- Showing the previous state before resetting?
- A confirmation prompt?

```
     1→// Package trail provides a git-native task queue for autonomous agent execution.
     2→//
     3→// Trails are task definitions stored in the entire/trails orphan branch.
     4→// Execution state is tracked via lightweight git refs (no commits for state changes).
     5→// The Trail Runner discovers open trails, claims them atomically, creates worktrees,
     6→// and runs agents to complete the work.
     7→package trail
     8→
     9→import (
    10→	"crypto/rand"
    11→	"encoding/hex"
    12→	"encoding/json"
    13→	"errors"
    14→	"fmt"
    15→	"regexp"
    16→	"time"
    17→)
    18→
    19→// TrailID is a 12-character hex identifier for trails.
    20→// This follows the same pattern as CheckpointID for consistency.
    21→//
    22→//nolint:recvcheck,revive // UnmarshalJSON requires pointer receiver; name follows CheckpointID pattern
    23→type TrailID string
    24→
    25→// EmptyTrailID represents an unset or invalid trail ID.
    26→const EmptyTrailID TrailID = ""
    27→
    28→// trailIDPattern is the regex pattern for a valid trail ID: exactly 12 lowercase hex characters.
    29→const trailIDPattern = `[0-9a-f]{12}`
    30→
    31→// trailIDRegex validates the format: exactly 12 lowercase hex characters.
    32→var trailIDRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`^` + trailIDPattern + `$`)
    33→
    34→// NewTrailID creates a TrailID from a string, validating its format.
    35→// Returns an error if the string is not a valid 12-character hex ID.
    36→func NewTrailID(s string) (TrailID, error) {
    37→	if err := ValidateTrailID(s); err != nil {
    38→		return EmptyTrailID, err
    39→	}
    40→	return TrailID(s), nil
    41→}
    42→
    43→// MustTrailID creates a TrailID from a string, panicking if invalid.
    44→// Use only when the ID is known to be valid (e.g., from trusted sources).
    45→func MustTrailID(s string) TrailID {
    46→	id, err := NewTrailID(s)
    47→	if err != nil {
    48→		panic(err)
    49→	}
    50→	return id
    51→}
    52→
    53→// GenerateTrailID creates a new random 12-character hex trail ID.
    54→func GenerateTrailID() (TrailID, error) {
    55→	bytes := make([]byte, 6) // 6 bytes = 12 hex chars
    56→	if _, err := rand.Read(bytes); err != nil {
    57→		return EmptyTrailID, fmt.Errorf("failed to generate random trail ID: %w", err)
    58→	}
    59→	return TrailID(hex.EncodeToString(bytes)), nil
    60→}

   469→// runValidation runs "entire validate --json" and returns whether validation passed.
   470→func (r *Runner) runValidation(ctx context.Context, worktreePath string) (bool, string, error) {
   471→	// Create context with timeout for validation (10 minutes)
   472→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 10*time.Minute)
   473→	defer cancel()
   474→
   475→	entireCLI := r.config.EntireCLI
   476→	if entireCLI == "" {

   481→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, entireCLI, "validate", "--json")
   482→	cmd.Dir = worktreePath
   483→
   484→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   485→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   486→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   487→
   488→	err := cmd.Run()
   489→
   490→	// Combine output for logging
   491→	output := stdout.String()
   492→	if stderr.Len() > 0 {
   493→		if output != "" {
   494→			output += "\n"
   495→		}
   496→		output += stderr.String()
   497→	}
   498→
   499→	// Try to parse JSON result

   501→	if jsonErr := json.Unmarshal(stdout.Bytes(), &result); jsonErr == nil {

   503→	}

  "new_string": "// runValidation runs \"entire validate --json\" and returns whether validation passed.\nfunc (r *Runner) runValidation(ctx context.Context, worktreePath string) (bool, string, error) {\n\t// Create context with timeout for validation (10 minutes)\n\tctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 10*time.Minute)\n\tdefer cancel()\n\n\tvar cmd *exec.Cmd\n\tif r.config.LocalDev {\n\t\t// Use \"go run ./cmd/entire\" for local development\n\t\trepoRoot := r.config.RepoRoot\n\t\tif repoRoot == \"\" {\n\t\t\trepoRoot = \".\"\n\t\t}\n\t\t//nolint:gosec // repoRoot is from trusted config\n\t\tcmd = exec.CommandContext(ctx, \"go\", \"run\", filepath.Join(repoRoot, \"cmd/entire\"), \"validate\", \"--json\")\n\t} else {\n\t\tentireCLI := r.config.EntireCLI\n\t\tif entireCLI == \"\" {\n\t\t\tentireCLI = \"entire\"\n\t\t}\n\t\t//nolint:gosec // entireCLI is from trusted config\n\t\tcmd = exec.CommandContext(ctx, entireCLI, \"validate\", \"--json\")\n\t}\n\tcmd.Dir = worktreePath"

   160→func runTrailRunnerStart(cmd *cobra.Command, daemon bool, pollInterval time.Duration, agentName, model string, timeout time.Duration, dryRun bool, maxAttempts int) error {
   161→	repo, err := openRepository()
   162→	if err != nil {
   163→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   164→	}
   165→
   166→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   167→	if err != nil {
   168→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repository root: %w", err)
   169→	}
   170→
   171→	config := trail.RunnerConfig{
   172→		PollInterval:  pollInterval,
   173→		MaxConcurrent: 1,
   174→		Daemon:        daemon,
   175→		AgentName:     agent.AgentName(agentName),
   176→		Model:         model,
   177→		Timeout:       timeout,
   178→		DryRun:        dryRun,
   179→		MaxAttempts:   maxAttempts,
   180→	}
   181→
   182→	runner := trail.NewRunner(repo, repoRoot, config)
   183→	ctx := cmd.Context()
   184→
   185→	if daemon {
   186→		fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Starting trail runner in daemon mode (poll interval: %s)\n", pollInterval)
   187→	} else {
   188→		fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Starting trail runner...")
   189→	}
   190→
   191→	if err := runner.Start(ctx); err != nil {
   192→		return fmt.Errorf("runner failed: %w", err)
   193→	}
   194→
   195→	return nil
   196→}

```
Manage trails - a git-native task queue for autonomous agent execution.

Trails are task definitions stored in the entire/trails orphan branch.
The Trail Runner discovers open trails, claims them atomically, creates worktrees,
and runs agents to complete the work.

Example workflow:
  entire trail create "Implement user authentication"
  entire trail list
  entire trail-runner run-once

Usage:
  entire trail [flags]
  entire trail [command]

Available Commands:
  create      Create a new trail
  delete      Delete a trail
  list        List trails
  reset       Reset a trail's execution state (return to open)
  show        Show trail details

Flags:
  -h, --help   help for trail

Use "entire trail [command] --help" for more information about a command.
```

```
Run a specific trail by ID.

This command claims the trail, creates a worktree, runs the agent,
and updates the trail state based on the result.

After each agent run, validation is performed using 'entire validate'.
If validation fails, the agent is run again with feedback about the
failures, up to --max-attempts times.

Usage:
  entire trail-runner run <trail-id> [flags]

Flags:
      --agent string       Agent to use (e.g., claude-code) (default "claude-code")
      --dry-run            Print what would be done without executing
  -h, --help               help for run
      --local-dev          Use 'go run ./cmd/entire' instead of installed binary
      --max-attempts int   Max agent attempts before marking as failed (default 3)
      --model string       Model override for the agent
      --timeout duration   Timeout per trail execution (default 30m0s)
```

> AGENT

The `--local-dev` flag is now available on all trail runner commands:

```bash
# Run a specific trail with local development binary
entire trail-runner run <trail-id> --local-dev

# Run once with local dev
entire trail-runner run-once --local-dev

# Start daemon with local dev
entire trail-runner start --daemon --local-dev
```

When `--local-dev` is set, validation will run `go run ./cmd/entire validate --json` instead of the installed `entire` binary. This uses the `RepoRoot` (where the CLI repo is) to find the source code.

> DEVELOPER

     1→// Package settings provides configuration loading for Entire.
     2→// This package is separate from cli to allow strategy package to import it
     3→// without creating an import cycle (cli imports strategy).
     4→package settings
     5→
     6→import (
     7→	"bytes"
     8→	"encoding/json"
     9→	"fmt"
    10→	"os"
    11→	"path/filepath"
    12→
    13→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/jsonutil"
    14→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    15→)
    16→
    17→// DefaultStrategyName is the default strategy when none is configured.
    18→// This is duplicated here to avoid importing the strategy package (which would create a cycle).
    19→const DefaultStrategyName = "manual-commit"
    20→
    21→const (
    22→	// EntireSettingsFile is the path to the Entire settings file
    23→	EntireSettingsFile = ".entire/settings.json"
    24→	// EntireSettingsLocalFile is the path to the local settings override file (not committed)
    25→	EntireSettingsLocalFile = ".entire/settings.local.json"
    26→)
    27→
    28→// EntireSettings represents the .entire/settings.json configuration
    29→type EntireSettings struct {
    30→	// Strategy is the name of the git strategy to use
    31→	Strategy string `json:"strategy"`
    32→
    33→	// Enabled indicates whether Entire is active. When false, CLI commands
    34→	// show a disabled message and hooks exit silently. Defaults to true.
    35→	Enabled bool `json:"enabled"`
    36→
    37→	// LocalDev indicates whether to use "go run" instead of the "entire" binary
    38→	// This is used for development when the binary is not installed
    39→	LocalDev bool `json:"local_dev,omitempty"`
    40→
    41→	// LogLevel sets the logging verbosity (debug, info, warn, error).
    42→	// Can be overridden by ENTIRE_LOG_LEVEL environment variable.
    43→	// Defaults to "info".
    44→	LogLevel string `json:"log_level,omitempty"`
    45→
    46→	// StrategyOptions contains strategy-specific configuration
    47→	StrategyOptions map[string]any `json:"strategy_options,omitempty"`
    48→
    49→	// Telemetry controls anonymous usage analytics.
    50→	// nil = not asked yet (show prompt), true = opted in, false = opted out
    51→	Telemetry *bool `json:"telemetry,omitempty"`
    52→}
    53→
    54→// Load loads the Entire settings from .entire/settings.json,
    55→// then applies any overrides from .entire/settings.local.json if it exists.
    56→// Returns default settings if neither file exists.
    57→// Works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository.
    58→func Load() (*EntireSettings, error) {
    59→	// Get absolute paths for settings files
    60→	settingsFileAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(EntireSettingsFile)
    61→	if err != nil {
    62→		settingsFileAbs = EntireSettingsFile // Fallback to relative
    63→	}
    64→	localSettingsFileAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(EntireSettingsLocalFile)
    65→	if err != nil {
    66→		localSettingsFileAbs = EntireSettingsLocalFile // Fallback to relative
    67→	}
    68→
    69→	// Load base settings
    70→	settings, err := loadFromFile(settingsFileAbs)
    71→	if err != nil {
    72→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("reading settings file: %w", err)
    73→	}
    74→
    75→	// Apply local overrides if they exist
    76→	localData, err := os.ReadFile(localSettingsFileAbs) //nolint:gosec // path is from AbsPath or constant
    77→	if err != nil {
    78→		if !os.IsNotExist(err) {
    79→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("reading local settings file: %w", err)
    80→		}
    81→		// Local file doesn't exist, continue without overrides
    82→	} else {
    83→		if err := mergeJSON(settings, localData); err != nil {
    84→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("merging local settings: %w", err)
    85→		}
    86→	}
    87→
    88→	applyDefaults(settings)
    89→
    90→	return settings, nil
    91→}
    92→
    93→// LoadFromFile loads settings from a specific file path without merging local overrides.
    94→// Returns default settings if the file doesn't exist.
    95→// Use this when you need to display individual settings files separately.
    96→func LoadFromFile(filePath string) (*EntireSettings, error) {
    97→	return loadFromFile(filePath)
    98→}
    99→
   100→// loadFromFile loads settings from a specific file path.
   101→// Returns default settings if the file doesn't exist.
   102→func loadFromFile(filePath string) (*EntireSettings, error) {
   103→	settings := &EntireSettings{
   104→		Strategy: DefaultStrategyName,
   105→		Enabled:  true, // Default to enabled
   106→	}
   107→
   108→	data, err := os.ReadFile(filePath) //nolint:gosec // path is from caller
   109→	if err != nil {
   110→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   111→			return settings, nil
   112→		}
   113→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w", err)
   114→	}
   115→
   116→	dec := json.NewDecoder(bytes.NewReader(data))
   117→	dec.DisallowUnknownFields()
   118→	if err := dec.Decode(settings); err != nil {
   119→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("parsing settings file: %w", err)
   120→	}
   121→	applyDefaults(settings)
   122→
   123→	return settings, nil
   124→}
   125→
   126→// mergeJSON merges JSON data into existing settings.
   127→// Only non-zero values from the JSON override existing settings.
   128→func mergeJSON(settings *EntireSettings, data []byte) error {
   129→	// First, validate that there are no unknown keys using strict decoding
   130→	dec := json.NewDecoder(bytes.NewReader(data))
   131→	dec.DisallowUnknownFields()
   132→	var temp EntireSettings
   133→	if err := dec.Decode(&temp); err != nil {
   134→		return fmt.Errorf("parsing JSON: %w", err)
   135→	}
   136→
   137→	// Parse into a map to check which fields are present
   138→	var raw map[string]json.RawMessage
   139→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   140→		return fmt.Errorf("parsing JSON: %w", err)
   141→	}
   142→
   143→	// Override strategy if present and non-empty
   144→	if strategyRaw, ok := raw["strategy"]; ok {
   145→		var s string
   146→		if err := json.Unmarshal(strategyRaw, &s); err != nil {
   147→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing strategy field: %w", err)
   148→		}
   149→		if s != "" {
   150→			settings.Strategy = s
   151→		}
   152→	}
   153→
   154→	// Override enabled if present
   155→	if enabledRaw, ok := raw["enabled"]; ok {
   156→		var e bool
   157→		if err := json.Unmarshal(enabledRaw, &e); err != nil {
   158→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing enabled field: %w", err)
   159→		}
   160→		settings.Enabled = e
   161→	}
   162→
   163→	// Override local_dev if present
   164→	if localDevRaw, ok := raw["local_dev"]; ok {
   165→		var ld bool
   166→		if err := json.Unmarshal(localDevRaw, &ld); err != nil {
   167→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing local_dev field: %w", err)
   168→		}
   169→		settings.LocalDev = ld
   170→	}
   171→
   172→	// Override log_level if present and non-empty
   173→	if logLevelRaw, ok := raw["log_level"]; ok {
   174→		var ll string
   175→		if err := json.Unmarshal(logLevelRaw, &ll); err != nil {
   176→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing log_level field: %w", err)
   177→		}
   178→		if ll != "" {
   179→			settings.LogLevel = ll
   180→		}
   181→	}
   182→
   183→	// Merge strategy_options if present
   184→	if optionsRaw, ok := raw["strategy_options"]; ok {
   185→		var opts map[string]any
   186→		if err := json.Unmarshal(optionsRaw, &opts); err != nil {
   187→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing strategy_options field: %w", err)
   188→		}
   189→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   190→			settings.StrategyOptions = opts
   191→		} else {
   192→			for k, v := range opts {
   193→				settings.StrategyOptions[k] = v
   194→			}
   195→		}
   196→	}
   197→
   198→	// Override telemetry if present
   199→	if telemetryRaw, ok := raw["telemetry"]; ok {
   200→		var t bool
   201→		if err := json.Unmarshal(telemetryRaw, &t); err != nil {
   202→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing telemetry field: %w", err)
   203→		}
   204→		settings.Telemetry = &t
   205→	}
   206→
   207→	return nil
   208→}
   209→
   210→func applyDefaults(settings *EntireSettings) {
   211→	if settings.Strategy == "" {
   212→		settings.Strategy = DefaultStrategyName
   213→	}
   214→}
   215→
   216→// IsSummarizeEnabled checks if auto-summarize is enabled in settings.
   217→// Returns false by default if settings cannot be loaded or the key is missing.
   218→func IsSummarizeEnabled() bool {
   219→	settings, err := Load()
   220→	if err != nil {
   221→		return false
   222→	}
   223→	return settings.IsSummarizeEnabled()
   224→}
   225→
   226→// IsSummarizeEnabled checks if auto-summarize is enabled in this settings instance.
   227→func (s *EntireSettings) IsSummarizeEnabled() bool {
   228→	if s.StrategyOptions == nil {
   229→		return false
   230→	}
   231→	summarizeOpts, ok := s.StrategyOptions["summarize"].(map[string]any)
   232→	if !ok {
   233→		return false
   234→	}
   235→	enabled, ok := summarizeOpts["enabled"].(bool)
   236→	if !ok {
   237→		return false
   238→	}
   239→	return enabled
   240→}
   241→
   242→// IsPushSessionsDisabled checks if push_sessions is disabled in settings.
   243→// Returns true if push_sessions is explicitly set to false.
   244→func (s *EntireSettings) IsPushSessionsDisabled() bool {
   245→	if s.StrategyOptions == nil {
   246→		return false
   247→	}
   248→	val, exists := s.StrategyOptions["push_sessions"]
   249→	if !exists {
   250→		return false
   251→	}
   252→	if boolVal, ok := val.(bool); ok {
   253→		return !boolVal // disabled = !push_sessions
   254→	}
   255→	return false
   256→}
   257→
   258→// Save saves the settings to .entire/settings.json.
   259→func Save(settings *EntireSettings) error {
   260→	return saveToFile(settings, EntireSettingsFile)
   261→}
   262→
   263→// SaveLocal saves the settings to .entire/settings.local.json.
   264→func SaveLocal(settings *EntireSettings) error {
   265→	return saveToFile(settings, EntireSettingsLocalFile)
   266→}
   267→
   268→// saveToFile saves settings to the specified file path.
   269→func saveToFile(settings *EntireSettings, filePath string) error {
   270→	// Get absolute path for the file
   271→	filePathAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(filePath)
   272→	if err != nil {
   273→		filePathAbs = filePath // Fallback to relative
   274→	}
   275→
   276→	// Ensure directory exists
   277→	dir := filepath.Dir(filePathAbs)
   278→	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o750); err != nil {
   279→		return fmt.Errorf("creating settings directory: %w", err)
   280→	}
   281→
   282→	data, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(settings, "", "  ")
   283→	if err != nil {
   284→		return fmt.Errorf("marshaling settings: %w", err)
   285→	}
   286→
   287→	//nolint:gosec // G306: settings file is config, not secrets; 0o644 is appropriate
   288→	if err := os.WriteFile(filePathAbs, data, 0o644); err != nil {
   289→		return fmt.Errorf("writing settings file: %w", err)
   290→	}
   291→	return nil
   292→}

All tests pass. Now `local_dev` is read from settings instead of a flag. To use it:

```json
// .entire/settings.local.json (not committed)
{
  "local_dev": true
}
```

When `local_dev: true` is set in settings, the trail runner will use `go run ./cmd/entire validate --json` from the repo root instead of the installed `entire` binary.

> DEVELOPER

I'm still getting: directory ../../cmd/entire outside main module or its selected dependencies

> AGENT

The issue is that `go run` needs to be executed from the module root (where `go.mod` is), but we're running from the worktree directory. Let me fix this by building the binary first, then running it:

> TOOL
