err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\", \"\", \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"cancelled: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif title == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\", \"\", \"\", errors.New(\"title is required\")\n\t}\n\n\t// Resolve base branch choice\n\tswitch baseBranchChoice {\n\tcase \"current\":\n\t\tbaseBranch = currentBranch\n\tcase \"default\":\n\t\tbaseBranch = defaultBranch\n\tcase \"pick\":\n\t\t// Show branch picker with autocomplete\n\t\tbranchOptions := make([]huh.Option[string], len(allBranches))\n\t\tfor i, b := range allBranches {\n\t\t\tbranchOptions[i] = huh.NewOption(b, b)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tform2 := NewAccessibleForm(\n\t\t\thuh.NewGroup(\n\t\t\t\thuh.NewSelect[string]().\n\t\t\t\t\tTitle(\"Select Branch\").\n\t\t\t\t\tDescription(\"Choose the base branch\").\n\t\t\t\t\tOptions(branchOptions...).\n\t\t\t\t\tValue(&baseBranch).\n\t\t\t\t\tFiltering(true).\n\t\t\t\t\tHeight(10),\n\t\t\t),\n\t\t)\n\n\t\tif err := form2.Run(); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn \"\", \"\", \"\", \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"cancelled: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn title, description, branch, baseBranch, nil\n}\n\n// getBranchInfo returns current branch, default branch (main/master), and all branch names.\nfunc getBranchInfo(repo *git.Repository) (current, defaultBr string, all []string) {\n\t// Get current branch\n\thead, err := repo.Head()\n\tif err == nil && head.Name().IsBranch() {\n\t\tcurrent = head.Name().Short()\n\t} else {\n\t\tcurrent = \"HEAD\"\n\t}\n\n\t// Get all branches\n\tbranches, err := repo.Branches()\n\tif err == nil {\n\t\t_ = branches.ForEach(func(ref *plumbing.Reference) error {\n\t\t\tname := ref.Name().Short()\n\t\t\tall = append(all, name)\n\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\n\t// Determine default branch (prefer main, then master)\n\tdefaultBr = \"main\"\n\tfor _, b := range all {\n\t\tif b == \"main\" {\n\t\t\tdefaultBr = \"main\"\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t\tif b == \"master\" {\n\t\t\tdefaultBr = \"master\"\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn current, defaultBr, all\n}"

   156→func (m *WorktreeManager) getDefaultBranch(ctx context.Context) (string, error) {
   157→	// Try to get the default branch from origin
   158→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "symbolic-ref", "refs/remotes/origin/HEAD")
   159→	cmd.Dir = m.repoRoot
   160→	if output, err := cmd.Output(); err == nil {
   161→		ref := strings.TrimSpace(string(output))

   163→		parts := strings.Split(ref, "/")

   166→		}

   177→		}
   178→	}

  "new_string": "// getBranchInfo returns current branch, default branch, and all branch names.\nfunc getBranchInfo(repo *git.Repository) (current, defaultBr string, all []string) {\n\t// Get current branch\n\thead, err := repo.Head()\n\tif err == nil && head.Name().IsBranch() {\n\t\tcurrent = head.Name().Short()\n\t} else {\n\t\tcurrent = \"HEAD\"\n\t}\n\n\t// Get all branches\n\tbranches, err := repo.Branches()\n\tif err == nil {\n\t\t_ = branches.ForEach(func(ref *plumbing.Reference) error { //nolint:errcheck,gosec // Collecting branches best-effort; partial list is acceptable\n\t\t\tname := ref.Name().Short()\n\t\t\tall = append(all, name)\n\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\n\t// Determine default branch from origin/HEAD\n\tdefaultBr = getDefaultBranch(repo)\n\n\treturn current, defaultBr, all\n}\n\n// getDefaultBranch returns the repository's default branch name.\n// First tries to read origin/HEAD, then falls back to checking main/master.\nfunc getDefaultBranch(repo *git.Repository) string {\n\t// Try to get the symbolic reference for origin/HEAD\n\toriginHead, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.ReferenceName(\"refs/remotes/origin/HEAD\"), true)\n\tif err == nil {\n\t\t// Extract branch name from refs/remotes/origin/main\n\t\tname := originHead.Name().Short()\n\t\t// name will be \"origin/main\" or \"origin/master\", extract the last part\n\t\tparts := strings.Split(name, \"/\")\n\t\tif len(parts) > 0 {\n\t\t\treturn parts[len(parts)-1]\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Fall back to checking common default branch names\n\tfor _, branch := range []string{\"main\", \"master\"} {\n\t\t_, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branch), true)\n\t\tif err == nil {\n\t\t\treturn branch\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn \"main\" // Ultimate fallback\n}"
}

   117→// getDefaultBranchFromRemote tries to determine the default branch from the origin remote.
   118→// Returns empty string if unable to determine.
   119→func getDefaultBranchFromRemote(repo *git.Repository) string {
   120→	// Try to get the symbolic reference for origin/HEAD
   121→	ref, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName("origin", "HEAD"), true)
   122→	if err == nil && ref != nil {
   123→		// ref.Target() gives us something like "refs/remotes/origin/main"
   124→		target := ref.Target().String()
   125→		if strings.HasPrefix(target, "refs/remotes/origin/") {
   126→			return strings.TrimPrefix(target, "refs/remotes/origin/")
   127→		}
   128→	}
   129→
   130→	// Fallback: check if origin/main or origin/master exists
   131→	if _, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName("origin", "main"), true); err == nil {
   132→		return "main"
   133→	}
   134→	if _, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName("origin", "master"), true); err == nil {
   135→		return "master"
   136→	}
   137→
   138→	return ""
   139→}

   332→func runTrailCreateInteractive() (title, description, branch, baseBranch string, err error) {
   333→	// Get current branch and available branches for selection
   334→	repo, err := openRepository()
   335→	if err != nil {
   336→		return "", "", "", "", fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   337→	}
   338→
   339→	currentBranch, defaultBranch, allBranches := getBranchInfo(repo)
   340→

   344→		huh.NewOption("Current branch ("+currentBranch+")", "current"),
   345→		huh.NewOption("Default branch ("+defaultBranch+")", "default"),
   346→		huh.NewOption("Pick from branches...", "pick"),
   347→	}
   348→
   349→	// First form: title, description, base branch choice

   351→		huh.NewGroup(
   352→			huh.NewInput().
   353→				Title("Title").
   354→				Description("Brief title for the task").
   355→				Value(&title),
   356→			huh.NewText().
   357→				Title("Description").
   358→				Description("Describe the task for the agent").
   359→				Value(&description).
   360→				CharLimit(4000),
   361→			huh.NewInput().
   362→				Title("Branch").
   363→				Description("Branch name for this trail (leave empty for auto-generated)").
   364→				Value(&branch),
   365→			huh.NewSelect[string]().
   366→				Title("Base Branch").
   367→				Description("Which branch should the trail start from?").
   368→				Options(baseOptions...).
   369→				Value(&baseBranchChoice),
   370→		),
   371→	)

   374→		return "", "", "", "", fmt.Errorf("cancelled: %w", err)

   408→		}
   409→	}

   414→// getBranchInfo returns current branch, default branch, and all branch names.
   415→func getBranchInfo(repo *git.Repository) (current, defaultBr string, all []string) {
   416→	// Get current branch
   417→	head, err := repo.Head()
   418→	if err == nil && head.Name().IsBranch() {
   419→		current = head.Name().Short()
   420→	} else {
   421→		current = "HEAD"
   422→	}
   423→
   424→	// Get all branches
   425→	branches, err := repo.Branches()

> AGENT

The branch picker feature is complete. Here's what was implemented:

**Branch picker for `entire trail create`** (interactive mode):
- Option 1: "Current branch (feature/xyz)" - uses the current HEAD branch
- Option 2: "Default branch (main)" - uses origin/HEAD (detected via `git symbolic-ref refs/remotes/origin/HEAD`)
- Option 3: "Pick from branches..." - shows a searchable list with autocomplete (`Filtering(true)`)

**Default branch detection** uses your suggested approach:
- First tries `refs/remotes/origin/HEAD` symbolic ref
- Falls back to checking if `main` or `master` branch exists locally

**Also fixed**:
- Added `branchMain`/`branchMaster` constants in `git_operations.go` to resolve goconst lint error

All tests pass and lint is clean.

   123→// getDefaultBranchFromRemote tries to determine the default branch from the origin remote.
   124→// Returns empty string if unable to determine.
   125→func getDefaultBranchFromRemote(repo *git.Repository) string {
   126→	// Try to get the symbolic reference for origin/HEAD
   127→	ref, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName("origin", "HEAD"), true)
   128→	if err == nil && ref != nil {
   129→		// ref.Target() gives us something like "refs/remotes/origin/main"
   130→		target := ref.Target().String()
   131→		if strings.HasPrefix(target, "refs/remotes/origin/") {
   132→			return strings.TrimPrefix(target, "refs/remotes/origin/")
   133→		}
   134→	}
   135→
   136→	// Fallback: check if origin/main or origin/master exists
   137→	if _, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName("origin", branchMain), true); err == nil {
   138→		return branchMain
   139→	}
   140→	if _, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName("origin", branchMaster), true); err == nil {
   141→		return branchMaster
   142→	}
   143→
   144→	return ""
   145→}
   146→
   147→// ShouldSkipOnDefaultBranch checks if we're on the default branch.
   148→// Returns (shouldSkip, branchName). If shouldSkip is true, the caller should
   149→// skip the operation to avoid polluting main/master history.

    85→// It determines the default branch by:
    86→// 1. Checking the remote origin's HEAD reference
    87→// 2. Falling back to common names (main, master) if remote HEAD is unavailable
    88→// Returns (isDefault, branchName, error)
    89→func IsOnDefaultBranch() (bool, string, error) {
    90→	repo, err := openRepository()
    91→	if err != nil {
    92→		return false, "", fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
    93→	}
    94→
    95→	// Get current branch
    96→	head, err := repo.Head()
    97→	if err != nil {
    98→		return false, "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
    99→	}
   100→
   101→	if !head.Name().IsBranch() {
   102→		// Detached HEAD - not on any branch
   103→		return false, "", nil
   104→	}
   105→
   106→	currentBranch := head.Name().Short()

  "old_string": "// IsOnDefaultBranch checks if the repository is currently on the default branch.\n// It determines the default branch by:\n// 1. Checking the remote origin's HEAD reference\n// 2. Falling back to common names (main, master) if remote HEAD is unavailable\n// Returns (isDefault, branchName, error)\nfunc IsOnDefaultBranch() (bool, string, error) {\n\trepo, err := openRepository()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to open git repository: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Get current branch\n\thead, err := repo.Head()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get HEAD: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif !head.Name().IsBranch() {\n\t\t// Detached HEAD - not on any branch\n\t\treturn false, \"\", nil\n\t}\n\n\tcurrentBranch := head.Name().Short()\n\n\t// Try to get default branch from remote origin's HEAD\n\tdefaultBranch := getDefaultBranchFromRemote(repo)\n\n\t// If we couldn't determine from remote, use common defaults\n\tif defaultBranch == \"\" {\n\t\t// Check if current branch is a common default name\n\t\tif currentBranch == branchMain || currentBranch == branchMaster {\n\t\t\treturn true, currentBranch, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn false, currentBranch, nil\n\t}\n\n\treturn currentBranch == defaultBranch, currentBranch, nil\n}\n\n// getDefaultBranchFromRemote tries to determine the default branch from the origin remote.\n// Returns empty string if unable to determine.\nfunc getDefaultBranchFromRemote(repo *git.Repository) string {\n\t// Try to get the symbolic reference for origin/HEAD\n\tref, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName(\"origin\", \"HEAD\"), true)\n\tif err == nil && ref != nil {\n\t\t// ref.Target() gives us something like \"refs/remotes/origin/main\"\n\t\ttarget := ref.Target().String()\n\t\tif strings.HasPrefix(target, \"refs/remotes/origin/\") {\n\t\t\treturn strings.TrimPrefix(target, \"refs/remotes/origin/\")\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Fallback: check if origin/main or origin/master exists\n\tif _, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName(\"origin\", branchMain), true); err == nil {\n\t\treturn branchMain\n\t}\n\tif _, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName(\"origin\", branchMaster), true); err == nil {\n\t\treturn branchMaster\n\t}\n\n\treturn \"\"\n}",
  "new_string": "// IsOnDefaultBranch checks if the repository is currently on the default branch.\n// Returns (isDefault, branchName, error)\nfunc IsOnDefaultBranch() (bool, string, error) {\n\trepo, err := openRepository()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to open git repository: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tisDefault, currentBranch := strategy.IsOnDefaultBranch(repo)\n\treturn isDefault, currentBranch, nil\n}"

> AGENT

All tests pass. Here's what was cleaned up:

1. **Removed** `getDefaultBranchFromRemote` and `branchMain`/`branchMaster` constants from `git_operations.go`
2. **Simplified** `IsOnDefaultBranch()` to just call `strategy.IsOnDefaultBranch(repo)`
3. **Simplified** `resume.go` to use `strategy.GetDefaultBranchName(repo)` instead of duplicating fallback logic
4. **`trail_cmd.go`** uses `strategy.GetDefaultBranchName(repo)` with a simple `"main"` fallback only for repos with no remote

Now default branch detection lives in one place: `strategy.GetDefaultBranchName`.

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/git_operations.go",
  "old_string": "// IsOnDefaultBranch checks if the repository is currently on the default branch.\n// Returns (isDefault, branchName, error)\nfunc IsOnDefaultBranch() (bool, string, error) {\n\trepo, err := openRepository()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to open git repository: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tisDefault, currentBranch := strategy.IsOnDefaultBranch(repo)\n\treturn isDefault, currentBranch, nil\n}",
  "new_string": "// IsOnDefaultBranch checks if the repository is currently on the default branch.\n// Returns (isDefault, branchName, error)\nfunc IsOnDefaultBranch() (bool, string, error) {\n\trepo, err := openRepository()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to open git repository: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Get current branch\n\thead, err := repo.Head()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get HEAD: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif !head.Name().IsBranch() {\n\t\t// Detached HEAD - not on any branch\n\t\treturn false, \"\", nil\n\t}\n\n\tcurrentBranch := head.Name().Short()\n\tdefaultBranch := strategy.GetDefaultBranchName(repo)\n\n\t// If we couldn't determine default branch, check common names\n\tif defaultBranch == \"\" {\n\t\tif currentBranch == \"main\" || currentBranch == \"master\" {\n\t\t\treturn true, currentBranch, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn false, currentBranch, nil\n\t}\n\n\treturn currentBranch == defaultBranch, currentBranch, nil\n}"
}
